Conclusion: ZipDepth成功弥合了基础模型的高精度与轻量级模型的低效率部署之间的鸿沟，证明了通过多域知识蒸馏可将基础模型的零样本泛化能力有效压缩至实时可部署的紧凑网络中。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation has seen remarkable progress through foundation models achieving robust zero-shot generalization, yet their computational demands place them far beyond the reach of embedded and mobile platforms. Lightweight alternatives exist, but have been developed almost exclusively within single-domain, self-supervised paradigms, failing silently under domain shift. We present ZipDepth, a compact monocular depth network that bridges this gap by combining an efficient reparameterizable encoder-decoder with large-scale knowledge distillation from a foundation model over a large multi-domain training set. Comprising just 6.1M parameters, ZipDepth runs at real-time rates from server GPUs to power-constrained devices, achieving the best trade-off between zero-shot accuracy and deployment efficiency among lightweight models across five benchmarks, taking a significant step towards the accuracy of foundation models with 50x more parameters.

</details>


### [72] [Wat3R: Underwater 3D Geometry Learning without Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08772)
*Jiangwei Ren,Xingyu Jiang,Zijie Song,Wei Xu,Hongkai Lin,Dingkang Liang,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Wat3R提出了一种跨域半监督学习框架，通过教师-学生架构仅利用无标注水下视频即可将空气中的3D重建模型迁移至水下场景，无需任何水下标注数据，并构建了Water3D基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境因光衰减、散射等问题，且缺乏大规模高质量3D标注，导致现有依赖密集标注的3D重建方法难以直接应用。需要一种无需水下标注即可将空气域3D重建能力迁移至水下场景的方法。

Method: 提出Wat3R跨域半监督学习框架：(1) 采用教师-学生架构，仅利用大量无标注真实水下视频学习鲁棒几何表征；(2) 设计跨视图一致性损失，利用其他视图的几何线索补偿当前视图因水体衰减和散射造成的信息退化；(3) 构建Water3D数据集作为评估基准。

Result: Wat3R在水下多视图深度估计和点云重建任务上均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: Wat3R成功实现了从空气到水下的跨域3D重建迁移，无需水下标注数据，为水下几何估计提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Estimating 3D geometry in underwater environments presents unique challenges due to light attenuation, scattering, and the absence of large-scale, high-quality 3D annotations. Pioneering methods rely on massive dense annotations that are impractical in underwater settings. In this paper, we propose Wat3R, a cross-domain semi-supervised learning framework designed to adapt feed-forward 3D reconstruction models from air to underwater scenes. Uniquely, our method eliminates the need for any annotated underwater data following a teacher-student architecture, that learns robust geometry representations merely on abundant unlabeled real underwater video footage. We also design a cross-view consistency loss that leverages geometric cues from other views to compensate for the information degradation in the current view caused by water attenuation and scattering. Furthermore, considering the lack of comprehensive evaluation benchmarks, we construct Water3D, a diverse dataset covering various water bodies and underwater scenarios, designed for geometric task evaluation. Experimental results demonstrate that Wat3R outperforms current state-of-the-art methods in underwater multi-view depth estimation and point cloud reconstruction. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/LSXI7/Wat3R .

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [73] [Unveiling Public Opinion: A Study of Sentiment Analysis Using LSTM and Traditional Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07772)
*Atiq Ur Rehman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了多种机器学习和深度学习方法在Twitter情感分析上的表现，发现LSTM网络以训练准确率90.98%、测试准确率80.00%和ROC-AUC 0.92的微平均得分优于逻辑回归、随机森林、朴素贝叶斯、梯度提升等传统方法，证明其在捕捉文本上下文和序列特征方面具有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体（如Twitter）上用户生成的海量文本数据蕴含丰富的观点与情绪，亟需高效准确的情感分析方法来提取洞察、辅助舆情解读与趋势预测。现有多种算法各有优劣，缺乏在统一预处理流程下的系统性对比，因此需要找出最优模型。

Method: 采用Kaggle平台的Twitter数据集，经过分词（tokenization）、词形还原（lemmatization）和停用词消除（stopword elimination）等预处理步骤后，系统评估了逻辑回归、随机森林、朴素贝叶斯、梯度提升以及LSTM网络等多种算法在情感三分类（正面、负面、中性）任务上的表现，并以训练准确率、测试准确率和微平均ROC-AUC作为评估指标。

Result: LSTM模型取得最佳表现：训练准确率90.98%，测试准确率80.00%，微平均ROC-AUC得分0.92，显著优于传统机器学习方法，表明深度序列模型能够更好地捕捉推文中的上下文依赖与顺序特征。

Conclusion: 在Twitter情感分析任务中，LSTM网络凭借其对序列数据的建模能力，在各项指标上均超越传统机器学习基线模型，是更优的选择。该结论为社交媒体情感分析领域的模型选型提供了明确的实证依据。

Abstract: In this age of social media, sites like Twitter have become meeting places for people to share their views and feelings on a wide range of issues and current events as they unfold in real time. Sentiment analysis, a critical application of NLP, has become indispensable due to the massive influx of user-generated content, enabling the extraction of meaningful insights from the opinions and emotions expressed in textual data. Sentiment analysis on Twitter employs sophisticated computational techniques to categorize tweets into positive, negative, or neutral sentiments. This method not only examines individual expressions but also analyzes vast databases related to specific subjects or events. By spotting these emotions, machine learning models help improve public opinion interpretation and trend forecasting. This paper examines the effectiveness of various machine learning and deep learning approaches. Designed for this use, the system evaluates logistic regression, random forest, naïve bayes, gradient boosting, and LSTM networks, among other algorithms applied in sentiment classification. This work identifies the optimal sentiment analysis model using a Kaggle Twitter dataset that has been preprocessed through tokenization, lemmatization, and stopword elimination. Emphasizing the better performance of the LSTM approach, the model attained a training accuracy of 90.98%, a testing accuracy of 80.00%, and a micro-average ROC- AUC score of 0.92. These results show that the model outperforms conventional machine learning techniques in capturing contextual and sequential textual aspects.

</details>


### [74] [From Solvers to Research: Large Language Model-Driven Formal Mathematics at the Research Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07779)
*Eric Jiang,Xiao Liang,Yikai Zhang,Yingjia Wan,Mengting Li,Haikang Deng,Alexander K. Taylor,Justin Baker,Rushil Raghavan,Junyi Zhang,Ying Nian Wu,Andrea L. Bertozzi,Kai-Wei Chang,Raghu Meka,Matthew Sottile,Nanyun Peng,Amit Sahai,Terence Tao,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统回顾了AI4Math领域（特别是LLM驱动的定理证明器）的现状，指出当前系统局限于预定义问题的形式化证明，无法胜任前沿数学研究中发现新定理、解决开放猜想等开放式任务。作者主张AI4Math的下一次飞跃需要从"问题求解器"转向具备严格形式化推理能力的"研究智能体"，并围绕数据集、关系结构、数学探索、工具生态和人机协作等维度提出了未来路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM驱动的定理证明器虽然在形式化证明生成上取得了显著成功，但本质上仍是被动的"问题求解器"，无法应对前沿数学研究中的核心挑战——开放性问题界定不清、涉及多层抽象、需要创造性探索与发现。作者认为必须实现从求解预定义问题到自主进行数学研究的范式转变。

Method: 这是一篇立场论文（position paper），采用系统性综述的方法：首先梳理了AI4Math领域在数据集构建、自动形式化（auto-formalization）和证明合成（proof synthesis）方面的进展，然后从五个关键维度（数据集、关系结构、数学探索、工具生态系统、人机协作）深入剖析现有系统的局限性，最后提出战略性发展路线图。

Result: 作者识别出当前系统的五大核心局限：(1)数据集缺乏前沿数学的开放性问题；(2)缺乏对数学知识关系结构的建模；(3)无法进行自主数学探索与猜想生成；(4)工具生态系统不完善；(5)人机协作模式尚未成熟。基于此提出了未来AI4Math研究智能体的发展路线图。

Conclusion: AI4Math的下一个突破口在于构建能够进行严格形式化推理的数学研究智能体，这需要在数据集设计、知识结构建模、探索机制、工具集成和人机协作等多个层面进行系统性革新，从而真正赋能前沿数学研究。

Abstract: Recent developments in AI for Mathematics (AI4Math), especially Large Language Model (LLM)-driven theorem provers, has achieved remarkable success in formal proof generation for well-defined mathematical problems through Interactive Theorem Proving (ITP) languages. However, current systems remain fundamentally limited in tackling frontier research mathematics, such as discovering new theorems or resolving open conjectures, which are often open-ended, under-specified, and involve multiple layers of abstraction. We argue that the next leap in AI4Math systems requires a decisive shift from predefined problem-solvers to research agents that can address frontier mathematical challenges with rigorous formal mathematical reasoning. In this position paper, we provide a systematic review of the field, covering datasets, auto-formalization, and proof synthesis. More importantly, we identify core limitations of existing systems in serving as mathematical research agents, examining issues across datasets, relational structure, mathematical exploration, tool ecosystem, and human-AI collaboration, outlining a strategic road-map for the future of AI4Math.

</details>


### [75] [DeepSearch-World: Self-Distillation for Deep Search Agents in a Verifiable Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07820)
*Xinyu Geng,Xuanhua He,Sixiang Chen,Yanjing Xiao,Fan Zhang,Shijue Huang,Haitao Mi,Zhenwen Liang,Tianqing Fang,Yi R. Fung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DeepSearch-Evolve 是一个基于自蒸馏的自我进化框架，在可验证的确定性环境 DeepSearch-World 中训练网页智能体，无需依赖更强模型的蒸馏即可在 BrowseComp、GAIA 和 HotpotQA 上取得有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工具使用智能体的自我改进面临两大困境：监督微调依赖固定的教师蒸馏轨迹，缺乏探索空间；而稀疏奖励的强化学习在长程交互中提供极弱的监督信号。需要一个可验证的环境来实现可扩展的自我进化。

Method: 提出 DeepSearch-World（包含 42 万条多跳 QA 任务的可验证确定性环境，基于实体级随机游走构建，支持进度验证、接地反思和失败恢复等认知行为）以及 DeepSearch-Evolve 框架（迭代执行轨迹生成、过滤、数据混合和微调四个阶段来训练更强的智能体）。

Result: DeepSearch-World-9B 在 BrowseComp 上达到 31.2%，在 GAIA 上达到 61.5%，在 HotpotQA 上达到 93.4%，与开源智能体相比具有竞争力，且全程未使用更强模型的蒸馏。

Conclusion: 可验证环境能够为长程网页智能体提供可扩展的自我进化能力，证明了自蒸馏框架在工具使用智能体自我改进方面的有效性。

Abstract: Training tool-use agents to improve from their own experience remains challenging, as supervised fine-tuning relies on fixed teacher-distilled trajectories, while sparse-reward reinforcement learning provides weak supervision for long-horizon interactions. We present DeepSearch-Evolve, a self-distillation framework for web agents built on DeepSearch-World, a deterministic and verifiable environment with reproducible search and page-reading tools. DeepSearch-World contains 420K multi-hop QA tasks constructed from entity-level random walks and supports key agentic cognitive behaviors useful for self-evolving, including progress verification, grounded reflection, and failure recovery. DeepSearch-Evolve iteratively performs trajectory generation, filtering, data mixing, and fine-tuning to train stronger agents. Without distillation from more capable models, DeepSearch-World-9B achieves competitive performance compared with open-source agents, reaching 31.2% on BrowseComp, 61.5% on GAIA, and 93.4% on HotpotQA, showing that verifiable environments enable scalable self-evolution for long-horizon web agents. We will release the environment, 420K training pool, validation set, model, and code to facilitate future research on self-improving deep search agents.

</details>


### [76] [How Do I Know What to Say Next? Barenholtz's Autogenerative Theory as an Enrichment of Harrisean Integrationism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07891)
*J. Mark Bishop,Stephen J. Cowley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将Elan Barenholtz的自生生成语言理论与Roy Harris的整合主义语言学进行综合，以填补后者在符号前瞻开放性结构机制、语言与非语言符号活动的连续性、以及过往整合档案的结构特性三个方面的解释空白，同时保留整合主义的核心承诺，并为LLM研究提供原则性说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Harris的整合主义语言学有力批判了计算语言学中的指称主义传统，认为语言是情境化的双向活动而非映射世界的代码。但整合主义在三个维度存在解释缺口：未能充分说明符号如何维持前瞻开放性的结构机制；对语言与非语言符号活动之间的连续性理论化不足；缺乏对过往整合积累档案的结构特性的详细阐述。

Method: 本文采用理论综合方法，将Barenholtz的自生生成语言理论与Harris的整合主义进行对接。通过概念映射，论证自生生成理论能为整合主义的三个缺口提供填补：（1）为双向沟通中的前瞻开放性提供结构机制；（2）为语言与其他符号活动之间的符号连续性提供计算对应物；（3）为档案——即过往整合积累的残余物的形态及新参与者如何调用它——提供理论说明。

Result: 综合结果显示：自生生成理论能够在保留整合主义对情境化整合行为本体论优先性的同时，补充整合主义自身未能提供的解释内容。对于自然语言处理和LLM设计的研究者而言，该综合提供了一个原则性说明：LLM所高效利用的统计结构本质上是什么，以及这种结构按其本性无法提供什么。

Conclusion: 该理论综合成功弥合了整合主义的解释缺口，不削弱其核心承诺。同时为NLP和LLM研究共同体提供了关于大语言模型能力边界的原则性理解：统计结构能捕捉过往整合的档案残余，但无法替代情境化的整合行为本身。

Abstract: Roy Harris's Integrationist linguistics offers a compelling critique of the referentialist tradition embedded deep at the heart of computational approaches to language, arguing that language is not a code that maps onto a pre-given world but a situated, bipartite activity oriented toward prospective joint action. Yet Integrationism leaves certain explanatory gaps: it does not fully account for the structural mechanism by which signs sustain prospective openness, it undertheorises the continuity between linguistic and non-linguistic semiotic activity, and it offers no detailed account of the structural properties of the accumulated archive of past integrations. This paper argues that Elan Barenholtz's autogenerative theory of language, developed in response to the behaviour of Large Language Models (LLMs), can fill precisely these gaps, enriching Integrationism without undermining any of its core commitments. Specifically, the autogenerative account provides: a structural mechanism for the prospective openness that Harris identifies as central to bipartite communication; a computational correlate for Harris's thesis of semiotic continuity between language and other sign-making activity; and a theory of the archive: what the accumulated residue of past integrations looks like and how new participants draw upon it. The synthesis preserves Harris's ontological primacy of the situated integrative act while adding explanatory content that Integrationism itself does not supply. For practitioners and researchers in natural language processing and large language model design, the argument offers a principled account of what the statistical structure that LLMs so effectively exploit actually is, and of what it cannot, by its nature, provide.

</details>


### [77] [Scalable and Culturally Specific Stereotype Dataset Construction via Human-LLM Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07895)
*Weicheng Ma,John Guerrerio,Soroush Vosoughi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个高效的人机协作标注框架，用于构建西班牙语刻板印象数据集EspanStereo，覆盖欧洲和拉丁美洲多个西班牙语国家，揭示了LLM在不同文化背景下的刻板印象行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM中的刻板印象研究主要集中在英语语境，其他语言数据集匮乏，且对代表性不足文化的手动标注成本高昂。因此需要一种低成本、可扩展的方法来构建多语言刻板印象基准。

Method: 提出人类-LLM协作标注框架：首先利用LLM生成候选刻板印象，再由来自目标文化的本土标注者进行验证和筛选，最终构建EspanStereo数据集。该数据集涵盖多个西语国家，包含文献中已有的刻板印象和英语中心资源中缺失的文化特异性偏见。

Result: 实验表明，支持西班牙语的LLM在不同国家间表现出显著的刻板印象行为差异，凸显了文化根基化评估的必要性。该框架可有效识别细微的、区域性偏见。

Conclusion: 该工作拓宽了LLM刻板印象分析的范围，为跨文化偏见评估奠定了基础。框架可适配其他语言和地区，为构建多语言刻板印象基准提供了可扩展路径。

Abstract: Research on stereotypes in large language models (LLMs) has largely focused on English-speaking contexts, due to the lack of datasets in other languages and the high cost of manual annotation in underrepresented cultures. To address this gap, we introduce a cost-efficient human-LLM collaborative annotation framework and apply it to construct EspanStereo, a Spanish-language stereotype dataset spanning multiple Spanish-speaking countries across Europe and Latin America. EspanStereo captures both well-documented stereotypes from prior literature and culturally specific biases absent from English-centric resources. Using LLMs to generate candidate stereotypes and in-culture annotators to validate them, we demonstrate the framework's effectiveness in identifying nuanced, region-specific biases. Our evaluation of Spanish-supporting LLMs using EspanStereo reveals significant variation in stereotypical behavior across countries, highlighting the need for more culturally grounded assessments. Beyond Spanish, our framework is adaptable to other languages and regions, offering a scalable path toward multilingual stereotype benchmarks. This work broadens the scope of stereotype analysis in LLMs and lays the groundwork for comprehensive cross-cultural bias evaluation.

</details>


### [78] [When Debiasing Backfires: Counterintuitive Side Effects of Preprocessing-Based Stereotype Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07937)
*Yahan Zheng,John Guerrerio,Soroush Vosoughi,Weicheng Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于预处理的刻板印象缓解方法会引发"副作用"——在降低目标群体刻板印象的同时，可能导致其他无关人口统计群体的刻板印象或反刻板印象意外上升，且标准基准测试通常无法检测到这些偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前NLP中广泛使用的基于预处理的刻板印象缓解方法（如去偏语料上的预训练/后训练）虽然在目标群体上有效，但其潜在的意外副作用尚未被充分研究。作者旨在系统揭示这些方法在其他人口统计类别中引发的非意图偏移，并呼吁建立副作用感知的透明缓解实践。

Method: 作者在两种模型架构（仅编码器和仅解码器）上，测试了三种预处理策略（移除刻板印象句子、移除群体提及、交换群体指称），分别在预训练和后训练阶段、不同数据规模下使用Wikipedia进行实验。通过标准基准测试和注意力流分析（attention-rollout）来检测副作用，并评估注意力模式的变化。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 预处理缓解策略在不同模型、策略类型、训练阶段和数据规模下均引发了副作用——其他无关人口统计群体的刻板印象或反刻板印象出现上升；(2) 标准基准测试经常遗漏这些偏移；(3) 注意力流分析显示这些副作用并未伴随注意力模式的大幅变化，使得机制解释更加困难。

Conclusion: 预处理去偏方法存在被忽视的副作用问题，仅关注目标群体指标是不够的。作者呼吁采用副作用感知的评估方式，提供了可操作的诊断方法，并主张建立更透明的缓解实践，以避免在解决一个偏见问题时无意中引入新的偏见。

Abstract: Preprocessing-based methods for stereotype mitigation, such as pre-/post-training on debiased corpora, are widely used in NLP. While these approaches reduce measurable stereotypes for targeted groups, we find they often induce unintended shifts-side effects, where stereotyping or counter-stereotyping can increase relative to neutral baselines for other demographics, including across unrelated demographic categories. We demonstrate these side effects across two model families (encoder-only and decoder-only), multiple preprocessing strategies (removing stereotypical sentences, removing group mentions, and swapping group references), and both pre- and post-training at different data scales on Wikipedia. Standard benchmarks frequently miss these shifts. Using attention-rollout analysis, we observe that such side effects are not accompanied by large changes in attention flow, complicating mechanistic explanations. We discuss implications for evaluation, provide actionable diagnostics, and argue for side-effect-aware, transparent mitigation practices.

</details>


### [79] [A Multi-cluster Boundary Learning Method for Out-of-Scope Intent Detection via MiniLM Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07974)
*Yihong Xu,Mingyu Kang,Linyuan Lü*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于MiniLM嵌入的多簇边界学习方法，将OOS意图检测转化为单类分类问题，通过学习训练话语嵌入的多簇边界来拒绝域外话语，在三个公开数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOS意图检测面临两大挑战：(1) 传统多分类方法随已知意图类别数增加，检测精度下降；(2) 基于LLM嵌入的方法参数量大，难以训练和实际部署。因此需要一种轻量且不受类别数影响的OOS检测方法。

Method: 采用all-MiniLM-L6-v2作为嵌入模型，将OOS意图检测构建为单类分类（one-class classification）工作流。方法从训练话语中学习MiniLM生成的多簇嵌入的边界，随后将域外话语拒绝为OOS意图。核心是多簇边界学习（multi-cluster boundary learning），在CLINC150、StackOverflow、Banking77三个公开数据集上验证，并进行消融实验证明MiniLM对嵌入需求的适应性。

Result: 在CLINC150、StackOverflow和Banking77三个公开数据集上，该方法相比其他基线模型取得了最先进的OOS意图检测性能。消融实验表明，所使用的MiniLM能更好地适应该工作流和话语嵌入需求。

Conclusion: 基于MiniLM嵌入的多簇边界学习方法有效解决了OOS意图检测中类别数增长导致精度下降和LLM嵌入部署困难的问题，具有SOTA性能和实际部署优势。

Abstract: Intent detection is a critical task that bridges human intents and system actions in human-machine interaction systems. However, there still exist challenges for detecting out-of-scope (OOS) intents. (i) The traditional methods view the OOS intent detection as a multi-class classification, then the detection accuracy decreases as the class number of the known intents increases; (ii) LLM-embedding methods require large parameters, that makes them difficult to train and practically deploy. Thus, this work proposes a multi-cluster boundary learning method to detect OOS intents via MiniLM embedding (i.e., all-MiniLM-L6-v2) in an one-class classification workflow. The method learns the boundaries of multi-cluster embeddings generated by MiniLM from the training utterances, and then rejects the out-of-domain utterances as OOS intents. Experiments are conducted on public CLINC150, StackOverflow and Banking77 datasets. The results show that the method achieves the state-of-the-art OOS intent detection performance compared the other baselines. Ablation studies are also conducted and the results show that the used MiniLM can better adapt to the workflow and utterance embedding requirements. The code is available at supplementary materials.

</details>


### [80] [When Implausible Tokens Get Reinforced: Tail-Aware Credit Calibration for LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07976)
*Xiuyi Lou,Zicheng Xu,Yu-Neng Chuang,Hoang Anh Duy Le,Zhaozhuo Xu,Guanchu Wang,Vladimir Braverman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文识别出无critic强化学习中"正信用污染"问题（低概率尾部token获得与合理token相同的正奖励），并提出TACO方法，通过尾部风险评分校准信用分配，抑制对错误token的强化，同时保留对有用稀有模式的累积。实验表明TACO在三个LLM和八个基准上一致优于GRPO基线，并提升了长期RL训练的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无critic强化学习方法（如GRPO）采用均匀信用分配，对同一轨迹中所有token广播相同的advantage，忽视了token间的语义差异。这导致"正信用污染"：上下文错误的低概率尾部token获得与合理token同等的正向强化，使模型不加区分地强化有缺陷的推理行为。作者旨在解决这一失败模式，实现更精细化的token级信用分配。

Method: 提出TACO（Tail-Aware Credit Calibration）方法，包含两个核心步骤：（1）计算尾部风险评分（tail-risk score），结合局部生成上下文评估每个token落入不可靠尾部的风险，区分意外稀有性与探索驱动的不确定性；（2）利用该评分调校高风险token的正向信用，采用渐进式衰减而非直接移除梯度，使真正有用的稀有模式能够累积强化，同时逐步抑制偶然噪声。

Result: 在三个LLM和八个基准上的实验表明，TACO一致性地优于GRPO风格的基线方法；TACO显著提升了训练稳定性，并在长时域RL训练中实现了持续的性能增益。

Conclusion: TACO通过尾部感知的信用校准有效缓解了正信用污染问题，在保持探索能力的同时抑制了错误推理模式的强化，为LLM推理能力的RL训练提供了更可靠的优化策略。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved remarkable success in enhancing the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, widely used critic-free RL methods rely on uniform credit assignment, broadcasting the same advantage to all tokens regardless of their differences. We identify a critical failure mode of this design, which we refer to as Positive-Credit Contamination: low-probability tail tokens that are contextually erroneous receive identical positive credit to plausible ones within the same trajectory, resulting in the indiscriminate reinforcement of flawed reasoning behavior. To mitigate this issue, we propose Tail-Aware Credit calibratiOn (TACO), a method that calibrates uniform credit assignment to suppress undesirable positive updates. TACO first computes a tail-risk score that incorporates the local generation context to assess each token's risk of falling into the unreliable tail, distinguishing unexpected rarity from uncertainty-driven exploration. TACO then uses this score to tune positive credit for risky tokens without removing their gradients entirely, so that recurring useful rare patterns can accumulate reinforcement while incidental noise is progressively dampened. Experimental results across three LLMs and eight benchmarks show that TACO consistently outperforms GRPO-style baselines. Notably, TACO improves training stability, supporting sustained performance gains in long-horizon RL. The source code is available at: https://github.com/xiuyilou/TACO.

</details>


### [81] [A Reliability Assessment of LALM Audio Judges for Full-Duplex Voice Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07985)
*A. Sayyad,J. Emmons,S. Jones,T. Lin,H. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文实证评估了Gemini系列模型（2.5 Flash、3.5 Flash、3.1 Pro）作为音频评判员（LALM）的可靠性，直接对全双工会话的立体声原始波形进行评分。以Gemini 2.5 Flash为基准，在209个立体声会话、8个生产维度上与人评进行对比，结果表明其在多数维度上与人评高度一致，且成本比人工评分低约两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工语音对话系统的评估通常依赖昂贵且耗时的人工评分。作者旨在验证大型音频语言模型（LALM）能否直接基于原始音频波形进行可靠的自动化评分，从而作为人工评分的低成本替代或补充方案。

Method: 以Gemini 2.5 Flash作为基准模型，在209个立体声会话（含152个全双工对话，覆盖13种口音和条件分层，以及57个对抗性缺陷注入片段）上，对8个生产维度进行评分。将LALM评分与三位校准后的人类评分者进行三重对比：(i) Spearman秩相关系数及Bootstrap置信区间重叠检验；(ii) 逐会话的精确一致率；(iii) Newcombe-Wilson置信区间下的缺陷检测灵敏度。同时横向对比了Gemini 3.5 Flash和3.1 Pro的迁移表现。

Result: Gemini 2.5 Flash在8个维度中的5个上，其与人类的Spearman ρ与人类间ρ的差距不超过0.07；7个维度的95% Bootstrap置信区间重叠。在6个维度上，LALM与三人均值在60%–92%的会话中偏差不超过1分。48个（缺陷×维度）组合中，45个组合LALM灵敏度不低于人类。Gemini 3.5 Flash在全部8个维度上简单一致率均有提升；3.1 Pro尽管秩相关系数相近，但多个维度的绝对评分显著偏低，说明仅凭秩相关不足以验证模型替换。

Conclusion: Gemini 2.5 Flash可在实证支持的维度上作为人工评分的替代或第四评分者部署，但需注意四个部署风险点。模型替换时必须重新进行校准验证，不能仅依赖秩相关系数。LALM方案的成本约为人工评分的百分之一。

Abstract: We report the empirical reliability of Gemini models as audio judges that score full-duplex agent conversations directly from the raw stereo waveform, tested across three models in the Gemini family: 2.5 Flash, 3.5 Flash, and 3.1 Pro. Our primary evidence base uses Gemini 2.5 Flash as the ground-truth model, validated against three calibrated human raters on 209 stereo sessions, scored on 8 production dimensions: 152 full-duplex conversations across 13 accent-and-condition strata, together with 57 adversarial defect-injected clips. The evidence for Gemini 2.5 Flash is consistent across three tests. (i) On 5 of 8 dimensions the LALM-human Spearman rho departs from the pairwise human-human rho by at most 0.07, and on 7 of 8 dimensions the two quantities 95 percent bootstrap confidence intervals overlap. (ii) The LALM agrees with the three-rater human mean within 1 point on 60 to 92 percent of sessions on 6 of 8 dimensions. (iii) On 45 of 48 (defect, dimension) cells the LALM is as sensitive as humans or better under Newcombe-Wilson 95 percent confidence intervals, though most of these are underpowered nulls rather than demonstrated parity. Rank-ordering ability transfers across the Gemini family: 3.5 Flash improves simple agreement to 8 of 8 dimensions, while 3.1 Pro rates several dimensions markedly lower than humans despite comparable rank correlation. A model swap should be re-validated on calibration specifically, not assumed from rank-correlation alone. We identify four areas where deployment requires care, and we estimate that human rating alone for our current evaluation cadence costs roughly two orders of magnitude more than the equivalent LALM workload. The data presented here provides a defensible empirical basis for deploying the LALM as a substitute or fourth rater on the dimensions where the evidence supports it.

</details>


### [82] [Hallucination Self-Play: Bootstrapping Reinforced Detector via Evolved Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07993)
*Shiping Yang,Shining Liang,Weihao Liu,Wenbiao Ding,Linjun Shou,Lu Cheng,Angel X. Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出幻觉自博弈（HSP）框架，通过生成器与检测器的迭代对抗训练，使得小规模LLM在缺乏外部监督的情况下，逐步提升幻觉检测能力，在RAGTruth基准上达到甚至超越先进LLM的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将生成器视为静态组件，无法实现检测器的迭代改进；同时高质量标注数据稀缺，限制了忠实性幻觉检测的发展。

Method: 提出HSP框架：检测器和生成器从同一基座模型初始化。首先用人工标注数据微调检测器，然后将其作为奖励模型，通过RLAIF训练生成器以产生更难检测的幻觉回复；进化后的生成器再合成幻觉数据，通过基于规则的强化学习进一步优化检测器，形成迭代自博弈循环。

Result: 在RAGTruth基准和两个模型家族上的实验表明，HSP能够逐步增强小规模LLM，使其幻觉检测能力匹配甚至超越先进LLM，且无需外部监督。

Conclusion: HSP通过生成器与检测器的对抗性自博弈，有效突破了静态生成器的瓶颈，为小模型在幻觉检测任务上提供了可扩展、自进化的训练范式。

Abstract: Identifying faithfulness hallucinations in LLM-generated outputs remains challenging due to the scarcity of high-quality annotated data. Recent work relies on advanced LLMs to synthesize training data, including rationales, labels, and hallucinated claims. However, these methods treat the generator as a static component, limiting iterative improvement of the detector. To address this limitation, we introduce Hallucination Self-Play (HSP), a novel framework that enables the detector to bootstrap with an evolved generator. HSP involves two roles initialized from the same base model, a detector that assesses the faithfulness of model outputs, and a generator that produces increasingly hard-to-detect hallucinated responses. Specifically, the detector is first fine-tuned on human-labeled data and then employed as a reward model to train the generator via reinforcement learning from AI feedback (RLAIF). In turn, the evolved generator synthesizes hallucination data to further optimize the detector through rule-based reinforcement learning. Experiments on RAGTruth benchmark and two model families demonstrate that the proposed framework can progressively enhance a small LLM to match or even outperform advanced LLMs without external supervision. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Hallucination-Self-Play-50B5 .

</details>


### [83] [From Execution to Education: A Bloom-Aligned Framework for Measuring Educational Control in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08009)
*Yi Zhang,Julia Rayz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于Bloom分类法的框架，评估LLM在编程教育任务中控制认知需求的能力。发现模型擅长提升但难以降低认知需求，且强编码能力不意味着强教育控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在解决编程任务上表现优异，但解决任务与为学习者适配任务之间存在差距。需要一种系统化方法评估LLM是否能在保持教学意图的同时，灵活调整任务的认知需求层级，以服务于计算机科学教育。

Method: 以修订版Bloom分类法为认知需求的操作化尺度，定义两种干预场景：一般难度控制（变难/变易）和Bloom控制（定向调整Bloom层级）。使用Qwen3-Next-80B-A3B-Instruct与Qwen3-Coder-Next匹配模型对，在三个基准的2,520个任务上评估。采用语义增量聚类（semantic-delta clustering）和逐层Fisher判别比探测（layer-wise Fisher's Discriminant Ratio probing）进行分析。

Result: 发现稳健的方向不对称性：两个模型均能可靠地提升认知需求，但在降低认知需求上表现困难。通用模型在一般难度和Bloom控制对比上均展现更清晰的中层可分离性；编码模型在一般难度上可分离性较弱，而在Bloom控制对比上呈现更深层的峰值。

Conclusion: 强执行性能（编码能力）并不自动意味着具备Bloom对齐的教育控制能力。LLM在教育场景中的任务适配能力需要被独立评估和专门设计。

Abstract: We introduce a Bloom-aligned framework for measuring educational control in Large Language Models (LLMs): the ability to preserve a task's instructional intent while shifting its cognitive demand toward specified learning objectives. We apply this framework to programming tasks in computer science education to study the gap between solving tasks and adapting them for learners. Using revised Bloom's Taxonomy as an operational scale of cognitive demand, we evaluate two intervention settings: general difficulty control, where models are asked to make tasks harder or easier, and Bloom's control, where models are asked to target higher or lower Bloom's levels. We evaluate a matched Qwen3-Next model pair, comparing Qwen3-Next-80B-A3B-Instruct with Qwen3-Coder-Next across 2,520 tasks from three benchmarks. The framework reveals a robust directional asymmetry: both models reliably increase cognitive demand, but struggle to lower it. We further characterize these outcomes with semantic-delta clustering and layer-wise Fisher's Discriminant Ratio probing. Within this controlled comparison, the general model shows clearer middle-layer separability for both general difficulty and Bloom-control contrasts, whereas the coder model shows weaker separability for general difficulty and a deeper peak for Bloom-control contrasts. These results show that strong execution performance does not automatically entail Bloom-aligned educational control.

</details>


### [84] [Tool-Making and Self-Evolving LLM Agents in Low-Latency Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08010)
*Kalle Kujanpää,Ning Liu,Shahnawaz Alam,Yeshwanth Reddy Sura,Tianyu Yang,Kristina Klinkner,Shervin Malmasi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自演化工具制作流水线，将LLM智能体中重复执行的标准操作步骤编译为经过验证的版本化工具，以替代每次请求时重复生成代码的推理循环。在物流中心告警诊断系统中部署后，p50延迟降低42%，端到端错误率降低53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的LLM智能体每次处理请求时，都会为相同的标准操作步骤（SOP）重复生成代码，造成推理延迟和可靠性的浪费。这种"推理时编码循环"既低效又引入了运行间方差，影响系统稳定性和可审计性。

Method: 提出Agentic Tool-Making Pipeline：在部署前，工具制作器在真实环境中收集执行轨迹、观察后端schema和实际取值，生成候选工具，并利用标注案例进行修复验证；编译产出的工具经版本化管理后直接供生产Agent调用，仅在工具无法覆盖时回退到代码生成。

Result: 在44节点SOP的物流中心告警诊断系统中部署：(1) 工具调用使p50延迟降低42%；(2) 在1500条历史告警上，端到端错误率降低达53%；(3) 工具返回紧凑结构化判定结果，进一步使p50延迟在受控消融实验中降低62%；(4) 版本化工具提升了可审计性，并暴露了规范缺口和上游数据漂移。

Conclusion: 自演化智能体能通过将重复性SOP步骤编译为可复用工具，使工业级LLM系统更快、更可靠且更易于运维，展示了工具化编译在实际生产系统中的显著价值。

Abstract: Production LLM agents often waste latency and reliability by regenerating code for the same procedural steps on every request. We replace this inference-time coding loop with an agentic tool-making pipeline that compiles repeated SOP steps into validated, versioned tools before deployment. The tool-maker grounds synthesis in the live environment as it collects execution traces, observes backend schemas and values, generates candidate tools, and repairs them against labeled cases. At runtime, the production agent calls these tools directly and falls back to code generation only when needed. We deploy the approach in a Fulfillment Center alarm-triage system, where an agent diagnoses alarms against a 44-node SOP over heterogeneous metric backends. In production, tool calls reduce p50 latency by 42%. On 1,500 historical alarms, they reduce end-to-end error rate by up to 53% by suppressing run-to-run variance in repeated steps. Because tools return compact structured verdicts, they also enable a simpler direct-call architecture, reducing p50 latency by a further 62% in a controlled ablation. Versioned tools also improve auditability and expose specification gaps and upstream data drift. Our results show that self-evolving agents can make industrial LLM systems faster, more reliable, and easier to operate.

</details>


### [85] [Can We Trust LLM's Logic? Quantifying Uncertainty, Coherence, and Robustness via a Graph-Based Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08017)
*Riccardo Revalor,Jalees Rehman,Debjit Pal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 GRAPHEVAL 框架，将 LLM 推理的不确定性量化重新定义为推理保真度问题。引入图推理一致性得分 (GRCS) 度量语义结构共识，并提出图自一致性 (GSC) 解码策略，以牺牲名义准确性换取更高推理保真度，揭示小模型中自一致性因侥幸正确而膨胀的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解码策略（如 Self-Consistency）仅评估最终答案一致性，忽略中间推理步骤的逻辑有效性，导致无法检测有缺陷和不忠实的推理。需要一种能够从语义、结构和因果层面量化推理忠实度的方法。

Method: 1) 构建 GRAPHEVAL 图推理框架，将推理链组织为图结构；2) 提出 GRCS (Graph Reasoning Coherence Score) 度量，量化推理空间的语义-结构共识，捕捉病态模式坍塌和自信幻觉；3) 设计 GSC (Graph Self-Consistency) 解码策略，基于 medoid（图中心点）选择推理路径；4) 通过对抗性 medoid 消融实验验证所选路径的"承重"特性。

Result: 1) GRCS 是唯一在大模型和小模型上均与推理忠实度保持一致负相关的度量指标；2) GSC 以名义准确性换取推理保真度，揭示小模型中 SC 因不忠实侥幸猜测而膨胀的现象，同时在更强模型上保持或提升准确性；3) 对抗性消融实验表明 GSC 选择的路径为"承重路径"，偏离该路径会降低推理忠实度甚至准确性。

Conclusion: GRAPHEVAL 框架和 GRCS 度量提供了比传统多数投票更可靠的推理质量评估手段。GSC 解码策略揭示了模型规模与推理忠实度之间的关系，对构建更可信的 LLM 推理系统具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large-Language Models (LLMs) can be prone to flawed and unfaithful reasoning that decoding strategies like Self-Consistency (SC) fail to detect as they evaluate only final-answer agreement while ignoring the logical validity of intermediate steps. This raises three fundamental questions: How can we reliably quantify uncertainty in LLM reasoning? Can semantic, structural, and causal awareness select more faithful reasoning compared to naïve majority voting? and How robust is reasoning topology under adversarial conditions? To address these questions, we introduce GRAPHEVAL, a graph-based reasoning framework that re-frames uncertainty quantification (UQ) as a holistic reasoning fidelity problem. We propose a novel UQ metric, Graph Reasoning Coherence Score (GRCS), that quantifies semantic-structural consensus of the reasoning space and captures pathological mode collapse and confident hallucinations. We find that GRCS is the only metric that is consistently negatively correlated with reasoning faithfulness across both more capable and smaller models. Additionally, we introduce Graph Self-Consistency (GSC), a medoid-based decoding strategy that trades nominal accuracy for reasoning fidelity, exposing the degree to which SC is inflated by unfaithful lucky guesses in smaller models, while preserving or improving accuracy in more capable ones. Finally, through adversarial medoid ablation, we demonstrate that the GSC-selected path acts as a "load-bearing path" and forcing models away from it degrades reasoning faithfulness and, in targeted cases, causes drops in accuracy.

</details>


### [86] [Structured Pruning of Large Language Models via Power Transformation and Sign-Preserving Score Aggregation with Adaptive Feature Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08027)
*Ryota Kobayashi,Tsubasa Hirakawa,Takayoshi Yamashita,Hironobu Fujiyoshi,Yasunori Ishii,Tomoyuki Okuno,Kazuki Kozuka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对将非结构化剪枝技术AFR适配到结构化剪枝时出现的三个核心问题（异构剪枝分数分布不匹配、符号信息丢失、离群值干扰），提出了一种统一方法，结合幂变换、符号保持聚合和百分位离群值去除，在保持与非结构化剪枝相当精度的同时实现结构化剪枝的实际推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应特征保留（AFR）作为一种非结构化剪枝技术效果显著，但将其应用于可实现实际推理加速的结构化剪枝时，面临三个关键障碍：异构剪枝分数之间的分布不匹配、指示优化方向一致性的符号信息丢失、以及离群值的影响。现有方法未能有效解决这些问题。

Method: 提出一种统一的三组件方法：(1) 幂变换（power transformation），用于实现异构剪枝分数的非线性分布对齐；(2) 符号保持的分数聚合（sign-preserving score aggregation），保留优化方向一致性的符号信息；(3) 基于百分位的离群值去除（percentile-based outlier removal），消除离群值对剪枝决策的干扰。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B、Vicuna-v1.5-13B和LLaVA-v1.5-13B三个模型上的实验表明，所提方法在保持与非结构化剪枝相当精度的同时，通过结构化剪枝实现了实际的推理加速。

Conclusion: 通过幂变换、符号保持聚合和百分位离群值去除三者的协同作用，成功将AFR从非结构化剪枝适配到结构化剪枝，有效解决了分布不匹配、符号丢失和离群值干扰三大问题，在多个主流大语言模型和多模态模型上验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: This paper proposes an improved structured pruning method for large language models (LLMs) that addresses key challenges in adapting Adaptive Feature Retention (AFR), an unstructured pruning technique, to structured pruning. When applying AFR to structured pruning, three major problems arise: distribution mismatch between heterogeneous pruning scores, loss of sign information indicating optimization direction consistency, and influence of outliers. To address these issues, we propose a unified approach combining power transformation for nonlinear distribution alignment, sign-preserving score aggregation, and percentile-based outlier removal. Experiments on Llama-3-8B, Vicuna-v1.5-13B, and LLaVA-v1.5-13B demonstrate that our method maintains accuracy comparable to unstructured pruning while achieving practical inference speedup through structured pruning.

</details>


### [87] [PLURAL: A Global Dataset for Value Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08034)
*Dhruv Agarwal,Anya Shukla,Tanya Goyal,Aditya Vashistha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PLURAL 是一个基于92国综合价值观调查的大规模偏好数据集，将调查响应转化为约50万条偏好三元组，覆盖20个国家，用于引导大语言模型实现价值多元化的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在全球广泛使用，但过度反映西方价值观，限制了对多元价值体系的表征能力。需要一种可扩展的资源来实现大语言模型的多元化对齐，使其更好地反映不同国家和文化的价值取向。

Method: 基于 Integrated Values Survey（覆盖92个国家的全国代表性调查），采用两阶段生成流程将调查响应转化为合成的偏好三元组（preference triplets）。该流程在生成真实场景的同时保留原始调查中的规范性价值信号。最终发布包含约50万条偏好三元组、覆盖20个国家的数据集。

Result: (1) 数据集层面验证：PLURAL 保留了原始调查中的跨国价值差异和国内价值多样性；(2) 自动化评估：在 PLURAL 上训练可将与目标国家文化特征的匹配误差（MAE）降低最高27.7%；(3) 盲法人工评估：印度、巴西和日本共176名评估者认为 PLURAL 对齐后的回复更能代表其本国价值。

Conclusion: PLURAL 包含可学习的价值引导信号，为大规模语言模型的多元化对齐提供了可扩展的资源，证明了通过合成偏好数据实现价值引导的可行性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are used worldwide, yet disproportionately reflect Western values, limiting their ability to represent diverse value systems. We introduce PLURAL, a large-scale, value-focused preference dataset grounded in the Integrated Values Survey (IVS), a nationally representative survey spanning 92 countries. Using a two-stage generation pipeline, we transform survey responses into synthetic preference triplets that preserve normative value signals while producing realistic scenarios. We release an initial version of PLURAL containing ~500,000 preference triplets representing people in 20 diverse countries. We evaluate PLURAL in three ways: (i) dataset-level validation showing that it preserves both cross-country value differences and within-country diversity from the original survey; (ii) automated evaluation showing that training on PLURAL improves alignment with target countries' cultural profiles, reducing mean absolute error by up to 27.7% relative to strong baselines; and (iii) blind human evaluation with 176 evaluators in India, Brazil, and Japan, who judge PLURAL-aligned responses as more representative of their national values. Together, these results show that PLURAL contains learnable signal for value steering, offering a scalable resource for pluralistic alignment. Dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/agdhruv/plural-alignment

</details>


### [88] [What LLM Forecasters Know but Don't Say: Probing Internal Representations for Calibration and Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08046)
*Raphaël Sarfati,Pratyush Ranjan Tiwari,Siddharth Boppana,Christopher J. Earls,Srikar Varadaraj,Eric Ho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在预测任务中的内部表征，发现通过探针（probes）提取中间层激活可实现更好的校准，且CoT推理并不忠实反映预测证据；预测答案在推理前已基本确定，据此可节省30-47%的生成token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经微调的LLM预测模型虽准确但校准差，其思维链推理可能不忠实反映预测背后的真实证据。作者希望探究模型内部表征是否能更直接地揭示校准质量和推理忠实度。

Method: 基于Eternis-Forecaster 8B在OpenForesight数据集上，训练表征池化探针（representation-pooling probes）于中间层激活；通过证据消融和干扰注入评估CoT忠实度；利用强制回答范式（forced answering）检查推理前激活中是否已包含最终预测，并根据预回答分布离散度进行路由以节省推理开销。

Result: 探针校准显著优于模型原生输出，该结果在GLM-4.7-Flash和GLM-4.5-Air上也成立；移除关键信息源可改变预测但推理文本不变，探针可作为"测谎器"追踪行为偏移，84%情况下能预测变化方向；单次推理前传递即可恢复最终答案和置信度，按预回答分布离散度路由可节省30-47%token且无损精度。

Conclusion: 内部表征探针是校准、审计和分流LLM预测模型及推理模型的实用工具，为提升预测系统的透明度和效率提供了新路径。

Abstract: Large language models fine-tuned for forecasting can be accurate yet poorly calibrated, and their chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning may not faithfully reflect the evidence behind a forecast. We ask whether internal representations offer a more direct window into both. Working with Eternis-Forecaster 8B on OpenForesight, we train representation-pooling probes on intermediate activations and find they achieve substantially better calibration; a result that also holds for GLM-4.7-Flash and GLM-4.5-Air. We then assess CoT faithfulness through evidence ablation and diversionary injection: removing an influential source in the prompt often changes the model's forecast while leaving the reasoning trace untouched. The same probes function as lie detectors: their activations track behavioral shifts far better than the reasoning trace does, and they also predict the direction of change in 84% of cases, including when the CoT conceals the perturbation's influence. Finally, forced answering reveals that forecasts are largely fixed before reasoning begins: a single pre-reasoning pass recovers the committed answer and confidence, and routing questions by the spread of this pre-set answer distribution saves 30-47% of generated tokens, with no loss of accuracy. Together, these results establish probing internal representations as a practical tool for calibrating, auditing, and triaging language model forecasters and reasoning models more broadly.

</details>


### [89] [Holographic Neural PCFG for Unsupervised Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08063)
*Ryosuke Yamaki,Daichi Mochihashi,Nobutaka Shimada,Tadahiro Taniguchi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Hol-PCFG 将 PCFG 规则评分转化为语法符号嵌入间的代数关系建模，利用 holographic embeddings 的循环相关操作，在环面约束嵌入上为左右子节点和词法发射关系赋予闭式规则概率，以 99.94% 的参数削减量在六种语言上达到 SOTA 解析性能，并可直接从字符级解析日语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Neural PCFG 系列模型虽有强大解析性能，但其规则评分依赖高容量黑箱神经网络，规则概率缺乏可解释的数学形式。该工作旨在赋予 PCFG 规则概率一个具有内在语法结构、可解释的闭式表达。

Method: 提出 Hol-PCFG，将 holographic embeddings（Nickel et al., 2016）中用于知识图谱三元组评分的循环相关（circular correlation）操作，适配到 PCFG 的左子节点、右子节点及词法发射三种关系上。语法符号嵌入被约束在环面（torus）上，每个规则概率因此具有封闭形式，天然携带语法规则的内在代数结构。

Result: 在六种语言上达到无监督句法解析的 SOTA 性能；相比基线模型，规则评分参数量削减 99.94%；训练过程更稳定；在日语上首次实现无需形态分割、直接从字符级进行解析，且几乎保持与词素级相同的性能。

Conclusion: Hol-PCFG 以极简的代数化规则评分替代黑箱神经网络，实现了高效、稳定且可解释的无监督成分句法解析，并展示了跨语言及细粒度（字符级）解析的潜力。

Abstract: Unsupervised constituency parsing aims to accurately induce latent tree structures from raw text alone. Recent neural parameterizations of PCFGs achieve strong performance in both supervised and unsupervised parsing, yet rely on high-capacity black-box networks for rule scoring -- as exemplified by the Neural PCFG family -- leaving rule probabilities without an interpretable mathematical form. In this paper, we propose Holographic Neural PCFG (Hol-PCFG), which recasts PCFG rule scoring as algebraic relation modeling among grammar-symbol embeddings. Hol-PCFG adapts Holographic Embeddings (Nickel et al., 2016), which scores knowledge-graph triples via circular correlation, to the left-child, right-child, and lexical-emission relations over torus-constrained embeddings, giving every rule probability a closed form that carries the intrinsic structure of grammar rules by construction. Hol-PCFG achieves state-of-the-art parsing performance in six languages while cutting rule-scoring parameters by 99.94% relative to the baseline model and training more stably. Additionally, we demonstrate that Hol-PCFG can parse Japanese directly from characters without any morphological segmentation, retaining nearly the same morpheme-level performance.

</details>


### [90] [COBART: Controlled, Optimized, Bidirectional and Auto-Regressive Transformer for Ad Headline Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08071)
*Yashal Shakti Kanungo,Gyanendra Das,Pooja A,Sumit Negi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于前缀控制令牌与BART微调的广告标题生成方法，在生成质量和点击率上均显著优于基线，同时支持长度可控和跨广告格式适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法能自动生成并优化广告标题的点击率，但面对不断演变的广告格式和创意需求，难以生成既优化又定制化的标题。

Method: 使用前缀控制令牌（prefix control tokens）结合BART模型的微调，实现对生成标题的长度控制和优化，且该方法具有架构无关的灵活性。

Result: 相比此前发表的最强基线，Rouge-L指标提升25.82%，预估点击率提升5.82%。

Conclusion: 所提方法在点击率和生成质量上均达到最优，同时具备灵活性和可迁移性，能适应不同架构、创意需求和优化目标。

Abstract: Online ads are essential to all businesses and ad headlines are one of their core creative component. Existing methods can generate headlines automatically and also optimize their click-through-rate (CTR) and quality. However, evolving ad formats and changing creative requirements make it difficult to generate optimized & customized headlines. We propose a novel method that uses prefix control tokens along with BART fine-tuning. It yields the highest CTR and also allows users to control the length of generated headlines for use across different ad formats. The method is also flexible and can easily be adapted to other architectures, creative requirements and optimization criteria. Our experiments demonstrate a 25.82% increment in Rouge-L and a 5.82% increment in estimated CTR over previously published strong ad headline generation baseline.

</details>


### [91] [MASTE: A Multi-Agent Pipeline for Zero-Shot Aspect Sentiment Triplet Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08080)
*Ao Hong,Lehang Wang,Zhirun Yue,Mingxin Wang,Zihan Wang,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MASTE，一种基于多智能体流水线的零样本方面情感三元组抽取方法，将ASTE任务分解为四个顺序子任务，由专用智能体分步处理，无需训练数据和领域示例即可显著超越现有零样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在ASTE任务上零样本性能有限，因为单次生成需同时确定span边界、意见分组和情感极性；少样本上下文学习和思维链提示提升有限且依赖标注数据或精心设计的提示，不适用于零样本部署场景。

Method: 提出MASTE多智能体流水线框架，将ASTE分解为四个顺序阶段：①方面词抽取、②方面词关联的意见词抽取、③未关联意见词配对、④情感极性分类。每阶段由专用智能体处理，并将前序输出作为条件显式传入下一阶段，实现完全无训练的零样本ASTE，且可泛化至不同骨干模型和数据集。

Result: 在四个ASTE基准数据集上的大量实验表明，MASTE在相同骨干模型下显著优于零样本和思维链LLM基线方法，在完全不使用标注三元组的情况下缩小了与全监督方法的差距。

Conclusion: 通过将复杂ASTE任务显式分解为子任务并由多智能体协作，可有效解决单次生成的结构性限制，为无训练零样本结构化情感分析提供了新范式。代码已开源。

Abstract: Aspect Sentiment Triplet Extraction (ASTE) requires jointly identifying (aspect, opinion, sentiment) triples from a given review sentence. While large language models (LLMs) achieve strong zero-shot performance on many NLP benchmarks, their effectiveness on ASTE remains limited, as single-pass generation forces the model to determine span boundaries, opinion grouping, and sentiment polarity in a single decoding step. Common remedies, such as few-shot in-context learning and chain-of-thought prompting, offer only marginal improvements and rely heavily on either in-domain demonstrations sampled from labeled training data or carefully engineered reasoning prompts, neither of which is broadly available in zero-shot deployment. Inspired by the classical agent paradigm, we propose MASTE, a multi-agent pipeline for zero-shot Aspect Sentiment Triplet Extraction. MASTE decomposes ASTE into four sequential stages, where specialized agents handle different compositional subtasks with explicit conditioning on prior outputs. This design enables entirely training-free zero-shot ASTE and generalizes across different backbones and datasets. Extensive experiments on four ASTE benchmarks show that MASTE substantially outperforms zero-shot and chain-of-thought LLM baselines under the same backbone, narrowing the gap to fully supervised methods without using any labeled triplets. Code is available at https://github.com/Hankerlove/MASTE.

</details>


### [92] [COALA: Robust Contextualized Speech-augmented Language Modeling for ASR via Contrastive Regularizer and Biasing Score Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08117)
*Jhih-Rong Guo,Bi-Cheng Yan,Tien-Hong Lo,Berlin Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: COALA 是一个面向语音增强语言模型的上下文偏置框架，通过将 SLM 潜在表示映射到判别空间来量化音频与候选实体的匹配强度，解决了多实体场景下的训练坍塌问题，在 LibriSpeech 上取得了优异的偏置性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音增强语言模型（SLM）存在固有的上下文窗口限制，难以从大规模偏置列表中准确识别相关目标实体；此外，先前方法在处理多目标话语（多个稀有词共现）时会出现训练坍塌问题。

Method: 提出 COALA 框架，将 SLM 的潜在表示映射到一个专门的判别空间，在该空间中量化音频片段与候选实体之间的匹配强度，从而有效筛选大规模偏置列表中的相关实体。

Result: 在 LibriSpeech 基准上的实验表明，COALA 在不同规模的偏置列表下均能一致地取得优越的上下文偏置性能。

Conclusion: COALA 是一种鲁棒的上下文偏置框架，能够有效增强 SLM 在复杂多实体场景下的识别能力，克服了上下文窗口限制和训练坍塌等关键挑战。

Abstract: Contextual biasing seeks to integrate external knowledge into automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems to accurately recognize domain-specific entities. In this paper, we propose COALA (Contextualized ASR Leveraging Biasing Scoring), a robust framework designed to enhance speech-augmented language models (SLMs) in complex multi-entity scenarios. Considering the inherent context-window limitations of SLMs, identifying relevant target entities from a large-scale biasing list is crucial for effective recognition. To this end, COALA maps SLM latent representations into a specialized discriminative space to quantify the matching intensity between audio segments and candidate entities. Furthermore, we address the training collapse in prior study when handling multi-target utterances-where multiple rare words co-occur. Experimental results on the LibriSpeech benchmark demonstrate that COALA consistently achieves superior contextual biasing performance across various biasing list scales.

</details>


### [93] [ICDAR 2026 HIPE-OCRepair Competition on LLM-Assisted OCR Post-Correction for Historical Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08143)
*Maud Ehrmann,Emanuela Boros,Juri Opitz,Andrianos Michail,Florian Wagner,Simon Clematide*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HIPE-OCRepair-2026竞赛的结果，评估了基于大语言模型的历史文档OCR后校正系统。竞赛涵盖英、法、德三种语言的17-20世纪历史报纸和印刷品，采用检索导向的评估方法。四支参赛队伍使用了从零样本提示到持续预训练和微调的策略，结果显示LLM辅助系统可显著提升OCR质量，但性能因数据集、语言和噪声水平而异，低噪声输入上的过度校正是主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模数字化馆藏长期受遗留OCR错误困扰，而大规模重新数字化不切实际。大语言模型为OCR后校正提供了新机遇，但其跨语言、跨文档类型和跨噪声条件的有效性，以及幻觉倾向尚不充分理解，亟需系统性评估。

Method: 构建了HIPE-OCRepair-2026多语言统一数据集，整合现有及新策展的历史文档资源；参赛者在段落/文章级别的连贯转录单元上进行OCR噪声校正，无源图像访问权限；采用检索导向（而非精确转录）的评分方法评估校正效果；参赛系统涵盖零样本提示、持续预训练和微调等多种适应策略。

Result: 现代LLM辅助系统能显著改善OCR质量，但性能在不同数据集、语言和噪声水平间存在明显差异；低噪声输入上的过度校正是一个反复出现的挑战，表明评估不能仅依赖字符错误率的降低。

Conclusion: LLM辅助OCR后校正前景广阔，但需关注跨场景的鲁棒性和过度校正问题。公开的数据集、评分器和评估流程将为后续研究提供可复现的基准框架。

Abstract: We present the results of HIPE-OCRepair-2026, an ICDAR competition on LLM-assisted OCR post-correction of historical documents. OCR post-correction remains a long-standing challenge in digital heritage: large-scale collections of digitized documents are affected by legacy OCR errors, while re-digitization at scale remains impractical. Large language models (LLMs) offers a major opportunity to revisit this challenge, yet their effectiveness across languages, document types, and noise conditions - and their tendency to hallucinate - remains insufficiently understood. HIPE-OCRepair-2026 pursues two objectives: (i) to evaluate the capabilities of modern OCR post-correction systems, and (ii) to provide a reproducible evaluation framework anchored in the HIPE-OCRepair-2026 dataset, a harmonized multilingual resource consolidating existing and newly curated historical datasets. Participants were tasked with correcting noisy OCR transcripts from historical newspapers and printed works in English, French, and German (17th-20th century), working at the level of coherent transcription units (paragraphs or articles) without access to source images. The evaluation adopts a retrieval-oriented rather than diplomatic scoring approach, reflecting the practical use case of search and access over digitized collections. Four teams submitted systems ranging from zero-shot prompting to continued pre-training and fine-tuning, offering insights into the merits of different adaptation strategies. Results show that modern LLM-assisted systems can significantly improve OCR quality, but performance varies across datasets, languages, and noise levels. Over-correction on low-noise inputs emerges as a recurring challenge, highlighting the importance of evaluation beyond character error reduction. The dataset, scorer, and evaluation pipeline are publicly released to support future research.

</details>


### [94] [LEXIC: Lightweight Eye-tracking eXtension via Injected Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08152)
*Sumin Lee,Kyeonghun Kim,Subeen Lee,Jiwon Yang,Tien Nguyen,Ken Ying-Kai Liao,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 基于EyeBench基准，本文探索在纯注视模型中注入词汇级难度信号（GPT-2 surprisal、词频、词长）以提升阅读理解预测性能，提出直接拼接（LEXIC-Concat）和残差机制（LEXIC-Res）两种方法，在OneStop任务上取得一致的AUROC提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本感知模型在EyeBench阅读理解预测上达到56-63% AUROC，而纯注视模型仅处于随机水平。作者试图探索在无需语言模型的情况下，通过注入轻量级词汇难度信号能将纯注视模型推到多远。

Method: 以EyeBench的AhnCNN基线（LEXIC-Base）为基础，提出两种注入三种预计算词汇级难度信号（GPT-2 surprisal、词频、词长）的机制：1) LEXIC-Concat：将信号直接拼接到每次注视的输入中；2) LEXIC-Res：使用一个小型预测头预测典型读者的注视响应，编码器以实际响应与预测的偏差为条件进行建模。采用K=5种子集成训练，十折交叉验证。

Result: 在OneStop阅读理解任务上，两种机制在Unseen Text设定下均取得一致的AUROC提升（+1.8至+2.2个百分点，Wilcoxon p≤0.065）。LEXIC-Concat在Unseen Reader设定上额外提升+2.9个百分点（p=0.010）。LEXIC-Res在Unseen Reader上仅提升+1.8个百分点（p=0.19），归因于预测头对训练读者过拟合，向分布外读者迁移不佳。

Conclusion: 轻量级词汇难度信号注入能有效提升纯注视模型在阅读理解预测上的性能。直接拼接策略更为鲁棒，而残差机制因预测头对训练读者分布校准不足，在跨读者泛化上存在架构性瓶颈。

Abstract: On the recent EyeBench benchmark, predicting reading comprehension from eye movements exposes a stark gap: text-aware models using pretrained language models reach 56--63% AUROC, while gaze-only models operate at chance. We ask how far a gaze-only model can be pushed by lightweight, language-model-free conditioning. Building on the EyeBench AhnCNN baseline, LEXIC-Base, we propose two mechanisms to inject three precomputed word-level difficulty signals, GPT-2 surprisal, word frequency, and word length, into the per-fixation input: direct concatenation, LEXIC-Concat, and a residual mechanism, LEXIC-Res, where a small head predicts typical-reader gaze response and the encoder is conditioned on the deviation. On the OneStop reading comprehension task, with K=5 seed-ensemble training across ten folds, both mechanisms produce statistically consistent AUROC gains on Unseen Text, +1.8 to +2.2 percentage points, Wilcoxon p <= 0.065. LEXIC-Concat additionally lifts Unseen Reader by +2.9 percentage points, p = 0.010. We trace an architectural boundary in LEXIC-Res on Unseen Reader, +1.8 percentage points, p = 0.19, to the prediction head being calibrated to training readers, transferring imperfectly to out-of-distribution readers.

</details>


### [95] [SQuaD-SQL: Efficient Text-to-SQL with Small Language Models via LLM-Guided Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08161)
*Wangyu Wu,Xiaojian Lin,Rong Fu,Zaiyang Yu,Xuhang Chen,Wenjun Yu,Zhenhong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SQuaD-SQL方法，通过知识蒸馏与合成数据生成，使小语言模型在Text-to-SQL任务上接近大模型性能（WikiSQL测试集执行准确率达86.9%），同时显著降低计算资源需求，适合资源受限环境部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）在Text-to-SQL任务上表现优异，但其巨大的计算资源需求阻碍了在资源受限场景下的部署。因此，作者希望探索如何让小语言模型（SLM）通过有效的训练策略，在保持高性能的同时大幅提升推理效率。

Method: SQuaD-SQL包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于LLM的合成数据生成——通过精心设计的提示策略从LLM中提取结构化知识；(2) 参数高效微调——使模型能在单张消费级GPU上完成完整训练；(3) 领域自适应微调——利用领域特定的合成数据进一步提升目标领域的表现。

Result: 在WikiSQL数据集上，SQuaD-SQL在测试集上达到86.9%的执行准确率，接近LLM的性能水平，同时推理速度更快、内存占用更低。

Conclusion: 在适当的训练策略下，小语言模型可以作为大模型在Text-to-SQL任务上的高效、实用替代方案，特别适合资源受限环境中的实际应用。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL is a fundamental task in natural language processing that enables users to interact with structured databases using natural language. While large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance on this task, their substantial computational requirements hinder deployment in resource-constrained settings. In this paper, we introduce SQuaD-SQL (Small-Qualified and Distilled for SQL), a novel approach that empowers small language models (SLMs) to approach the performance of LLMs on the Text-to-SQL task while significantly improving efficiency through knowledge distillation and synthetic data generation. Our method comprises three key components: (1) LLM-based synthetic data generation, where structured knowledge is extracted from LLMs via carefully designed prompting strategies; (2) parameter-efficient fine-tuning, enabling full model training on a single consumer-grade GPU; and (3) domain-adaptive fine-tuning, where domain-specific synthetic data further enhances performance in targeted domains. Experiments on the WikiSQL dataset demonstrate that SQuaD-SQL achieves an execution accuracy of 86.9% on the test set, approaching the performance of LLMs while offering faster inference and lower memory usage. These results suggest that, with proper training strategies, SLMs can serve as practical and efficient alternatives for Text-to-SQL applications in resource-limited environments.

</details>


### [96] [Hidden Decoding at Scale: Latent Computation Scaling for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08186)
*Aiwei Liu,Cheng Shi,Chuhan Wu,Ci Lei,Di Lu,Donald He,Fan Zhang,Fanhao Kong,Feifei Zhang,Guan Wang,Haicheng Wang,Haoyu Liu,Houjin Yu,Jiachen Ding,Jiayi Feng,Jie Zhou,Jijun Chi,Jindi Shi,Jing Lei,Junjie Zhang,Laiyi Li,Le Tian,Linhao Zhang,Miao Fan,Sijun Zhang,Wei Jia,Weiwei Shi,Wenhan Li,Wentao Zhao,Wenteng Liang,Xiao Zhou,Xiaojin Zhou,Xihuai Wang,Xinyu Gao,Xuanliang Wang,Xuyang Ao,Yang Yu,Yangxiu You,Yinuo Zhao,Yufei Kuang,Yufei Wang,Yuan Liu,Yuan Liu,Yuwen Chen,Zhencong Tian,Zhongyin Zhao,Zilin Yu,Zitao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Hidden Decoding，一种沿序列长度维度扩展计算量的方法，通过在继续预训练时将每个 token 扩展为多个流并引入流分解注意力机制，在不改变 Transformer 骨干的情况下实现固定骨干的规模化扩展，并在 100B+ MoE 规模上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 扩展主要依赖扩大 Transformer 骨干，成本高昂；循环深度方法虽可在固定骨干下增加每 token 计算量，但与主流流水线并行训练不兼容。因此需要一种能在固定骨干下增加每 token 计算量、且兼容现有大规模训练范式的方法。

Method: 提出 Hidden Decoding：在继续预训练（CPT）阶段，将每个 token 扩展为 n 条流，每条流拥有独立的嵌入表，并将中间流的 KV 缓存作为上下文保留，使每个 token 在不增加或不扩宽 Transformer 层的情况下执行更多内部计算。同时提出 Stream-Factorized Attention：大多数层仅在各自流内做注意力计算，仅少数层跨流混合，将注意力成本从 n 的二次降低到近似线性。

Result: 在 WeLM-HD4-80B 和 WeLM-HD4-617B（n=4）上训练，均优于匹配的非 HD 基线；随扩展因子 n 增大，性能增益持续提升。Hidden Decoding 成为首个在 100B+ MoE 规模上验证成功的序列长度扩展方法。

Conclusion: 序列长度扩展是一条切实可行的固定骨干规模化路径，Hidden Decoding 为前沿规模 LLM 提供了无需重新预训练骨干即可持续提升性能的有效方案。

Abstract: Scaling Large Language Models (LLMs) has been driven mainly by enlarging the Transformer backbone, but for an already-strong model this requires another round of costly pretraining. We study whether an existing backbone can keep improving by allocating more computation to each token while leaving the Transformer backbone fixed. Depth-recurrent (looped) Transformers pursue this goal but are hard to scale, because looped computation does not fit naturally with the pipeline parallelism used to train the largest models. We add computation along the sequence-length dimension, where the extra computation is simply a longer input and stays compatible with standard large-model training. We propose Hidden Decoding, a sequence-length scaling method applied during continued pretraining (CPT). It expands each token into n streams with independent embedding tables and keeps the intermediate streams' key-value cache as context, so each token performs more internal computation without adding or widening Transformer layers. To keep this affordable at scale, we introduce Stream-Factorized Attention, in which most layers attend only within each stream and only a few layers mix across streams, reducing the attention cost from quadratic to roughly linear in n. Experiments support two scaling results. At frontier scale, we train WeLM-HD4-80B and WeLM-HD4-617B at n=4 and improve their matched non-HD baselines, making Hidden Decoding the first demonstrated sequence-length scaling method at the 100B+ MoE scale. Across expansion factors, the gains grow as n increases, showing that sequence-length expansion is a practical fixed-backbone scaling path for frontier-scale LLMs.

</details>


### [97] [Diarization-Guided Qwen-ASR Adaptation for Multilingual Two-Speaker Conversational Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08208)
*Hao Wu,RongQi Han,Zhen Wang,Wei Liang,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文描述了一个面向MLC-SLM 2026挑战赛Task 1的多语种双说话人对话语音识别系统，结合说话人日志前端与经监督微调、合成语音LoRA适配和GRPO强化学习优化的Qwen3-ASR-1.7B模型，在开发集和评估集上分别达到23.70和17.97的平均tcpMER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语种双说话人对话场景下语音识别面临说话人重叠、语言切换等挑战，需要在说话人日志和语音识别两个维度上协同优化以降低错误率。

Method: 系统采用模块化设计：(1) 说话人日志前端依次执行VAD、子段生成、CAMPPlus说话人嵌入提取、双说话人谱聚类和基于RTTM的音频分割；(2) ASR适配分三阶段——官方数据全量监督微调、基于三条流水线TTS合成语音增强的LoRA微调、以及以WER/CER为奖励并加入幻觉/重复/长度偏差惩罚的GRPO强化学习精调。

Result: 在官方开发集上平均tcpMER为23.70，比基线Qwen-ASR-1.7B降低6.83个绝对点；最终评估集上达到17.97。消融实验表明监督微调增益最大，合成语音LoRA和强化学习进一步提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 模块化说话人日志前端与多阶段ASR适配（监督微调+合成数据LoRA+GRPO强化学习）的组合策略能有效应对多语种双说话人对话场景，显著降低tcpMER，各适配阶段均有正向贡献。

Abstract: This paper describes our self-designed system for Task 1 of the MLC-SLM 2026 Challenge for multilingual two-speaker conversational speech. The system combines a modular speaker diarization front end with a challenge-adapted Qwen3-ASR-1.7B recognizer. The diarization front end performs voice activity detection, subsegment generation, CAMPPlus speaker embedding extraction, two-speaker spectral clustering, and RTTM-based audio segmentation. The resulting speaker-attributed segments are grouped by language or region and decoded by the adapted ASR model. For ASR adaptation, we first perform supervised full fine-tuning on the official training data, then apply LoRA fine-tuning with synthetic speech generated by a three-pipeline TTS-based synthetic speech augmentation framework, and finally refine the model using GRPO reinforcement learning with rewards based on WER/CER and penalties for hallucination, repetition, and length deviation. On the official development set, the full system achieves an average tcpMER of 23.70, reducing the error rate by 6.83 absolute points relative to the released Qwen-ASR-1.7B performance. On the final evaluation set, the system achieves an average tcpMER of 17.97. Ablation results show that supervised fine-tuning provides the largest gain, while synthetic-speech LoRA adaptation and reinforcement learning further improve robustness.

</details>


### [98] [Best-of-$N$ TTS Evaluation is Confounded by ASR Family Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08256)
*Taehyung Yu,Seongjae Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BoN推理通过ASR验证器提升零样本文本转语音的一致性，但验证器的性能排名会随评估所用的ASR家族不同而逆转；论文提出跨家族排序集成方法，在多个独立评估器上取得最低平均WER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示BoN推理中一个被忽视的评估混淆问题：验证器的表现高度依赖评估所用的ASR家族，同一家族的验证器-评估器配对会虚高恢复2-3倍的oracle headroom，这种耦合来自身份/谱系层面而非表征重叠。

Method: 1) 系统性实验：在LibriSpeech-PC test-clean上，用F5-TTS模型，对比Whisper、wav2vec 2.0、HuBERT三个ASR家族分别作为验证器和评估器时的WER表现；2) 线性CKA分析验证表征相似度；3) 提出两种跨家族排序集成策略：排序平均(rank-averaging)和联合最大排序(conjunctive max-rank)。

Result: 跨家族排序集成在N=10时达到1.61%的平均WER（相对F5-TTS基线降低12%），且自动SIM-o/UTMOS指标无退化；最佳单一验证器在官方F5-TTS评估器下将WER从2.06%降至1.72%（-16.5%）。

Conclusion: 验证器与评估器之间的家族耦合是一个严重的评估陷阱；跨评估器三角验证应作为默认报告规范；跨家族排序集成是克服该问题的有效方法。

Abstract: Best-of-$N$ (BoN) inference improves content consistency in zero-shot text-to-speech by selecting from $N$ candidates with an automatic speech recognition (ASR) verifier. We identify an underexplored evaluation confound: a verifier's apparent quality depends strongly on which ASR family judges it. On LibriSpeech-PC test-clean~\citep{librispeechpc} with F5-TTS~\citep{f5tts}, verifier rankings reverse across Whisper, wav2vec~2.0, and HuBERT evaluators, and same-family verifier-evaluator pairs recover 2-3$\times$ more oracle headroom than cross-family pairs despite near-identical representations (linear CKA $0.978$) -- a pattern consistent with identity- or lineage-level coupling rather than representational overlap. We propose two \textbf{cross-family rank ensembles} (rank-averaging and conjunctive max-rank) that attain the lowest mean WER across three independent evaluators -- $1.61\%$ at $N{=}10$ ($-12\%$ relative to F5-TTS) -- with no measurable degradation under automatic SIM-o/UTMOS metrics; the best single verifier drives WER from $2.06\%$ to $1.72\%$ ($-16.5\%$) under the official F5-TTS evaluator. We recommend cross-evaluator triangulation as default reporting practice.

</details>


### [99] [XALPHA: A Memory-Driven AI Quant Researcher for Hypothesis-to-Code Alpha Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08332)
*Fengyuan Liu,Yuchen Fu,Yuqi Wang,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: XAlpha是一个记忆驱动的AI量化研究员智能体，通过多源研究记忆系统将Alpha因子挖掘从孤立的因子生成转变为"阅读-假设-编码-验证-反思-进化"的闭环研究过程，在CSI300上表现优于代表性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Alpha发现方法（因子设计、机器学习、进化搜索、LLM框架）大多仅自动化孤立步骤，缺乏端到端的量化研究员能力——无法吸收外部知识、无法闭合"假设到代码验证"的循环、无法从累积的发现反馈中学习。

Method: XAlpha包含：(1)多源研究记忆系统，整合研报金融知识与历史发现反馈；(2)Macro Brain负责任务主题规划和Archetype选择；(3)Micro Brain将假设转化为可执行因子代码，并进行"假设-代码逻辑-金融合理性"三方对齐验证；(4)Cross Brain将实证结果整合为生成级反馈、周期级总结和Archetype级研究线索，驱动持续进化。

Result: 在CSI300数据集上的实验表明，XAlpha在整体Alpha发现性能上优于多个代表性基线方法。

Conclusion: XAlpha通过记忆驱动的闭环研究架构，实现了从单步自动化到持续进化的端到端量化研究的范式转变。

Abstract: Financial markets are noisy, non-stationary, and high-dimensional, making it difficult to discover predictive and robust trading signals. Alpha discovery has evolved from manual factor design to machine learning, evolutionary search, and recent LLM-based frameworks, improving the efficiency of factor generation, search, and evaluation. However, existing methods still mostly automate isolated steps, rather than functioning as end-to-end quant researchers that can absorb external knowledge, close the hypothesis-to-code validation loop, and learn from accumulated discovery feedback. To fill this gap, we introduce XAlpha, a memory-driven AI Quant Researcher for continuous hypothesis-to-code alpha discovery. XAlpha maintains a multi-source research memory system that integrates report-grounded financial knowledge with discovery feedback from prior generations and research cycles. Guided by this memory system, a Macro Brain plans research themes and selects suitable Archetypes; a Micro Brain transforms the planned hypothesis pool into executable factor code and verifies ex-ante tri-alignment among the hypothesis idea, code logic, and financial plausibility; and a Cross Brain consolidates empirical outcomes into generation-level feedback, cycle-level summaries, and archetype-level research cues for future exploration. In this way, XAlpha turns alpha mining from isolated factor generation into a closed-loop research process that continuously reads, hypothesizes, implements, validates, reflects, and evolves. Experiments on CSI300 show that XAlpha achieves stronger overall alpha discovery performance than representative baselines.

</details>


### [100] [TypeProbe: Recovering Type Representations from Hidden States of Pre-trained Code Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08339)
*Giuliano Gorgone,Fausto Carcassi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 探究预训练代码模型内部是否编码了类型信息，发现跨语言的类型表征即使在无类型代码中也会涌现，且线性探针可跨语言推断函数应用的结果类型，该结构对词法扰动和语法变化具有部分鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的代码模型性能卓越，但模型内部是否编码了形式化类型语义尚不清楚。此前代码模型可解释性研究未直接针对形式类型语义或跨语言类型表征，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 使用Java和Python代码示例的平行数据集，对预训练代码模型的残差流进行探针探测；训练线性探针在一种语言上推断另一种语言中函数应用的参数类型和结果类型；测试类型表征对词法扰动和跨语言语法变化的鲁棒性。

Result: 即使在无类型代码上，跨语言类型表征也会自然涌现；隐藏状态线性编码了类型化函数应用所隐含的结果类型，探针可实现跨语言类型推断；该结构对词法扰动和跨语言语法变化具有部分鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 预训练代码模型内部确实涌现了跨语言的形式类型语义表征，这为理解代码模型的工作原理提供了新视角，也暗示类型信息是代码理解的内在学习信号。

Abstract: State-of-the-art code models achieve impressive performance, yet the extent to which they internally encode type information remains poorly understood. We probe the residual streams of pretrained code models for internal type representations using a parallel dataset of Java and Python code examples. Our results show that cross-lingual type representations emerge even from untyped code. Moreover, we test whether hidden states linearly encode the result type implied by typed function application by training probes on one language to infer argument and result types in the other. Finally, we find that this structure is partly robust to lexical perturbations and cross-language syntactic variations. To the best of our knowledge, prior work on interpretability of code models has not directly targeted formal type semantics or cross-lingual type representations. We release our code and datasets.

</details>


### [101] [Grounded Event Extraction from SEC 8-K Filings with a Fine-Grained Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08346)
*Rian Dolphin,Joe Dursun,Jarrett Blankenship,Katie Adams,Quinton Pike*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段系统，利用大语言模型对8-K文件进行细粒度事件标注（119种事件类型的三层分类体系），并通过引文锚定与质量评分机制确保标注可追溯、可靠。在292,984份文件中生成601,088个有据可查的事件标签，精度随质量评分单调递增（12%→96%），并通过事件研究验证了分类体系的经济学区分能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前8-K文件的SEC事项代码过于粗糙：同一代码既涵盖常规行政变更又涵盖CEO离职等重大事件，许多最具市场影响力的披露被归入兜底类别。大语言模型使大规模细粒度标注成为可能，但标注必须可追溯到原文且经过可靠性验证。

Method: 设计了一个两阶段系统：(1) 第一阶段将输出约束到有效分类条目，并通过模糊n-gram验证将每个标签锚定到原文逐字引用；(2) 第二阶段对每条引用根据类别定义重新评分，生成质量分数。分类体系为三层结构，共119种事件类型。在2022-2026年的292,984份申报文件上部署，并在5,125个分层标签上进行LLM评审验证。

Result: 系统产出601,088个有据可查的事件标签并公开发布。精度随质量评分单调递增，从12%升至96%，而无依据标签比例从8%降至接近零。消融实验表明，质量分数仅在独立的第二阶段评分时才具有校准性。基于未签名异常收益率的事件研究证实，该分类体系能够区分共享同一事项代码但经济含义不同的事件。

Conclusion: 通过"锚定引用+独立质量评分"的两阶段设计，LLM可以在8-K文件上实现高质量、可追溯的大规模细粒度事件标注，且分类体系具有实际的经济学区分效力。

Abstract: Form 8-K filings are the primary channel through which U.S. public companies disclose material events, but the SEC item codes attached to them are coarse: a single item spans routine administrative changes and chief executive departures, and many of the most market-moving disclosures fall into a catch-all item. Large language models make fine-grained labelling feasible at corpus scale, but only if the labels can be traced to the source text and shown to be reliable. We present a two-stage system that tags 8-K disclosures against a three-tier taxonomy of 119 event types. The first stage constrains output to valid taxonomy entries and anchors every tag to a verbatim quote via fuzzy n-gram validation; the second re-grades each cited quote against the category definition to produce a quality score. Applying the system to 292,984 filings from 2022 to 2026 yields 601,088 grounded event tags, which we release. Over 5,125 stratified tags, an LLM judge finds precision rises monotonically with the quality score, from 12% to 96%, while unsupported tags fall from 8% to near zero. Ablation shows the score is calibrated only when assigned in a dedicated second pass. An event study on unsigned abnormal returns confirms, without any language model, that the taxonomy separates economically distinct events sharing an item code.

</details>


### [102] [Echoes Across Vietnam's Highlands, Delta, and Coast: A Multilingual Corpus for Cham, Khmer, and Tay-Nung](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08362)
*Anh Trac Duc Dinh,Khang Nhat Hoang Vo,Vinh Cong Doan,Tai Tien Ta,Khoa Duc Anh Lam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了首个越南少数民族语言（占语、高棉语、岱依-侬语）NLP语料库CKTN，揭示了现有多语言编码器对这些语言的严重碎片化问题，并提出了一种脚本感知的自适应方法，显著改善了语言表征和分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越南少数民族语言（占语、高棉语、岱依-侬语）在NLP领域几乎空白。这三类语言在文字系统、与越南语接触程度及标准化程度上差异巨大，导致标准多语言自适应方法容易学到错误的信号。此外，常用的自适应评估指标（如语言建模损失、基于词汇重叠的检索）可能产生误导，无法反映真实的语义泛化能力。

Method: 1) 构建CKTN语料库：涵盖占语(Cham)、高棉语(Khmer)、岱依-侬语(Tay-Nung)，包含44,367篇文档、24M子词token，覆盖持续预训练、类别分类和摘要-文档检索三个任务。2) 提出脚本感知自适应方案：词汇增强（vocabulary augmentation）结合校准替换token预训练（calibrated replaced-token pretraining），防止判别器利用表层脚本差异进行投机，从而迫使模型学习深层语义表征。

Result: 现有多语言编码器对这三种语言产生了严重的表征碎片化；常用自适应指标存在误导性——模型可能在语言建模损失上表现良好或在基于词汇重叠的检索任务中表现优异，但在跨文档语义泛化上仍然失败。所提出的脚本感知自适应方案显著减少了碎片化，并在分类任务上取得了最优性能，同时暴露了词汇重叠检索作为评估信号的局限性。

Conclusion: 针对文字系统差异显著的弱势语言，脚本感知的自适应策略是关键。仅依赖语言建模损失或词汇重叠检索等传统指标不足以评估模型的真实语义理解能力，需要更全面的跨文档语义评估范式。

Abstract: Vietnam's ethnic minority languages are almost absent from the field of Natural Language Processing (NLP), and the challenge goes beyond data scarcity: Cham, Khmer, and Tay-Nung differ sharply in script, Vietnamese contact, and standardization, conditions under which standard multilingual adaptation can learn the wrong signals. We introduce CKTN, the first corpus and benchmark for these languages (44,367 documents, 24M subword tokens), spanning continued pretraining, category classification, and summary-document retrieval. We show that existing multilingual encoders severely fragment these languages, and that common adaptation metrics can mislead: models may lower language-modeling loss or excel at lexical-overlap retrieval while still failing at semantic generalization across documents. We address this with a script-aware adaptation recipe - vocabulary augmentation combined with calibrated replaced-token pretraining - that prevents the discriminator from exploiting trivial script mismatches. The result is an encoder with substantially less fragmentation and the strongest classification performance among evaluated models, exposing the limits of lexical-overlap retrieval as an evaluation signal.

</details>


### [103] [Large-Language-Models-as-a-Judge in Theory-Agnostic Adaptive Metric-Alignment for Prototypical Networks in Personality Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08374)
*Jing Jie Tan,Ban-Hoe Kwan,Danny Wee-Kiat Ng,Yan-Chai Hum,Shih-Yu Lo,Po-An Chen,Noriyuki Kawarazaki,Kosuke Takano,Anissa Mokraoui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: JAM is a theory-agnostic framework for personality recognition that learns unified latent pseudo-facets from heterogeneous datasets without relying on predefined psychological taxonomies, using an Attention-Pooled Graph Prototypical Network, Cross-Theory Harmonization, and LLM-as-a-Judge mechanisms to achieve improved cross-framework generalization.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人格识别受限于依赖特定心理学理论框架的标注数据，不同理论对同一潜在特质可能给出部分甚至不一致的标注，导致模型泛化能力受限。本文的动机在于：人格本身应是理论不变的（theory-invariant），因此需要一种不绑定任何特定分类法的理论无关框架，从异构数据中发现共享的底层行为结构。

Method: 提出JAM框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) Attention-Pooled Graph Prototypical Network，通过嵌入空间中的聚类学习结构化表征；(2) Cross-Theory Harmonization (CTH)，结合 Human-Guided Linkage（人工引导的关联）与 Machine-Induced Consensus（机器诱导的共识），在不依赖预定义标签的情况下统一异构数据集；(3) LLM-as-a-Judge 机制，以 LLM-before-the-loop 和 LLM-in-the-loop 两种配置识别模糊样本，引导自适应度量学习。

Result: 实验表明，JAM在跨框架泛化能力和整体性能上均有提升，在低资源人格理论场景下表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: JAM向理论无关的人格推理迈出了坚实一步，展示了不依赖特定理论标签、从文本直接推断个体潜在心理画像的可行性，并为低资源人格理论提供了有效支持。

Abstract: Personality recognition has traditionally been constrained by theory-dependent formulations, where models are trained to fit predefined psychological taxonomies rather than uncovering shared underlying behavioral structure. This limits generalization, as personality itself is better understood as theory-invariant, while existing annotations reflect only partial and sometimes inconsistent views of the same latent traits. In this work, we introduce JAM ((J)udge for (A)daptive (M)etric-Alignment), a theory-agnostic framework that shifts learning from adapting to predefined personality theories toward discovering unified latent pseudo-facets that capture shared psychological structure. Rather than constraining the model to any personality taxonomy during training or inference, the framework learns generalizable psychological representations and can infer an individual's latent psychological profile directly from the textual samples, without requiring theory-specific labels. JAM achieves this through an Attention-Pooled Graph Prototypical Network that learns structured representations via clustering in embedding space, together with a Cross-Theory Harmonization (CTH) approach that integrates (i) Human-Guided Linkage and (ii) Machine-Induced Consensus to unify heterogeneous datasets without relying on predefined labels. To further improve robustness and data quality, we incorporate an LLM-as-a-Judge mechanism operating in two configurations, (i) LLM-before-the-loop and (ii) LLM-in-the-loop which identifies ambiguous samples to guide adaptive metric learning. Experiments show that JAM improves cross-framework generalization and performance, establishing a strong step toward theory-agnostic personality inference and supporting low-resource personality theories. The related code repository, model weights, and artifacts are available at https://research.jingjietan.com/JAM

</details>


### [104] [Prompt Compression via Activation Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08399)
*Thibaud Ardoin,Semira Einsele,Evis Bregu,Gerhard Wunder*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM指令提示中的任务相关信息压缩为单一激活向量，并在早期层重新注入以替代原始token序列，仅造成不到2%的精度损失，同时揭示了LLM激活空间中的跨层兼容性和语义可恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM处理固定指令提示时，每次查询都需在数十层中传播完整的token序列，造成重复计算。研究者希望探究提示中的任务相关信息能否被压缩为单一激活向量，从而在后续查询中绕过对原始提示的重复处理，降低计算开销。

Method: 从中间层提取激活值，学习一组权重对其进行加权求和，得到一个紧凑的压缩向量；将该向量注入目标LLM的早期层，替代原始token序列的逐层传播。

Result: 压缩向量能有效保留任务相关信息，相比完整提示处理，精度下降不超过2%。此外，分析揭示了三个激活空间的结构特性：中间层表示可向早期层有意义地迁移、单一激活向量可编码可量化的语义信息、加权求和是一种鲁棒的表示压缩方式。

Conclusion: 指令提示可被有效压缩为单一激活向量并跨层注入，这既为固定提示场景下的推理效率优化提供了新思路，也为理解LLM内部激活空间的信息编码结构提供了实证洞见。

Abstract: Large language models process prompts by propagating activations through dozens of layers before generating a response. We ask whether the task-relevant information contained in an instruction prompt can be compressed into a single activation vector and re-injected into the model, replacing the original token sequence? We show this is achievable using a learned weighted sum of activations extracted at an intermediate layer and injected at an early layer of the target LLM. The compressed vector preserves task-relevant information, incurring an accuracy drop of under $2\%$ relative to full prompt processing. Beyond its practical implications, including reducing per-query computation for fixed instruction prompts without reprocessing the original token sequence, our analysis reveals structure in the activation space of LLMs: (i) mid-layer representations transfer meaningfully to early layers, suggesting a degree of cross-layer compatibility in how information is encoded; (ii) a single activation vector encodes a quantifiable and recoverable amount of semantic information; (iii) a weighted sum of activations is a robust representation compressor.

</details>


### [105] [When Synthetic Speech Is All You Have: Better Call GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08409)
*Shashi Kumar,Yanis Labrak,Hasindri Watawana,Sergio Burdisso,Esaú Villatoro-Tello,Kadri Hacioğlu,Petr Motlicek,Andreas Stolcke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对银行等受监管领域ASR系统因隐私限制难以获取真实语音数据的问题，提出用强化学习（GRPO）替代监督微调（SFT）来适配合成语音，将WER相对降低40-45%，并分析指出性能提升源于行为优化（插入错误减少、注意力对齐改善）而非表征变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银行等受监管领域的LLM-based ASR面临隐私瓶颈：真实语音数据采集成本高且受法律约束，合成TTS数据成为替代方案，但合成语音与真实录音存在声学不匹配问题。此前解决该问题的工作仅限于监督微调（SFT），亟需探索更有效的方法来从有限的合成语音中提取更多价值。

Method: 采用Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO），一种无critic的强化学习方法，以低WER假设作为奖励信号进行合成语音适配。实验对比了纯GRPO适配和SFT+GRPO两阶段组合策略，并深入分析了GRPO与SFT在行为层面和表征层面的差异。

Result: 纯GRPO适配相比SFT将WER相对降低40%（36.71%→22.09%），SFT+GRPO组合进一步将相对降低推至45%。分析表明，GRPO的增益源于行为层面：通过改善停止校准（stopping calibration）显著减少插入错误，并通过更好地将注意力锚定到音频上来改善语音-文本对齐；而早期层的表征则未被改变。

Conclusion: 当合成语音是主要资源时，强化学习应优先于监督微调。GRPO通过优化解码行为而非改变表征来实现更高效的领域适配。

Abstract: LLM-based ASR adapted to regulated domains such as banking is bottlenecked by privacy: real speech is costly and legally constrained to collect, making synthetic text-to-speech (TTS) an attractive substitute. Yet synthetic speech stays acoustically mismatched with real recordings, and work on this gap has stayed within supervised fine-tuning (SFT). We instead turn to reinforcement learning, and show that Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) extracts far more from the same synthetic speech than SFT. Synthetic-only adaptation of the model with GRPO, a critic-free method rewarding low-WER hypotheses, reduces WER by 40\% relative to SFT (36.71\%$\to$22.09\%), and an SFT-then-GRPO combination pushes this further to 45\%. We trace the gain to behavior rather than representation: GRPO reduces insertion errors by improving stopping calibration and speech-to-text alignment by better anchoring attention to audio, leaving early-layer representations intact. When synthetic speech is the main resource, reinforcement learning should be preferred over supervised fine-tuning.

</details>


### [106] [Detecting Ladder Logic Bombs in IEC 61131-3 PLC Programs using ESBMC-PLC+: A Formal Verification Approach with Trigger Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08417)
*Pierre Dantas,Lucas Cordeiro,Waldir Junior*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ESBMC-LLB 通过形式化验证检测 PLC 中的梯形逻辑炸弹，首次暴露功能块内部逻辑，利用 k-induction 实现无界证明，在多个数据集上高效检测并恢复触发器，与 CFG-triage 形成互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯形图验证工具会将功能块（function-block）体从中间表示（IR）中丢弃，导致隐藏在功能块内部的恶意逻辑（LLB）对证明器不可见。此外，现有检测方法（签名、异常、CFG-triage）缺乏对触发器（trigger）的无界证明保证，且无法处理自适应触发器。

Method: 提出 ESBMC-LLB，以 ESBMC-PLC+ 为验证引擎，增加建模层以暴露功能块逻辑，将炸弹检测重新定义为形式化验证问题：通过扫描看门狗（scan-watchdog）暴露非终止载荷，通过输出连线暴露执行器伪造载荷，将其转化为安全违规。使用 k-induction 实现跨所有扫描的无界炸弹缺失证明，有界模型检查器返回的反例即为触发器。

Result: 在 Iacobelli 2024 公开数据集上，检测全部 30 个炸弹并恢复每个触发器，包括 CFG-triage 无法检测的自适应触发器（计算型、不透明算术型、多扫描型）。在 PLC-Defuser 的 SWaT 语料库上首次进行语义模型检查器评估：模拟扩展使整个语料库可解析；v1.0.0 上检测到 149/150 个炸弹（99%），零误报；后续版本中因 SMT 求解器对非线性非终止炸弹超时，检测率降至 49%。
