Conclusion: 语义模型检查与 CFG-triage 是互补的：前者提供无界证明、对自适应触发器鲁棒，并处理布尔/整数和线性模拟逻辑；后者擅长非线性模拟非终止场景。文中明确划分了各自的优势边界。

Abstract: A Ladder Logic Bomb (LLB) is malicious control logic in a Programmable Logic Controller (PLC) program that lies dormant until a trigger activates a payload to manipulate actuators, forge sensor readings, or deny operator control. We observe that real malicious logic hides inside function-block bodies, which existing ladder-diagram verifiers drop from their intermediate representation (IR), making bombs invisible to provers. We present ESBMC-LLB, which uses ESBMC-PLC+ as its verification engine and adds a modeling layer that exposes function-block logic and recasts bomb detection as a formal verification problem: a scan-watchdog exposes non-termination payloads, and output wiring exposes actuator-forgery payloads as safety violations. k-induction gives an unbounded proof of bomb-absence across all scans, and the bounded model checker returns a counterexample that is the trigger - guarantees that signature, anomaly, and CFG-triage detectors lack. On the public Iacobelli 2024 dataset, ESBMC-LLB detects all 30 bombs and recovers every trigger; it also detects adaptive triggers (computed, opaque-arithmetic, multi-scan) that evade CFG-triage. We also report the first semantic model-checker evaluation on PLC-Defuser's SWaT corpus: our analog extension makes the full corpus parseable; on v1.0.0, it detects 149/150 bombs (99%) with zero false positives, recovering each trigger; on a later version with nonlinear non-termination bombs, detection drops to 49% as the SMT solver times out. We conclude that semantic model checking and CFG-triage are complementary - the former gives unbounded proofs, adaptive-trigger robustness, and handles Boolean/integer and linear analog logic; the latter leads to nonlinear analog non-termination, and we delineate where each wins.

</details>


### [107] [Two Axes of LLM Abstention: Answer Correctness and Question Answerability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08456)
*Benedikt J. Wagner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文揭示了模型在回答问题时存在两个独立维度：答案正确性（answer-confidence）和问题可回答性（answerability）。现有单一置信度阈值无法区分二者，而通过在隐藏状态上训练线性探针可有效检测不可回答/虚假前提问题。作者据此提出双轴校准策略，在可控不可回答率的同时保证正确回答覆盖率，显著优于单一阈值方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模型拒绝机制依赖单一置信度分数，无法区分"模型会答错的问题"和"本不该回答的问题"（如不可回答或基于虚假前提的问题）。这导致模型要么过度回答错误问题，要么错误拒绝可回答的问题。作者希望解耦这两个维度，实现更精细的拒绝策略。

Method: 在5个指令微调模型（2B-14B，覆盖三个家族）上进行实验：1）分析答案置信度与可回答性之间的相关性，发现二者近乎正交；2）在隐藏状态上训练线性探针来检测问题是否可回答；3）在CREPE（自然虚假前提问题）数据集上验证探针性能；4）提出双轴校准策略，分别设定可回答性分数和正确性分数的阈值，仅当两者均达标时才回答；5）对比直接指令模型检查前提的效果（发现会适得其反），并展示用探针路由指令可将挑战精度提升约三倍。

Result: 1）答案置信度跟踪答案正确性，但对问题可回答性几乎盲视；隐藏状态探针则相反。2）在CREPE虚假前提问题上，P(IK)、P(True)及直接询问模型前提是否错误均接近随机，而隐藏状态探针AUROC达0.69-0.77——模型内部表征了问题却不会报告。3）直接指令模型检查前提适得其反，57%的挑战为误报；用探针路由同一指令将挑战精度提高约三倍。4）双轴策略在0.75正确覆盖率下可同时认证两个预算，而单一阈值仅0.31；在14B模型上，双轴是唯一能认证的策略。

Conclusion: 答案正确性与问题可回答性是模型行为中两个独立轴，盲点不随模型规模增大而缩小。隐藏状态中已编码了模型不会口头报告的问题信息，这一缺陷可通过探针修复。双轴校准策略提供了一种更精细、可认证的拒绝机制，在可控性与覆盖率之间实现了更好的平衡。

Abstract: A model should refuse two different things: answers it would get wrong, and questions it should not answer at all, such as unanswerable ones or ones resting on a false premise. The usual recipe thresholds a single confidence score, which cannot tell these apart. Across five instruction-tuned models from three families (2B to 14B), we find they are separate axes. Ordinary answer-confidence tracks whether an answer is right but is nearly blind to whether the question is answerable; a linear probe on hidden states does the reverse. The blind spot does not shrink with scale. It is worst on naturally occurring false-premise questions (CREPE). There, answer-confidence, P(IK), P(True), and even asking the model outright whether a premise is false all stay near chance, while a hidden-state probe reaches 0.69 to 0.77 AUROC: the model represents a problem it will not report. This turns out to be fixable. Instructing a model to check premises backfires, because it then disputes sound and false premises alike (57% false challenges), unable to tell them apart; routing the same instruction with the probe roughly triples challenge precision. We turn the two axes into a calibrated policy that answers only when an answerability score and a correctness score each clear a separately certifies behave differently: the unanswerable-answer rate is controllable at every scale, while the wrong-answer rate is capped by model accuracy, so the guarantee tightens as threshold policy certifies both budgets at 0.75 coverage of correct answers, against 0.31 for a single threshold; at 14B it is the only policy that certifies at all.

</details>


### [108] [Cross-seed explainability using Procrustes-conditioned Joint End-to-end Top-K Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08499)
*Bendegúz Váradi,Zoltán Kmetty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种Procrustes条件化的联合端到端Top-K稀疏自编码器，用于从独立训练的BERT模型中提取跨种子通用特征。通过正交Procrustes旋转对齐激活空间后联合训练SAE，在三个基准数据集上实现了跨种子特征通用性（Pearson r ≥ 0.70），优于事后对齐基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性中，由于字典学习的非凸性，独立训练的神经网络会学到未对齐的特征空间，即使表面上相同的特征也可能因随机初始化而不同。这构成了跨种子特征通用性的根本挑战，即需要提取在不同随机种子下训练的同架构模型中稳定存在的通用特征。

Method: 提出Procrustes条件化的联合端到端Top-K稀疏自编码器（SAE），核心步骤包括：(1) 在联合SAE训练前，计算两个种子模型激活空间之间的正交Procrustes旋转进行对齐；(2) 结合Top-K稀疏性约束；(3) 端到端下游任务优化；(4) 引入基于先前SAE文献的辅助死特征复活损失，防止特征退化。

Result: 在五对独立种子（共十个BERT模型）和三个基准数据集（SST-2、Stanford Politeness、TweetEval Emotion）上评估，完整流程在所有三个数据集上均实现了跨种子特征通用性（Pearson r ≥ 0.70），显著优于事后对齐基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过Procrustes对齐与联合SAE训练的协同，可以有效克服不同随机种子下BERT模型特征空间的失配问题，提取出高通用性的可解释特征。最小定性分析证实，高通用性特征编码了可解释的社会语言学模式。

Abstract: We present a Procrustes-conditioned Joint End-to-end Top-K Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) for extracting cross-seed universal features from independently trained BERT models. Cross-seed feature universality is a fundamental challenge in mechanistic interpretability: because dictionary learning is non-convex, independently trained networks learn misaligned feature spaces, so apparently identical features may differ by random initialization. We address this by computing an orthogonal Procrustes rotation between seeds' activation spaces before joint SAE training, combining Top-K sparsity, end-to-end downstream optimization, and an auxiliary dead-feature revival loss based on previous SAE literature. Evaluating on five independent seed pairs (ten BERT models) across three benchmark datasets (SST-2, Stanford Politeness, TweetEval Emotion), our full pipeline produces more universal features (Pearson r $\geq$ 0.70 across seeds) than post-hoc alignment baselines on all three datasets. A minimal qualitative analysis confirms that high-universality features encode interpretable sociolinguistic patterns.

</details>


### [109] [When the Judge Changes, So Does the Measurement: Auditing LLM-as-Judge Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08535)
*Zongyou Yang,Yinghan Hou,Xiaokun Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示LLM-as-judge评分中"评委替换模糊性"问题：即使候选回答固定，更换评委模型也会导致评分变化。通过对比Qwen3系列（1.7B→32B）和MiniMax系列（M2→M2.7）的升级路径，发现评委升级不可互换，只有Qwen3 1.7B到4B带来稳健提升，且更大评委仍存在位置偏差和冗长度偏差。重复采样陪审团和结构化辩论等方法效果有限。作者呼吁建立标准化报告规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-judge评分的可复现性面临威胁：评分变化可能并非源于候选回答质量差异，而仅仅是因为评委模型被替换了。这种"评委替换模糊性"本质上是一个测量效度问题，意味着当前广泛使用的LLM评判范式缺乏跨模型的一致性保证。

Method: 在四个评判数据集上，系统比较两条实际可用的评委升级路径：(1) Qwen3稠密模型从1.7B扩展到32B参数；(2) MiniMax从M2到M2.7的API发布版本。分析维度包括：相邻版本升级的增益稳健性、位置偏差和冗长度偏差的变化、重复采样陪审团（repeated-sample juries）的附加价值、以及结构化辩论对决策的影响程度。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 评委升级不可互换——仅Qwen3 1.7B→4B提供稳健的相邻增益，MiniMax相邻版本间无此效果；(2) 更强的评委会减弱但无法消除位置偏差和冗长度偏差；(3) 当误差相关时，重复采样陪审团贡献甚微；(4) 结构化辩论可显著改变决策，但缺乏解析器和回退日志时无法将这些变化归因于真正的审议过程。

Conclusion: 作者主张LLM-as-judge报告应包含四个关键要素：数据集切片（dataset slices）、偏差探测（bias probes）、误差依赖性估计（error-dependence estimates）和协议审计轨迹（protocol audit trails），以提升评判结果的可信度和可复现性。

Abstract: An LLM-as-judge score can move even when the candidate responses stay fixed, simply because the evaluator has changed. We treat this evaluator-replacement ambiguity as a measurement-validity problem. Across four judgment datasets, we compare two upgrade paths available in practice: scaling Qwen3 dense judges from 1.7B to 32B parameters and moving across MiniMax M2-M2.7 released APIs. The main pattern is that judge upgrades are not interchangeable: only Qwen3 1.7B to 4B gives a robust adjacent gain, while MiniMax adjacent releases do not. Stronger judges reduce but do not remove position and verbosity bias. Repeated-sample juries add little when errors are correlated. Structured debate can move decisions substantially, but without parser and fallback logs those shifts cannot be attributed to deliberation. We argue that LLM-as-judge reports should include dataset slices, bias probes, error-dependence estimates, and protocol audit trails.

</details>


### [110] [It Takes a MAESTRO To Prune Bad Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08601)
*Palaash Goel,Ayush Maheshwari,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MAESTRO 是一种面向 MoE 语言模型的结构化剪枝框架，它将自回归专家激活轨迹建模为遍历马尔可夫链，利用稳态分布捕捉跨层依赖关系，从而得出全局感知的专家重要性启发式指标，在 50% 压缩率下性能保持率显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏激活的 MoE 模型虽然推理效率高，但所有专家仍需常驻内存，造成严重的部署瓶颈。现有结构化剪枝方法多针对稠密 Transformer 设计，使用局部启发式指标评估专家重要性，忽视了 MoE 路由中专家之间的跨层相互依赖关系，导致剪枝效果不佳。

Method: MAESTRO 框架将自回归生成过程中专家的激活轨迹建模为遍历马尔可夫链（Ergodic Markov chain），通过计算该链的稳态分布（stationary distribution）来编码跨层路由依赖，从而获得全局一致的专家重要性排序，并据此进行结构化剪枝。

Result: 在 Safety、Bias、Ethics 等五个不同领域的数据集上，MAESTRO 在严格的 50% 压缩率下，平均性能保持率比最先进的基线方法高出最多 10.61%，且跨任务方差显著更低。

Conclusion: 全局的、路由一致的剪枝策略能够产生跨异构任务泛化更一致的高质量模型，MAESTRO 为 MoE 模型的高效部署提供了一种有效的结构化压缩方案。

Abstract: Sparsely-activated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models achieve remarkable inference efficiency by activating only a small fraction of parameters per token, yet their full expert banks reside in memory at all times, creating a prohibitive deployment bottleneck. Existing structured pruning methods, largely designed for dense transformers, assess expert importance using locally derived heuristics that are blind to the interdependent nature of MoE routing. We introduce MAESTRO (Markov-chain Approximated Expert Sparsification via Transition-based ROuting), a structured pruning framework designed for MoE architectures that models autoregressive expert activation trajectories as Ergodic Markov chains whose stationary distributions encode cross-layer dependencies, yielding a globally aware importance heuristic. Evaluated across five diverse domains including Safety, Bias, and Ethics, MAESTRO outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by up to 10.61% in average performance retention under a strict 50% compression regime, while exhibiting substantially lower cross-task variance, indicating that global, routing-congruent pruning produces models that generalize more consistently across heterogeneous tasks.

</details>


### [111] [DominoTree: Conditional Tree-Structured Drafting with Domino for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08642)
*Saw S. Lin,Jyh-Shing Roger Jang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DominoTree 是一种训练无关的 best-first 树搜索推测解码方法，利用 Domino 的路径条件化、非因子化修正对候选树进行评分，结合 GPU 原生的 CUDA-graph 构建器，在多个基准和温度下取得了领先的接受长度和吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有块扩散推测解码器（如 DFlash）仅建模逐位置边缘分布，而 best-first 树方法（如 DDTree）基于因子化形式无法表达路径依赖的分布。Domino 的 GRU 因果修正引入了路径条件化，但 DDTree 无法利用这一结构。因此需要一种能利用 Domino 条件化、非因子化修正的树搜索方法。

Method: 提出 DominoTree：训练无关的 best-first 草稿树方法，沿每条根到节点的路径使用 Domino 的条件化、非因子化修正进行评分。通过将每节点修正限制在候选 top-M 内实现实用性。同时开发了 GPU 原生 CUDA-graph 树构建器，与 Python 参考实现位一致，保持接受结果不变且构建开销低。

Result: 在 Qwen3-4B 八个基准上，DominoTree 相比自回归解码实现最高 6.6 倍加速，平均接受长度达每轮 10.7 tokens，在所有测试温度下均为最高。吞吐量在所有温度下优于 Domino（整体 9-10%，Alpaca 上达 +22%），且优于 DDTree/CaDDTree。在 Qwen3-8B 上，T=0 时吞吐量领先 DDTree 24%，高温下略有优势或持平。

Conclusion: DominoTree 成功将 Domino 的条件化修正融入 best-first 树搜索，实现了训练无关的高效推测解码，在多项指标上达到最优，且通过 CUDA-graph 构建器保证了实用性和一致性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting several tokens and verifying them in parallel. Block-diffusion drafters such as DFlash produce
  a draft block in one pass but model only per-position marginals; best-first tree methods such as DDTree expand candidate trees from those marginals. The
  released Domino drafter adds a GRU-based causal correction that makes each draft token's distribution path-dependent, a structure DDTree's factorized
  formulation cannot represent. We introduce DominoTree, a training-free best-first draft tree scored by Domino's conditional, non-factorized correction
  along each root-to-node path, made practical by restricting the per-node correction to a candidate top-M. On Qwen3-4B across eight benchmarks, DominoTree
  reaches up to 6.6x speedup over autoregressive decoding and the highest mean accept length of any evaluated method, up to 10.7 tokens per round, at every
  temperature we test. DominoTree constructs its tree with a GPU-native, CUDA-graph builder that is bit-identical to a reference Python implementation, so
  acceptance is unchanged, while keeping per-round tree construction cheap. With this builder as default, DominoTree wins throughput over the released
  Domino decoder at every temperature, 9-10% overall on Qwen3-4B and up to +22% on Alpaca, and over DDTree/CaDDTree at every temperature we test. On Qwen3-
  8B, DominoTree keeps the highest accepted length at every temperature and adds a decisive throughput win at T=0, +24% over DDTree; at higher temperature
  that edge over DDTree/CaDDTree narrows to a tie and a small loss, while its Overall aggregate wins over DFlash and Domino persist.

</details>


### [112] [UltraX: Refining Pre-Training Data at Scale with Adaptive Programmatic Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08646)
*Xinlong Zhao,Dongsheng Liu,Hengyu Zhao,Zixuan Fu,Zheng Wang,Jie Cai,Jie Zhou,Qiang Ma,Xuanhe Zhou,Xu Han,Yudong Wang,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UltraX 是一个面向大规模预训练数据的函数调用式精炼框架，通过引入插入操作扩展编辑函数空间（增删改），结合程序监督生成流水线和推理执行阶段的规范化机制，实现了细粒度实例级数据编辑，在各类语料上以更少的训练 token 达到最优或匹配基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着训练数据接近物理极限，Scaling Laws 的收益递减，提升 LLM 更依赖高质量数据利用而非数据扩张。现有精炼方法存在瓶颈：基于规则的方法受限于固定启发式，无法处理实例级差异；基于 LLM 的方法虽提升质量，但无法满足大规模数据处理对效率和可靠性的要求。

Method: 提出 UltraX 框架，核心包括：(1) 在删除和修改之外引入插入操作，扩展编辑函数空间，实现细粒度实例级编辑；(2) 构建程序监督生成流水线：先通过数据集自适应提示优化引导专家 LLM 生成高质量端到端精炼文本，再通过行对齐映射和动态上下文替换将原文-精炼文对转换为结构化程序监督；(3) 通过低置信度样本过滤和按操作组合的比例控制采样来提升监督质量、稳定训练分布；(4) 推理执行阶段通过滑动窗口预测、全局操作聚合和系统化后处理实现输出规范化与验证，提升大规模执行的稳定性和可靠性。

Result: 实验表明，UltraX 在所有语料上取得最高平均性能，并且在更少的训练 token 下即可匹配或超越基线方法，展现出更强的数据效率和精炼可靠性。

Conclusion: UltraX 通过函数调用式精炼框架有效解决了大规模预训练数据精炼中质量、效率和可靠性的三重挑战，为数据高效利用提供了新范式。

Abstract: As available training data approaches its physical limit, gains from Scaling Laws have begun to diminish. Consequently, improving Large Language Models (LLMs) now depends less on data expansion and more on higher-quality data utilization. However, in the context of large-scale corpora, existing refinement methodologies face significant limitations in quality, efficiency, and reliability: Rule-based approaches are constrained by fixed heuristics and struggle with instance-level variations; LLM-based approaches improve quality but fail to meet the efficiency and reliability requirements of large-scale data processing. To address these challenges, we propose UltraX, a function-calling refinement framework for large-scale pre-training data that completes the editing function space by introducing insertion in addition to deletion and modification, enabling fine-grained instance-level editing. Specifically, UltraX builds a reliable program-supervision generation pipeline. In this pipeline, dataset-adaptive prompt optimization first guides an expert LLM to produce high-quality end-to-end refined texts, and Line Alignment Mapping and Dynamic Context Replacement then convert original-refined text pairs into structured program supervision. Meanwhile, UltraX improves supervision quality and stabilizes the training distribution with low-confidence example filtering and ratio-controlled sampling by operation combination. During inference and execution, it normalizes and validates model outputs through sliding-window prediction, global operation aggregation, and systematic post-processing, improving the stability and reliability of large-scale execution. Experiments show that UltraX achieves the highest average performance across all corpora and also matches or surpasses baselines with fewer training tokens, demonstrating stronger data efficiency and refinement reliability.

</details>


### [113] [WebSwarm: Recursive Multi-Agent Orchestration for Deep-and-Wide Web Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08662)
*Xiaoshuai Song,Liancheng Zhang,Kangzhi Zhao,Yutao Zhu,Zhongyuan Wang,Guanting Dong,Jinghan Yang,Han Li,Kun Gai,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WebSwarm 是一种渐进式递归委托框架，通过动态实例化搜索节点、支持递归分解与协作，在深度、广度及深度广度交织的搜索任务上均优于现有的单智能体和多智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ReAct 式单智能体受限于单一长轨迹和有限上下文，难以同时兼顾搜索深度与覆盖广度；现有多智能体系统虽通过并行执行提升覆盖，但在递归深度、协作自适应性和证据驱动的扩展方面仍存在不足。

Method: 提出 WebSwarm 框架：(1) 渐进式递归委托机制，推理过程中动态构建任务分解、递归扩展与智能体协作；(2) 动态实例化搜索节点，每个节点将局部目标与搜索模式耦合，决定搜索和协作的组织方式；(3) 节点可选择自行求解或进一步委托子节点，完成后向上返回证据与结果，供父节点扩展、修正或聚合；(4) 先探测 web 上任务相关信息的组织方式以指导后续节点扩展，并在同质兄弟节点间复用过程级经验。

Result: 在 BrowseComp-Plus、WideSearch、DeepWideSearch 和 GISA 四个基准上，WebSwarm 在深度、广度及深度广度交织任务中均一致优于单智能体和多智能体基线。

Conclusion: WebSwarm 通过递归委托与证据驱动的协同机制有效解决了复杂搜索任务中的深度-广度权衡问题，消融实验、任务难度分析、工具效率和模型泛化分析进一步验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based web search agents are transforming information seeking from simple factoid question answering into complex, deep-and-wide search and research-oriented tasks. A single ReAct-style agent is constrained by one long trajectory and limited context, making it difficult to handle depth and coverage simultaneously. Existing multi-agent systems improve search coverage through parallel execution and aggregation, but still exhibit clear limitations in recursive depth, collaboration adaptability, and evidence-grounded expansion. We propose WebSwarm, a progressive recursive delegation framework that jointly constructs task decomposition, recursive expansion, and agent collaboration during inference. WebSwarm dynamically instantiates agentic search nodes, each coupling a local objective with a search mode that specifies how the node should organize search and collaboration. Each node can either solve its objective itself or further delegate child nodes; after solving, it returns evidence and results upward, enabling parent nodes to further expand, revise, or aggregate the search process. To guide this process, WebSwarm first probes how task-relevant information is organized on the web to ground subsequent node expansion, and reuses process-level experience across homogeneous sibling nodes. Experiments on BrowseComp-Plus, WideSearch, DeepWideSearch, and GISA show that WebSwarm consistently outperforms single-agent and multi-agent baselines on deep, wide, and interleaved deep-and-wide tasks. Further analyses of ablation, task difficulty, web tool efficiency, and model generalization explain WebSwarm's effectiveness and provide insights for multi-agent search systems.

</details>


### [114] [Do You Need a Frontier Model as a Citation Verifier? Benchmarking Rubric LLMs for Deep-Research Source Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08700)
*Ethan Leung,Elias Lumer,Corey Feld,Austin Huber,Vamse Kumar Subbiah,Kevin Paul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究评估了8种LLM裁判在引文质量评分中的校准能力，发现较便宜的模型（如GPT-5-mini）在源相关性上F1达0.908，而在事实支持维度上各模型统计无显著差异。关键发现是：标量F1掩盖了方向性偏差（如通过率漂移、假阳性/假阴性率），而这些偏差正是强化学习会强化的，因此在使用引文评分作为奖励信号前必须校准裁判模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习训练中越来越多地使用LLM作为奖励模型来评分各项标准，但在此之前需要明确LLM裁判的能力下限和偏差程度。现有评估仅关注标量指标（如F1），忽视了方向性偏差对下游强化学习训练的关键影响。

Method: 构建了一个对抗性长文本基准，包含1,248条评分决策（全部经人工复核，其中378条为裁判分歧的困难案例）。在引文质量的结构化评分任务中，沿两个维度（源相关性和事实支持）对来自3个模型家族的8种现成LLM裁判进行评分，并与黄金标签对比，分析F1、Cohen's κ、通过率漂移、假阳性率和假阴性率等指标。

Result: GPT-5-mini在源相关性上取得最强F1（0.908，κ=0.636）；在事实支持维度上各裁判模型置信区间重叠，统计上无显著差异。在相近F1下，各裁判在通过率漂移、假阳性率和假阴性率上差异显著。标量F1掩盖了方向性偏差，而这正是强化学习训练中会被强化的内容。

Conclusion: 将引文评分标准用作奖励信号前，校准裁判模型是先决条件，且该校准不需要使用最昂贵的模型。评估裁判模型时应超越单一标量指标，纳入方向性偏差等维度。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning increasingly relies on an LLM judge to score each rubric criterion, and that judge acts as the reward model during training. Before such a signal can be trusted, we need to know how capable the judge must be and how biased it is. We study this calibration question for citation quality in deep-research systems, where a search-grounded LLM must support each claim it writes with a cited source. Citation quality is a structured rubric task in which each attribution-citation pair is judged along two dimensions that require an LLM, source relevance and factual support. On an adversarial long-form benchmark, we score 8 off-the-shelf LLM judges from 3 model families against gold labels over 1,248 rubric decisions, all of which were human-reviewed and 378 of which were hard cases adjudicated from judge disagreements. Cheaper judges remain competitive across both dimensions, with GPT-5-mini attaining the strongest source-relevance pass-class F1 at 0.908 ($κ$=0.636), while on factual support the judges are statistically indistinguishable (overlapping confidence intervals), so no single model dominates. At comparable F1, the judges still differ substantially in pass-rate drift, false positive rate, and false negative rate. Scalar F1 obscures this directional bias, yet it is exactly what a downstream reinforcement learning loop would reinforce. Calibrating the judge is therefore a prerequisite for using citation rubrics as reward signals, and our results show that this calibration does not require the most expensive available model.

</details>


### [115] [Validity of LLMs as data annotators: AMALIA on authority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08731)
*Manuel Pita*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究以葡萄牙国家级语言模型AMALIA为案例，通过"恢复差距"测试揭示：模型在道德基础编码任务上与人类标注者的一致性可能源于表面特征关联（如权威人物附近的道德愤怒），而非真正理解理论构念。当将整体提示分解为原子条款并按理论规则重组时，AMALIA性能大幅下降，表明一致性强不等同于构念效度高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对语言模型性能的评估普遍依赖"与人类标注者的一致性"这一信度指标，但信度不等于效度。对于必须通过推理而非表面特征提取的理论构念，模型可能通过相关捷径而非真正遵循构念理论来达成正确编码。研究旨在揭示这一隐患，并为主权语言模型的评测体系提供方法论补正。

Method: 提出"恢复差距"（recovery gap）作为诊断工具：将整体性编码提示分解为编码手册中的原子条款，再按理论显式规则重新组合，测量该分解-重组过程导致的性能损失。若校准能弥合差距，则表明一定跨模型、跨语言的可迁移性；若不能，则构念-模型工具本身是失效位点。以AMALIA-9B模型为对象，在权威道德基础上进行英语校准工具向葡萄牙语的迁移测试，并对比开放多语言大模型在同语料上的表现。

Result: 分解后仅恢复AMALIA整体性能的约一半；错误分析显示模型依赖表面关联，尤其是权威人物附近的道德愤怒信号。而开放多语言LLM在相同葡萄牙语语料和指令下可弥合差距，排除了语料本身作为主要解释因素。校准后的英语工具未能成功迁移至AMALIA及欧洲葡萄牙语场景。

Conclusion: AMALIA可用于大规模筛选和预编码，但尚不足以独立测量该道德构念。研究作为单一反例，并非对国家模型的否定，而是主张：主权LLM基准评测体系不仅应测试与人类标注者的一致性，还应检验达成该一致性的证据路径是否有效。

Abstract: A national language model offers a linguistic community its own instrument for measuring what its citizens say and value. Portugal's AMALIA, a publicly funded 9B-parameter model for European Portuguese, appears competitive on agreement alone: asked to code the moral foundation of authority, it agrees with trained human coders to within six F1 points of open models eight to thirteen times its size. Yet agreement is reliability, not validity. For theoretical constructs that must be inferred rather than read from surface features, the question is whether the model follows the construct's theory or reaches the right code by correlated shortcuts. We test this with the recovery gap: the loss in performance when a holistic prompt is decomposed into the codebook's atomic clauses and recombined by the theory's explicit rule. If calibration closes that gap, some portability should survive across models and languages; where it does not, the construct-model instrument is the likely locus of failure. We ask whether a calibrated English instrument transfers to AMALIA-9B and to European Portuguese. For one construct and one corpus, it does not. Decomposition recovers only about half of AMALIA's holistic performance, and error analysis suggests reliance on surface correlates, especially moral outrage near authority figures. An open multilingual LLM closes the gap on the same Portuguese corpus under the same instructions, pointing away from the corpus as the main explanation. AMALIA can still screen and pre-code at scale, but it cannot yet measure this construct well enough to stand alone. The study is a single counterexample, not a verdict on national models; it argues that sovereign-LLM benchmark batteries should test not only agreement with human coders, but the evidential route by which that agreement is warranted.

</details>


### [116] [UniClawBench: A Universal Benchmark for Proactive Agents on Real-World Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08768)
*Zhekai Chen,Chengqi Duan,Kaiyue Sun,Bohao Li,Yuqing Wang,Manyuan Zhang,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniClawBench 是一个基于能力驱动的基准测试，包含 400 个双语真实世界任务，围绕五项基础模型能力设计，在动态 Docker 容器中以闭环多轮评估范式评测主动式智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试存在三个核心缺陷：(1) 依赖沙盒环境和单轮评估范式，无法体现代理在真实动态环境中的表现；(2) 基于场景的任务分类法将多种模型能力混杂在同一类别中，难以定位代理失败的根因；(3) 静态预录答案无法适应开放式交互。因此需要一个能力驱动、真实环境、精细评估的基准来解耦分析模型能力与框架设计对代理性能的影响。

Method: UniClawBench 围绕五项基础能力（Skill Usage、Exploration、Long-Context Reasoning、Multimodal Understanding、Cross-Platform Coordination）设计了 400 个中英双语真实世界任务。评估在实时 Docker 容器中进行，采用细粒度的逐步完成检查点。同时设计了闭环评估策略，由执行代理、隐藏监督代理和用户代理组成，模拟真实的多轮人类反馈而不泄露评分标准。为解耦基础模型能力与框架设计，在多个代理框架下评估了当前最先进模型。

Result: 通过跨模型和跨框架的全面对比，揭示了基础模型能力与代理框架设计如何共同塑造在真实环境中的性能表现。具体数据见原文。

Conclusion: UniClawBench 作为首个能力驱动的主动式代理基准，能够有效诊断代理失败的能力根源，并为模型与框架的联合优化提供指导。公开代码与基准数据以促进后续研究。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models and multimodal large language models has accelerated the emergence of proactive agents capable of operating everyday tools and assisting users in real-world environments. However, existing benchmarks struggle to evaluate such agents effectively, as they often rely on sandboxed environments and single-turn evaluation paradigms. Moreover, their scenario-based task taxonomies mix multiple model capabilities within the same task category, making it difficult to identify the root causes of agent failures. To address these limitations, we introduce UniClawBench, the first capability-driven benchmark designed to evaluate proactive agents in dynamic, real-world settings. UniClawBench is built around five foundational model capabilities: Skill Usage, Exploration, Long-Context Reasoning, Multimodal Understanding, and Cross-Platform Coordination. Based on these capabilities, we design 400 bilingual real-world tasks. Unlike previous benchmarks that rely on static, pre-recorded answers, our benchmark evaluates agents in live Docker containers using fine-grained, step-by-step completion checkpoints. Furthermore, we design a closed-loop evaluation strategy comprising an executor agent, a hidden supervisor agent, and a user agent to simulate realistic multi-turn human feedback without leaking grading criteria. To disentangle base model capabilities from framework-level design choices, we evaluate state-of-the-art models under multiple agent frameworks. Through comprehensive comparisons across both models and frameworks, we show how base model capabilities and agent framework designs jointly shape performance in real-world environments. To facilitate future research, we make our benchmark and code publicly available at https://github.com/HKU-MMLab/UniClawBench.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Mixtures of spatial factor analyzers for tensor-variate data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07887)
*Hanzhang Lu,Keiran Malott,Kirsty Milligan,Sanjeena Subedi,Edana Cassol,Vinita Chauhan,Connor McNairn,Prarthana Pasricha,Sangeeta Murugkar,Rowan Thomson,Andrew Jirasek,Jeffrey L. Andrews*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合空间因子分析器(MSFA)，利用样条基空间衰减协方差结构和矩阵变量因子分析器来解决高维空间数据的聚类问题，并通过EM算法与广义最小二乘法进行参数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维空间数据聚类面临维度灾难，传统协方差结构随维度增加导致参数膨胀；同时需要有效建模空间依赖与非空间依赖，以准确推断和区分不同的空间模式。

Method: 提出混合空间因子分析器(MSFA)框架：(1) 利用坐标信息，构建基于样条函数的灵活空间衰减协方差结构，防止高维下参数膨胀；(2) 采用矩阵变量因子分析器进行非空间依赖的进一步降维；(3) 使用EM算法的变体结合广义最小二乘估计器进行参数估计；(4) 将模型拓展至张量变量数据分析场景。

Result: 在模拟研究和拉曼光谱、高光谱纹理数据库的实际应用中，所提模型展示了准确推断和区分不同空间模式的能力。

Conclusion: MSFA通过将空间衰减协方差与因子分析器结合，有效解决了高维空间数据聚类中的参数膨胀问题，在张量变量数据分析中具有良好应用前景。

Abstract: A mixture of spatial factor analyzers (MSFA) is introduced to address the challenges of clustering high-dimensional spatial data. By leveraging the underlying coordinate system, the proposed framework incorporates a flexible, spline-based spatial decay covariance structure that prevents parameter inflation as dimensionality increases. To model non-spatial dependence, matrix variate factor analyzers are employed for further dimensionality reduction. Parameter estimation is conducted via a variant of the expectation-maximization algorithm combined with a generalized least squares estimator. The proposed models are explored in the context of tensor-variate data analysis, where simulation studies and applications to Raman spectroscopy and hyperspectral texture databases demonstrate their capacity to accurately infer and differentiate distinct spatial patterns.

</details>


### [118] [Beta Regression with Autoregressive Errors for Interrupted Time Series Analysis of Proportion and Rate Outcomes: A Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07914)
*Ariel Linden*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 该文介绍了betark——一种Stata命令，用于实现带自回归误差的Beta回归联合条件最大似然估计。蒙特卡洛研究表明，相比准二项GLM+HAC标准误，betark在多数场景（尤其高持久性自相关下）提供更好校准的推断，但高度持久AR(3)过程下自身一类错误仍偏高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中断时间序列分析（ITSA）中比例和率结局常用OLS回归，忽略了结局的有界性。现有适用有界结局的方法（准似然GLM+HAC标准误）无法联合建模Beta分布条件密度与自回归误差结构，需要一个能同时估计均值、精度和AR系数的统一框架。

Method: 提出betark——基于递归替换的联合条件最大似然估计器，在单一似然函数中联合估计Beta回归的均值、精度及任意阶AR(k)系数，产生闭合形式的条件Beta似然。蒙特卡洛模拟比较betark与使用Newey-West HAC标准误的准二项GLM，涵盖AR(1)-AR(3)过程、三种序列长度、四种效应量，设计为单组ITSA。

Result: 两种方法均基本无偏。betark在多数场景下比GLM+HAC提供更好校准的推断，在高持久性自相关下优势最大（短序列时GLM+HAC一类错误超60%）。AR阶数错设1阶、改变起始均值和干预前趋势对性能影响不大。但betark在高度持久AR(3)过程下即使最长序列其一类错误仍偏高。

Conclusion: betark为有界结局的ITSA提供了一种优于现有GLM+HAC方法的联合建模方案，尤其适用于中低阶自相关场景，但在高阶高度持久自相关下仍需谨慎使用。

Abstract: Interrupted time series analyses (ITSA) of proportion and rate outcomes are frequently estimated using ordinary least squares regression despite the bounded nature of these outcomes. When methods appropriate for bounded outcomes are used, the standard approach is a quasi-likelihood generalized linear model (GLM) with heteroskedasticity- and autocorrelation-consistent (HAC) standard errors. However, no existing estimator jointly models the beta-distributed conditional density and autoregressive (AR) error structure. We introduce betark, a Stata implementation of a joint conditional maximum likelihood estimator for beta regression with AR(k) errors based on a recursive substitution that yields a closed-form conditional beta likelihood with autoregressive dependence of arbitrary order. Unlike two-stage approaches, betark jointly estimates the mean, precision, and AR(k) coefficients in a single likelihood, so reported standard errors directly account for autocorrelation without separate correction. A Monte Carlo study compared betark with a quasi-binomial GLM using Newey-West HAC standard errors across AR(1)-AR(3) processes, three series lengths, and four effect sizes in a single-group ITSA design. Both methods were essentially unbiased, but betark produced better-calibrated inference than GLM+HAC in most scenarios, with the largest gains under highly persistent autocorrelation, where GLM+HAC Type I error exceeded 60% for short series. Misspecifying the AR order by one lag and varying the starting mean and pre-intervention trend had only modest effects on performance. However, betark's own Type I error remained elevated under highly persistent AR(3) processes even for the longest series examined.

</details>


### [119] [Size independence of consistency index for pairwise comparison matrices in analytic hierarchy process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07950)
*Tsuneshi Obata,Shunsuke Shiraishi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究了层次分析法(AHP)中成对比较矩阵的一致性评估问题，重点探讨了一致性指标与矩阵规模之间的关系。作者分析了Saaty提出的CI/CR指标固定阈值0.1在不同矩阵规模下是否等价的问题，提出了一个与规模无关的一致性指标应满足的基本性质，并对先前提出的基于特征多项式系数的指标进行了改进，证明其与现有指标一致，最后通过随机生成的矩阵可视化展示了矩阵规模对一致性评估的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Saaty提出的CI和CR一致性指标使用固定阈值0.1作为可接受一致性水平的准则，但该阈值是否在不同矩阵规模（即不同评价项数量）下代表等价的一致性水平尚不清楚。这一问题在AHP领域一直是开放且热门的研究话题，需要深入分析一致性与矩阵规模之间的关系。

Method: 作者通过分析由评价项子集构建的成对比较矩阵，来考察一致性与矩阵规模之间的关系。基于此分析，提出规模无关一致性指标应满足的基本性质。随后对先前提出的基于特征多项式系数的一致性指标进行改进，使其满足该性质，并证明其与已有一致性指标的一致性。最后通过随机生成成对比较矩阵进行可视化，揭示矩阵规模对一致性评估的影响。

Result: (1) 提出了一个规模无关一致性指标应满足的基本性质；(2) 改进后的指标满足该性质，并与现有一致性指标一致；(3) 通过随机矩阵可视化，明确了矩阵规模对一致性指标值的影响规律。

Conclusion: Saaty的0.1固定阈值在不同矩阵规模下并不代表等价的一致性水平；需要一个规模无关的一致性指标来更合理地评估成对比较矩阵的一致性。本文提出的基本性质为该类指标的设计提供了理论依据，改进后的指标能够消除矩阵规模对一致性评估的干扰。

Abstract: Pairwise comparisons are fundamental in the analytic hierarchy process. Various consistency indices have been proposed to assess inconsistencies in these comparisons. Since Saaty first proposed his consistency index, the assessment of the degree of consistency in pairwise comparison matrices has remained an open and hot topic in the study of the analytic hierarchy process. The consistency indices CI and CR proposed by Saaty are defined using the principal eigenvalue of the pairwise comparison matrix. In our previous study, we introduced an alternative index derived from the relationship between the coefficient of the characteristic polynomial and the consistency of comparisons.
  Saaty proposed a fixed threshold of 0.1 for CI or CR as a guideline for an acceptable level of consistency, regardless of the matrix size. However, whether this threshold represents an equivalent level of consistency across different matrix sizes, that is, across different numbers of evaluation items, remains unclear. This study analysed the relationship between consistency and matrix size by examining pairwise comparison matrices constructed from subsets of evaluation items. Based on this analysis, we propose the fundamental property to be satisfied by a size-independent consistency index.
  Furthermore, we refine our previously proposed index to ensure that it satisfies this property, demonstrating that it coincides with the existing consistency index. Finally, we visualise the relationship between the matrix size and consistency index values using randomly generated pairwise comparison matrices, thereby providing insights into the impact of matrix size on consistency evaluation.

</details>


### [120] [Evaluation of Combination Therapy amid Patient-Level Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08058)
*Lingjie Wei,Xinzhou Guo,Shuoxun Xu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的无模型统计推断框架，用于在调整患者对单一疗法反应的异质性后评估联合疗法，解决了异质性调整中的不可识别性和非线性问题，并通过ACTG 175试验的再分析验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合疗法的获益传统上被归因于药物间的药理学相互作用，但近期实验发现其可能主要由患者对单一疗法的个体反应差异驱动。经典统计推断框架未考虑这种患者层面的异质性，可能给出不充分且过于乐观的评估结果。

Method: 作者提出了一个无模型的统计推断框架，核心包括：(1) 建立跨世界目标参数（cross-world target parameter）的部分可识别性条件，以解决异质性调整中的非线性与不可识别性问题；(2) 开发基于结局的最优匹配方案（outcome-based optimal matching），实现渐近正态性，并构建√N速率的置信区间。

Result: 所提框架能够对患者层面的异质性进行可靠、高效且透明的调整，从而更准确地评估联合治疗效果。通过对ACTG 175试验的再分析，验证了该方法的实际优势。

Conclusion: 在评估联合疗法时，必须考虑患者对单一疗法的异质性反应，否则可能产生误导性结论。所提出的模型无关推断框架为此提供了一种可靠、高效且透明的解决方案。

Abstract: Combination therapy, a treatment approach that involves two or more monotherapies, is widely considered to enhance therapeutic efficacy across different medical conditions. It was often believed that combination therapy is beneficial because of pharmacological interactions between its component monotherapies. However, through laboratory experiments, pharmacologists have recently noted that the benefits of some combination therapies might be largely driven by varying patient-level responses to their component monotherapies. Without accounting for such patient-level heterogeneity, classical statistical inference frameworks for combination therapy might be inadequate and overly optimistic. In this paper, we introduce a novel and model-free statistical inference framework to complement the classical one and evaluate combination therapy after adjusting for patient-level heterogeneity in responses to monotherapies. We address the non-identifiability and nonlinearity issue inherent in adjustment of patient-level heterogeneity and establish conditions for the (partial) identifiability of the cross-world target parameter. We develop an outcome-based optimal matching scheme to achieve asymptotic normality and construct $\sqrt{N}$-rate confidence intervals for the target parameter, thereby enabling reliable, efficient and transparent evaluation of combination therapy amid patient-level heterogeneity. The benefits of the proposed framework are demonstrated through a reanalysis of the ACTG 175 trial.

</details>


### [121] [Bias-Corrected Multiplier Bootstrap Inference for Spectral Edges of Large Covariance Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08089)
*Xiucai Ding,Yichen Hu,Jiahui Xie*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对高维协方差矩阵谱边缘推断中Tracy-Widom波动带来的中心化和标度困难，本文提出一种偏差校正的乘数bootstrap方法，通过精心校准的乘数扰动将边缘波动正则化到高斯近似可行的更大尺度，从而构造出长度仅略大于Tracy-Widom尺度的置信区间，并证明该区间可在局部尺度大于n^{-1/6}的备择假设下实现覆盖率为零，进而提供一种无阈值的尖峰数量估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维协方差矩阵最大非尖峰样本特征值作为谱边缘的自然估计量，其波动服从Tracy-Widom分布。在未知总体协方差结构下，进行有效推断需要精确的中心化（确定性谱边缘）和标度常数，而这两者在实践中往往难以估计。因此需要一种绕过这些估计困难的新方法。

Method: 提出偏差校正的乘数bootstrap方法：引入精心校准的乘数扰动，将边缘波动正则化到稍大的尺度，使条件高斯近似成为可能；利用bootstrap特征值直接构造置信区间，并通过数据驱动的再中心化步骤校正bootstrap引起的确定性谱边缘偏移。

Result: 理论证明：(1)偏差校正和重标度后，最大的若干非尖峰bootstrap特征值在给定数据下渐近服从高斯分布；(2)所提置信区间具有渐近有效性，长度仅略大于Tracy-Widom尺度；(3)在尖峰以大于n^{-1/6}的局部尺度与主体分离的备择假设下，覆盖率趋于零；(4)该置信区间可转化为无阈值的尖峰数量估计器，即数据驱动的碎石图截断准则。

Conclusion: 该方法在不要求尖峰互异或很大的前提下，为高维协方差矩阵谱边缘推断提供了一种实用且理论严密的解决方案，同时给出了尖峰数量和碎石图截断的数据驱动判据，具有重要的理论和应用价值。

Abstract: Inference for spectral edges of large covariance matrices is a fundamental problem in high-dimensional statistics. A major difficulty is that the largest non-spiked sample eigenvalues, which serve as natural estimators of the edge, fluctuate on the Tracy--Widom scale. Consequently, valid inference requires accurate centering by the deterministic spectral edge together with a precise scaling constant, both of which are often difficult to estimate in practice under general unknown population covariance structures. In this paper, we propose a bias-corrected multiplier bootstrap procedure for inference on the deterministic edge of the bulk spectrum. The key idea is to introduce a carefully calibrated multiplier perturbation that regularizes the edge fluctuation to a slightly larger scale at which Gaussian approximation becomes tractable. The resulting confidence interval is constructed directly from bootstrap eigenvalues, together with a data-driven recentering step that corrects the bootstrap-induced shift of the deterministic edge. On the theoretical side, we show that, after bias correction and rescaling, the largest few non-spiked bootstrap eigenvalues are asymptotically Gaussian conditionally on the data. Building on this result, we establish the asymptotic validity of the proposed confidence interval, whose length is only slightly larger than the Tracy--Widom scale, and prove vanishing coverage under alternatives in which additional spikes separate from the bulk at a local scale larger than $n^{-1/6}$. As a consequence, the same confidence interval yields a threshold-free estimator for the number of spikes, without requiring the spikes to be distinct or very large. Equivalently, the procedure yields a data-driven and theoretically justified cutoff for the scree plot.

</details>


### [122] [Joint estimation of high-dimensional spiked covariance matrices via a partially shared subspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08123)
*Changwon Yoon,Minwoo Kim,Sungkyu Jung,Jeongyoun Ahn*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种尖峰协方差模型，用于两个共享部分协方差结构的高维数据集，支持联合估计共享子空间及其秩，并给出闭式池化权重和渐近保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据分析常受限于小样本，但来自相关来源的辅助数据集容易获得。当两个数据集共享部分协方差结构时，利用共享部分可显著改善估计，但现有模型未能充分捕捉这种部分共享。

Method: 提出尖峰协方差模型，两数据集共享一个未知秩和任意谱位置的子空间，各自保留独有的尖峰方向。开发完整估计流程：联合估计共享子空间及其秩、推导闭式池化权重用于合并两数据集、基于随机矩阵理论在比例增长机制下给出渐近保证。

Result: 严格推广了现有共享协方差结构模型；填补了对比降维在高维场景下的估计器空白；在新冠疫情早期投资组合构建和脑肿瘤基因表达对比分析中验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架对称处理两数据集，通过部分共享协方差结构的显式建模，在高维小样本场景下实现了更优的协方差估计，具有理论和应用双重贡献。

Abstract: Statistical analysis of high-dimensional data is often hampered by limited sample sizes, yet auxiliary datasets from related sources are often readily available. When two such datasets share part of their covariance structure, but not all of it, exploiting the shared part can substantially improve estimation. We propose a spiked covariance model that explicitly captures this partial sharing: two datasets share a subspace of unknown rank and arbitrary position in the spectrum, while each retains its own distinct spiked directions. The model treats the two datasets symmetrically and strictly generalizes existing models for shared covariance structure. We develop a complete estimation procedure that includes joint estimation of the shared subspace and its rank, a closed-form pooling weight for combining the two datasets, and asymptotic guarantees derived from random matrix theory in the proportional-growth regime. The framework also resolves a gap in contrastive dimension reduction by providing a principled estimator for high-dimensional settings. We illustrate the methodology on portfolio construction during the early COVID-19 pandemic and on contrastive analysis of brain tumor gene expression.

</details>


### [123] [Bayesian Donor Set Selection in Synthetic Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08142)
*Seul Lee,Johan Lim,Joungyoun Kim,Xinlei Wang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种贝叶斯层次模型，在合成控制法中同时进行供体集合选择与权重估计。通过分层Gamma-Bernoulli先验结构，模型能够将无关供体的权重精确收缩至零，从而自动筛选活跃供体，并在简化模型下证明了后验收敛性。模拟与应用表明该方法在供体池含噪声时优于传统SCM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统合成控制法(SCM)的效果高度依赖供体集合的恰当选择，但现有方法多假设供体集合固定已知，缺乏在估计合成权重的同时自动筛选活跃供体的机制。当供体池中包含不相关或弱相关单元时，固定供体集的SCM估计精度会显著下降。

Method: 构建贝叶斯层次模型，核心创新在于对供体权重采用分层Gamma-Bernoulli先验：引入二元潜变量指示供体是否活跃，活跃供体的权重服从截尾Dirichlet或Gamma分布，满足单纯形约束；通过后验推断同时完成供体筛选与权重估计，非活跃供体的权重可精确为零。文中还在简化干预前模型下建立了供体集合的后验收缩一致性理论结果。

Result: 数值模拟显示：当供体池包含无关单元时，所提模型在供体恢复与权重估计上均优于传统SCM；在全供体场景下仍具竞争力。对西德GDP轨迹的实证应用验证了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 将供体集合选择纳入贝叶斯合成控制框架，提供了一种更精简、更灵活的扩展方案。该方法填补了贝叶斯SCM在自动供体筛选方面的空白，兼具理论保证与实用价值。

Abstract: The Synthetic Control Method (SCM) is a widely used approach for assessing the effects of interventions by constructing a synthetic counterfactual using a donor set of untreated units. However, the effectiveness of SCM heavily relies on the careful selection of an appropriate donor set. In this paper, we propose a Bayesian hierarchical model that performs donor set selection while preserving the standard SCM simplex constraint on donor weights. Unlike approaches that assume a fixed donor set, our model allows for the simultaneous estimation of the synthetic control weights and the active donor set. By using a hierarchical Gamma-Bernoulli construction for the donor weights, the proposed model assigns posterior mass to simplex faces and allows exact zero weights for excluded donors. We establish a posterior donor-set consistency result under a simplified pre-intervention model. Through numerical simulations, we show that our model improves donor recovery and weight estimation when the donor pool contains irrelevant or weakly related units, while remaining competitive in full-donor settings. Finally, we apply our model to the GDP trajectory of West Germany, illustrating its practical applicability. Our findings suggest that incorporating donor set selection offers a more parsimonious and flexible extension of existing Bayesian synthetic control methods.

</details>


### [124] [Circular Expectiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08306)
*Bernhard Klar*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文引入圆形expectiles，作为基于弦距离的非对称圆形损失函数的极小化子，将线性expectile推广到方向数据，证明了存在唯一性、一致性及渐近正态性，并给出几何解释和潜在应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将线性expectile的概念推广到圆形（方向）数据领域，填补了方向数据统计分析中缺乏非对称损失函数对应的位置度量（如expectile）这一空白，同时保留圆形均值作为对称情形（α=1/2）的特例。

Method: 基于弦距离构造非对称圆形损失函数，以该损失函数的极小化子定义圆形expectile；分析该非凸优化问题的目标函数和识别函数；利用样本圆形均值作为参考方向以诱导圆上的线性序，定义经验圆形expectile；通过分布具有正密度的条件证明解的存在唯一性，并建立一致性和有限维渐近正态性。

Result: 证明了在圆上具有正密度的分布下，圆形expectile极小化子的存在性与唯一性；经验圆形expectile同样具有唯一性，且满足一致性与有限维渐近正态分布收敛。

Conclusion: 圆形expectile成功将线性expectile扩展到方向数据，具有良好统计性质，可用于圆形数据的散度度量、偏度分析及对称性诊断。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce circular expectiles as minimizers of an asymmetric circular loss function based on chord distance. In contrast to the linear expectile criterion, the resulting circular optimization problem is non-convex, so existence and uniqueness require a separate analysis. The construction extends linear expectiles to directional data while preserving the circular mean as the symmetric case corresponding to $α=1/2$. We derive basic representations of the objective function and the associated identification function, and give a geometric interpretation that generalizes the corresponding representation for the circular mean. Furthermore, we prove the existence and uniqueness of the minimizers for distributions with positive density on the circle. The empirical circular expectile is defined by using the sample circular mean as reference direction for the induced linear order on the circle. We prove the uniqueness of the empirical expectile, as well as its consistency and finite-dimensional asymptotic normality. Finally, we indicate possible applications to circular measures of dispersion, skewness, and symmetry diagnostics.

</details>


### [125] [A scalable version of MADD for big-data classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08334)
*Annesha Ghosh,Adrija Saha,Soham Sarkar*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出可扩展的MADD距离度量变体，通过代表集选择和随机傅里叶特征两种策略大幅降低计算复杂度，使其能应用于大规模高维数据，同时保持与原始MADD相似的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MADD在高维低样本量场景下能有效规避欧氏距离的距离集中、邻域结构破坏和hub节点等问题，但其计算复杂度随训练样本量二次增长，导致无法应用于大样本量的高维大数据集。

Method: 提出两类加速策略：(1) 在MADD计算中选取代表性子集代替全量样本，降低对样本量的二次依赖；(2) 结合随机傅里叶特征(Random Fourier Features)进一步加速，适用于超大规模样本场景。论文从理论和数值实验两方面验证了所提方法的有效性。

Result: 所提方法在分类性能上与原始MADD相当，但计算时间仅为其一小部分，从理论和实验两个层面均得到验证。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将MADD的应用范围扩展至具有大量观测样本的大数据场景，在保持分类优势的同时实现了显著的计算加速。

Abstract: Distance-based classifiers are very popular, and the Euclidean distance is one of the most commonly used metrics in distance-based classifiers. However, classifiers based on the Euclidean distance often suffer in high-dimensional setups due to issues such as distance concentration, violation of neighborhood structures, and the presence of hubs. In high-dimension, low-sample-size (HDLSS) situations, a data-driven semi-metric called the Mean Absolute Difference of Distances (MADD) is known to circumvent these issues. But one major problem with MADD is that its computational complexity increases quadratically with the training sample size. As a result, the application of MADD becomes computationally challenging for big datasets that have both a high dimension as well as a large number of observations. In this paper, we propose a scalable version of MADD that significantly reduces its computational complexity while retaining its advantages. This speed-up is achieved by selecting a representative set during the computation of MADD. Further speed-ups are achieved by using the idea of Random Fourier Features, particularly when the sample size is very large. We establish that our proposed methods achieve performances similar to MADD but only at a fraction of its computing time, both theoretically as well as numerically. Our approach broadens the scope of MADD, allowing its use to big-data with a very large number of observations.

</details>


### [126] [Testing Covariance Separability in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08388)
*Tomas Masak,Marcus Mayrhofer,Una Radojičić*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的高维矩阵变量数据可分性检验方法，通过将问题转化为白化后的球形检验，并利用蒙特卡洛模拟校准，同时引入角度版本以减少分布假设依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵变量数据的协方差可分性假设能极大简化协方差相关统计任务的解释与计算，但在高维情形下检验该假设非常困难，现有方法存在不足。

Method: 1. 使用可分离最大似然估计对数据进行白化，将可分性检验转化为球形检验；2. 通过蒙特卡洛模拟进行有限样本校准；3. 引入基于白化后径向标准化的角度版本检验，减少对分布假设的依赖。

Result: 检验在有限样本下实现水平控制；在密集备择假设下证明了检验的高维一致性；角度版本降低了对分布假设的依赖。

Conclusion: 所提出的检验方法具有实际效用、良好的经验功效和计算效率，为高维矩阵变量数据的可分性假设检验提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Separability is an important structural assumption often placed on the covariance when working with matrix-variate data, because it greatly simplifies both interpretation and computation of subsequent covariance-based statistical tasks. Yet testing the separability assumption is difficult in the high-dimensional regime. We propose to test separability by recasting the problem as a sphericity test after whitening the data using the separable maximum likelihood estimate of the covariance. The test is calibrated by Monte Carlo simulation, yielding finite-sample level control. Furthermore, we prove the test's high-dimensional consistency under dense alternatives. To reduce its reliance on distributional assumptions, we introduce an angular version of the test based on radial normalization after whitening. We demonstrate the practical utility, empirical power, and computational efficiency of the prop

</details>


### [127] [Bayesian DAG Structure Learning with Simultaneous Shrinkage Covariance Estimation under Scale-Mixture Error Distributions in the Proportional High-Dimensional Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08428)
*Samaneh Nazari,Mohammad Arashi,Abdolnasser Sadeghkhani*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出R-DACH框架，在高维比例渐近体制下联合进行DAG结构学习和精度矩阵估计，通过Cholesky因子上的horseshoe先验实现稀疏性，并利用逐观测逆伽马混合实现鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高维DAG学习方法对重尾误差和污染观测敏感，缺乏在比例渐近体制（p/n→c）下兼具结构学习、精度估计与鲁棒性的统一贝叶斯框架。

Method: 在修正Cholesky因子的严格下三角元素上直接施加全局-局部horseshoe型先验，诱导与拓扑排序一致的父集选择稀疏性；引入逐观测逆伽马尺度混合对抗重尾/污染误差；设计部分塌缩分块Gibbs采样器在排序、稀疏模式和连续参数的联合空间上遍历。

Result: 在p达数百的(n,p)配置仿真中验证了理论速率；在污染条件下相比graphical-horseshoe、DAG-Wishart和PC类方法有显著增益；在TCGA RNA-seq数据上恢复了生物学可解释的调控结构。

Conclusion: R-DACH框架在比例渐近体制下实现了DAG结构学习、精度矩阵估计与自动鲁棒性的统一，兼具理论保证和实用价值，在污染数据和真实基因表达数据上均表现优越。

Abstract: We propose a unified Bayesian framework namely robust DAG-Cholesky horseshoe (R-DACH) for joint directed acyclic graph (DAG) structure learning and precision matrix estimation in the high-dimensional proportional asymptotic regime $p/n \to c \in (0,\infty)$, under the scale mixture of normal errors. The construction places a global-local horseshoe-type prior directly on the strictly lower-triangular entries of the modified Cholesky factor of the DAG-Markov precision matrix, so that sparsity in the Cholesky parameters induces a coherent parent-set selection consistent with a topological ordering of the variables. A per-observation inverse-gamma scale mixture yields automatic robustness to heavy-tailed and contaminated observations and admits Student-$t$, Laplace, and slash distributions as special cases. We design a partially-collapsed blocked Gibbs sampler that traverses the joint space of orderings, sparsity patterns and continuous parameters. Simulations across $(n,p)$ configurations with $p$ up to several hundreds confirm the theoretical rates and demonstrate substantial gains over graphical-horseshoe, DAG-Wishart, and PC-based competitors under contamination. An application to RNA-seq gene-expression data from \emph{The Cancer Genome Atlas} reveals biologically interpretable regulatory structure that competing methods fail to recover.

</details>


### [128] [weightflow: declarative, recipe-aware survey weighting in R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08491)
*Juan Pablo Ferreira*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: weightflow是一个基于R语言的依赖自由包，通过声明式、可管道化的API将复杂调查权重的完整层级调整流程（非响应校正、校准等）封装为可审计的recipe对象，并利用重缩放bootstrap和delete-a-PSU jackknife将每一阶段估计的不确定性传播到复制权重中，从而支持设计一致的方差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有R工具仅覆盖复杂调查权重构建流程的部分环节，缺乏将整个层级调整序列表达为单一可审计对象的能力，且无法将每个调整阶段（如非响应校正、校准）的估计变异性传播到复制权重中，导致最终方差估计未能完整反映所有中间步骤的不确定性。

Method: 作者开发了weightflow包，采用tidymodels风格的声明式API：通过step_*()函数链式定义权重调整recipe（延迟定义），再用prep()进行估计（定义与执行分离）。实现的方法包括：raking、事后分层、线性/GREG校准（含边界约束、岭惩罚、域分区及积分变体）、Wu-Sitter模型辅助校准、加权分类与基于机器学习（含交叉拟合）的响应倾向调整，以及R指标。复制权重通过重缩放bootstrap和delete-a-PSU jackknife对整个recipe进行再应用，确保所有估计阶段的不确定性均被携带。

Result: 该包生成的权重及复制权重可桥接到survey和srvyr包进行基于设计的推断。校准与方差结果与survey包验证一致。在捆绑样本及乌拉圭ECH住户调查微观数据上展示了完整流程，成功恢复了已知贫困率并附有基于设计的不确定性度量。

Conclusion: weightflow提供了一个统一、可重复、可审计的框架，将复杂调查权重构建的完整层级流程及其各阶段不确定性传播整合为一体，弥补了现有R工具在该工作流中的空白，为调查统计实践提供了更严谨的设计一致推断工具。

Abstract: Producing analysis weights for a complex survey requires a sequence of hierarchical adjustments (resolving unknown eligibility, dropping out-of-scope units, restoring within-household selection, correcting for nonresponse, and calibrating to known population totals), after which design-consistent variances must account for the fact that several adjustments were themselves estimated from the sample. Existing R tools cover parts of this workflow, but none expresses the whole cascade as a single auditable object, nor propagates the variability of every stage into the replicate weights. We present weightflow, a dependency-free (base R) package that builds survey weights through a declarative, pipeable, tidymodels-style API: a recipe is defined lazily as a chain of step_*() adjustments and estimated with prep(). Separating definition from application makes the process reproducible and auditable, and lets a rescaling bootstrap and a delete-a-PSU jackknife re-apply the entire recipe on each replicate, so the replicate weights carry the uncertainty of every estimated stage, not only of the final calibration. The package implements raking, post-stratification and linear/GREG calibration (with bounds, ridge penalisation, and domain-partitioned and integrative variants), model-assisted (Wu-Sitter) calibration, weighting-class and machine-learning response-propensity adjustments with cross-fitting, and representativity (R-)indicators. Weights and replicate weights bridge to the survey and srvyr packages for design-based inference. We validate the calibration and variance results against survey, and illustrate the full cascade on a bundled sample and on real household-survey microdata (the Uruguayan ECH), where it recovers a known poverty rate with design-based uncertainty.

</details>


### [129] [Calibrated Persistent-Laplacian CUSUM for Online Change-Point Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08635)
*Shiheng Nie,Yunguang Yue*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出PL-CUSUM方法，将持久拉普拉斯谱与Page-CUSUM递归结合，用于高维非线性时间序列的在线变点检测，在误报控制和检测延迟方面具有理论保证和实验优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于持久同调摘要的变点检测方法仅利用拓扑特征的计数信息（如Betti数），未能捕捉同维度内的连通性和几何结构信息。需要一种能利用更丰富谱信息的高维非线性时间序列在线检测方法。

Method: 提出PL-CUSUM：（1）将滑动窗口数据转化为点云；（2）计算持久拉普拉斯谱作为监测得分；（3）将得分输入Page-CUSUM递归进行在线变点检测；（4）设计Phase I/Phase II流程，Phase I进行参数选择和阈值校准，Phase II执行在线递归检测。

Result: 理论上：推导了Oracle检测器的误报延迟界，证明plug-in白化得分在有限监测窗口内仍能控制误报。实验上：在模拟系统及真实监测数据上，PL-CUSUM展现出稳定的误报控制和有竞争力的检测性能。

Conclusion: PL-CUSUM通过引入持久拉普拉斯谱信息，在保留拓扑特征表达能力的同时增强了同尺度内的结构捕捉能力，是一种在高维非线性场景下兼具理论保障和实用效果的在线变点检测方法。

Abstract: We propose the Persistent Laplacian Cumulative Sum (PL-CUSUM), an online change-point detection method for high-dimensional nonlinear time series. The method converts sliding windows into point clouds and uses persistent Laplacian spectra to construct the monitoring score for the Page cumulative sum (Page-CUSUM) recursion. Compared with detectors based only on persistent-homology summaries, PL-CUSUM further uses spectral information to capture within-scale connectivity and geometric structure beyond homology counts. Theoretically, we analyze two key performance criteria: false-alarm control and detection delay. We derive false-alarm-delay bounds for the oracle detector and show that the plug-in whitened score still controls false alarms over a finite monitoring horizon. Methodologically, we provide a Phase I/Phase II procedure that performs parameter selection and control-limit calibration before online recursion. Experiments on simulated systems and real monitoring data show that PL-CUSUM provides stable false-alarm control and competitive detection performance.

</details>


### [130] [Severity estimation in dependent collective risk models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08719)
*Christopher Blier-Wong*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 该论文研究了聚合风险模型中索赔次数与索赔强度存在相依性时的严重性问题。作者证明，池化索赔的经验分布收敛于一个规模偏倚的混合分布，而非边际严重性分布，因此直接对池化索赔拟合边际分布是不一致的。基于此，作者构建了一个复合似然估计方法，以保单为抽样单元，并建立了估计量的一致性和渐近正态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在保险精算的聚合风险模型中，标准做法假设索赔次数与索赔强度独立，从而池化后的索赔可视为来自边际严重性分布的样本。然而实际中两者常存在相依性（如高风险保单既可能更频繁出险，单次索赔金额也可能更高），此时池化索赔的经验分布究竟收敛于何种分布尚不明确，直接拟合边际分布的做法缺乏理论依据。

Method: (1) 理论推导：证明当索赔次数与强度存在任意相依时，池化索赔的经验分布函数收敛于"任意观测索赔的分布律"——即条件严重性分布的规模偏倚混合分布；(2) 估计方法：基于上述识别出的分布构建复合似然估计过程，以保单而非索赔作为抽样单元；(3) 统计推断：推导估计量的一致性和渐近正态性，并给出基于Godambe信息的保单层面标准误；(4) 具体化：在Sarmanov聚合风险模型中给出观测索赔密度和聚合均值的闭式表达式；(5) 模拟研究：验证朴素池化拟合的偏差、复合似然估计的修正效果以及保单层面标准误的覆盖率。

Result: (1) 池化索赔的经验分布收敛于规模偏倚的混合分布，而非边际严重性分布，因此直接对池化索赔拟合边际严重性的一般做法是不一致的；(2) 复合似然估计量具有一致性和渐近正态性，Godambe信息矩阵以保单为抽样单元；(3) 在Sarmanov模型下，观测索赔密度和聚合均值存在闭式解；(4) 模拟显示朴素池化方法存在显著偏差，复合似然估计能有效修正，保单层面标准误具有良好的覆盖率。

Conclusion: 当索赔次数与强度存在相依性时，精算师不能简单地将池化索赔视为来自边际严重性分布的样本。本文识别了池化索赔的真实目标分布，并提供了基于保单层面复合似然的一致估计框架，为相依聚合风险模型的严重性建模提供了理论支撑和实用工具。

Abstract: The collective risk model represents the aggregate loss of an insurance portfolio as a random sum of individual claim severities. When claim counts and severities are dependent, the claims pooled across policies are no longer a sample from the marginal severity distribution. We show that their empirical distribution converges to the law of an arbitrary observed claim, a size-biased mixture of the conditional severity distributions, so any procedure that fits the severity margin directly to pooled claims is inconsistent in general. The same result identifies the distribution that the pooled claims do sample, and we build a composite likelihood estimation procedure on that distribution. We establish consistency and asymptotic normality, with Godambe information in which the policy, rather than the claim, is the sampling unit. In a Sarmanov collective risk model, the observed-claim density and the aggregate mean are in closed form. A simulation study measures the bias of naive pooled-severity fitting, its correction by the composite likelihood, and the coverage of the policy-level standard errors.

</details>


### [131] [On improving the estimates of the sampling variances via Global-Local priors in Small Area Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08720)
*Sirapat Watakajaturaphon,Jairo Fúquene-Patiño*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的贝叶斯方法，在Fay-Herriot模型框架下利用Global-Local先验改进小域估计中抽样方差的估计，通过自适应MCMC算法解决计算难题，并在美国玉米产量和哥伦比亚教育程度指数两个实际案例中验证了方法的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典FH模型假设抽样方差已知，但实际中方差是从数据估计得到的，且往往波动较大。传统做法使用广义方差函数(GVF)进行稳定化，但仍有改进空间。作者旨在通过贝叶斯建模进一步改善抽样方差的后验估计质量。

Method: 提出一个分层贝叶斯模型，在抽样方差上施加Global-Local先验（如马蹄形先验、马蹄形+先验等），以自适应地控制向GVF后验估计的收缩程度。为处理条件分布中涉及Gamma函数的计算困难，开发了自适应MCMC算法。

Result: 通过模拟研究，将所提方法与竞争方法（经典FH、直接使用GVF等）进行比较，结果表明GL先验能有效改善方差估计。在美国农业部玉米产量和哥伦比亚市级教育程度指数两个真实数据应用中进一步验证了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 引入Global-Local先验的贝叶斯FH模型能够灵活地实现对方差估计的自适应收缩，在小样本域中提供更可靠的推断，具有良好的理论和实际应用价值。

Abstract: The Fay-Herriot (FH) model is widely used in official statistics to produce reliable estimates for domains with small sample sizes. In the classical FH model, the sampling variances are treated as known, even though they are typically estimated from the data. In practice, these variance estimates can be highly variable. To address this issue, practitioners often use Generalized Variance Functions (GVFs) to borrow strength across areas and stabilize estimation. In this work, we propose a new Bayesian model to improve the posterior estimation of the sampling variances in Small Area Estimation (SAE). Our proposed model incorporates Global-Local (GL) priors to improve the level of shrinkage toward the posterior estimates obtained with the GVF function. We study the theoretical properties of the proposed model and develop adaptive Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) algorithms to address computational challenges arising from conditional distributions involving Gamma functions. The performance of the proposed Bayesian model is investigated through simulation studies and compared with competing approaches. Finally, we implement our proposal in two real applications by estimating the Corn production from the U.S. Department of Agriculture and the Prevalence of the Educational Attainment Index at the municipality levels in Colombia.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [The Regularization Parameter: Sparse Precision Matrix Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07735)
*Aryan Eftekhari,Daniel Sergio Vega,Ernst-Jan Camiel Wit,Olaf Schenk*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于采样分布的闭式矩阵值正则化参数选择方法，用于稀疏精度矩阵估计，无需交叉验证即可实现与交叉验证相当的估计精度、更优的支持恢复，并将运行时间降低数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维低样本数据中，ℓ₁正则化高斯最大似然估计的稀疏精度矩阵估计高度依赖正则化参数的选择，传统交叉验证方法计算开销巨大，且缺乏理论指导。亟需一种有理论保证、计算高效的参数选择方案。

Method: 从ℓ₁正则化高斯最大似然估计的一阶最优性条件的采样分布出发，推导出闭式矩阵值正则化参数。核心思想是：预设估计量每个非零条目在重采样下满足其最优性条件的概率，从而直接确定正则化参数，完全规避交叉验证。同时证明了该参数在标准条件下具备渐近标度性质，可保证估计量的一致性和稀疏一致性（sparsistency）。

Result: 在合成高斯/非高斯数据、基因微阵列和神经影像等真实数据上，所提方法估计精度与交叉验证相当，支持恢复更优，且运行时间减少数个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过最优性条件的采样分布理论直接确定正则化参数，是一种兼具统计保证和计算效率的有效策略，为高维稀疏精度矩阵估计中的超参数选择提供了新范式。

Abstract: Sparse precision matrix estimation provides an interpretable and computationally efficient framework for modeling conditional dependencies in high-dimensional, low-sample-size data. A recurring challenge is appropriately selecting the regularization parameter that controls estimator sparsity and strikes a balance between underfitting and overfitting. We propose a closed-form, matrix-valued regularization parameter derived from the sampling distribution of the first-order optimality conditions of the $\ell_1$-regularized Gaussian maximum-likelihood estimator. By prescribing the probability that each nonzero entry of the estimator satisfies its optimality condition under resampling, we eliminate the need for cross-validation. The resulting regularization parameter is shown to attain asymptotic scaling properties that, under standard conditions, provide consistency and sparsistency of the estimator. On synthetic Gaussian and non-Gaussian datasets, as well as real-world gene microarray and neuroimaging applications, the proposed approach achieves estimation accuracy comparable to cross-validation, delivers superior support recovery, and reduces runtime by several orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [133] [Distributionally Faithful Imputation via Positive Semi-Definite Kernel Density Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07767)
*Andrea Basteri,Carlo Ciliberto,Alessandro Rudi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 将MCAR下的缺失值插补重新定义为从掩码观测值中进行密度估计的问题，利用正半定核密度构建凸经验风险问题，通过Newton内点法求解，实现一致且高效的单一/多重插补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数插补方法依赖启发式规则或限制性参数假设，忽略了联合数据分布，导致统计推断和机器学习流程受到缺失值严重影响。需要一种基于分布估计的、理论上更可靠的插补方法。

Method: 将MCAR缺失机制下的插补重新表述为掩码观测的密度估计问题；采用正半定(PSD)核密度函数，使得观测边际分布具有闭合形式，从而将问题转化为凸经验风险最小化；使用Newton内点法求解该凸问题；从拟合的同一密度中可同时生成单一插补和多重插补。

Result: PSD Impute模型具备统计一致性，在高度正则的概率分布上展现出快速的自适应超额风险，能够突破维度灾难。在1个合成数据集和11个真实数据集上的初步实验表明，其分布准确度已与主流插补基线相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: PSD Impute通过核密度估计框架为MCAR缺失值插补提供了理论上优雅、实践上有效的解决方案，具有广阔的应用前景。

Abstract: Missing values undermine statistical inference and machine learning pipelines, yet most imputation methods rely on heuristics or restrictive parametric assumptions that ignore the joint data distribution. We recast imputation under missing completely at random (MCAR) as density estimation from masked observations: estimate a distribution whose observed marginals exactly match those in the data. Leveraging positive semi definite (PSD) kernel densities we obtain a convex empirical risk problem with closed form marginals, solvable by a Newton interior point method. The resulting PSD Impute model yields both single and multiple imputations from the same fitted density, enjoys statistical consistency with fast adaptive excess risk beating the curse of dimensionality for very regular probabilities. Preliminary experiments on one synthetic and eleven real world datasets already indicate competitive distributional accuracy compared with popular imputation baselines, suggesting strong practical promise.

</details>


### [134] [Expressivity and Statistical Trade-offs in Diffusion Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07967)
*Viet Vu,Renyuan Xu,Jiacheng Zhang,Yufei Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文识别出漂移Lipschitz预算K是决定扩散策略表达能力和统计行为的核心量：表达能力随K增大而提升（值逼近误差为O(1/K)），但统计复杂度也随之增加；通过平衡两者，给出了不同漂移类下的有限样本性能界，并据此提出根据样本量选择K的实践原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在实践中展现出强大的表达能力，能建模复杂、多模态、非高斯的动作分布，但其表达性的数学根源尚不清楚，且如何在有限数据下充分利用该表达能力也缺乏理论指导。

Method: 将漂移Lipschitz预算K作为核心分析量，从逼近论角度量化表达能力（证明值逼近误差上界O(1/K)及匹配下界），结合神经网络漂移参数化下的统计复杂度分析，通过平衡逼近误差与统计误差，推导有限样本性能界。

Result: (1) 表达性：K-Lipschitz漂移的扩散策略可实现O(1/K)的值逼近误差，且存在匹配下界；(2) 统计：通用神经网络漂移的性能界为Õ(n^{-2/(m+6)})，单边耗散漂移类可达更优界Õ(n^{-2/(m+4)})；(3) 数值实验验证了K与样本量间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 扩散策略的表达性与统计效率由漂移Lipschitz预算K统一刻画，实践中应根据可用样本量选择K，再据此确定具有相应固定Lipschitz系数的神经网络架构。

Abstract: Diffusion-based policies have recently emerged as powerful policy parameterizations for reinforcement learning, representing state-conditioned action distributions as terminal laws of diffusion processes with parameterized drifts. This terminal-law representation has shown substantial expressive flexibility in practice, enabling diffusion policies to model complex, multimodal, and highly non-Gaussian action distributions; however, it remains unclear what mathematically drives this expressivity and how to fully exploit it when the policy is learned from finite data. In this paper, we identify the drift Lipschitz budget $K$ as a central quantity governing the expressivity and statistical behavior of diffusion policies. We quantify expressivity through approximation: diffusion policies with $K$-Lipschitz drifts can concentrate near optimal deterministic policies and achieve value approximation error of order $1/K$; moreover, we prove a matching lower bound under nondegenerate diffusion noise. This increased expressivity comes with a statistical cost. When the drift is parameterized by neural networks, increasing $K$ improves approximation but increases statistical complexity. Balancing these two terms yields a finite-sample performance gap of order $\tilde{O}(n^{-2/(m+6)})$ for generic neural-network drifts, and a sharper rate $\tilde{O}(n^{-2/(m+4)})$ for one-sided dissipative drift classes, where $n$ is the sample size and $m$ is the dimension of the state space. Numerical experiments provide empirical evidence for the sample-dependent trade-off in $K$, supporting both theoretical regimes. Our framework also suggests a practical implementation principle: choose the diffusion budget $K$ according to the available sample size, and then select a neural-network architecture with the corresponding fixed Lipschitz coefficient.

</details>


### [135] [Bayesian Experimental Design via Score Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08335)
*Angus Phillips,Gavin Kerrigan,Tom Rainforth*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出将期望信息增益（EIG）的双重不可解性与策略学习解耦：先独立求解一个与策略无关的得分匹配问题，再利用学到的得分近似以单重不可解方式训练策略，从而将乘性计算代价转为加性代价，大幅降低策略训练的计算负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于策略的贝叶斯实验设计（BED）的核心瓶颈在于EIG的双重不可解性（涉及对参数空间和结果空间的双重积分），导致训练深度策略网络时需要昂贵或复杂的近似，限制了在策略优化本身（如架构搜索、超参数调优）上的投入。

Method: 提出将EIG的双重不可解性与策略学习分离的两阶段方法：(1) 首先求解一个独立于策略的得分匹配问题，学习EIG的得分函数近似；(2) 利用学到的得分近似，以单重不可解（仅需对结果空间积分）的方式训练策略网络。这使得原本乘性的似然评估代价变为加性代价。

Result: 实验表明，该方法可以在不引入乘性似然评估代价的情况下训练多个竞争性策略，通过选择最优策略提升性能，即使不进行显式的超参数或架构搜索也能获得增益。

Conclusion: 通过得分匹配将EIG的双重不可解性从策略学习中解耦，显著降低了策略训练的重复成本，使得多次训练策略（用于架构搜索、超参数调优或规避局部最优）变得经济可行，为贝叶斯实验设计的策略优化开辟了更高效的技术路径。

Abstract: Policy-based approaches to Bayesian experimental design (BED) allow the learning of deep policy networks that adaptively make intelligent design decisions based on previously collected data. However, the training of such policies is often held back by a fundamental challenge: the double intractability of the expected information gain (EIG). This necessitates expensive or complex approximations that restrict the effort one can invest in optimising the policy itself. To address this, we show that the double intractability of the EIG can be isolated from the policy learning by first solving a score matching problem that is independent of the policy used, then using the learned score approximation to train the policy in a singly intractable manner. This turns the key multiplicative cost into an additive one and reduces the computational burden on the policy training itself, making it far cheaper to train the policy multiple times when needed, e.g. for architecture search, hyperparameter tuning, or avoiding local optima. In our experiments we train multiple competitive policies without inducing a multiplicative cost in likelihood evaluations, which can increase performance by allowing us to select the best policy even without performing hyperparameter or architecture searches.

</details>


### [136] [Prediction-Powered Active Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08347)
*Kianoosh Ashouritaklimi,Valentin Kilian,Daolang Huang,Tom Rainforth,François Caron*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: PPAT 将 LURE 无偏估计器与基于预测的控制变量相结合，利用黑箱模型的预测对损失进行残差化，在保持无偏性的同时降低方差，并设计了专门的采集规则和渐近正态置信区间，在表格回归和图像分类任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动测试（Active Testing）的风险估计器未能利用强大黑箱模型提供的信息性预测。尽管这些预测在标签仍昂贵的场景中日益可用，但现有方法要么将它们作为有偏的伪标签使用，要么完全忽略，导致估计效率低下。

Method: 提出 Prediction-Powered Active Testing (PPAT) 框架：(1) 使用预测驱动的控制变量（control variate）对损失进行残差化，结合 LURE 无偏估计器，实现无偏且降方差的估计；(2) 推导面向估计器方差最小化的 oracle 采集规则及可操作的 surrogate-based 采集规则；(3) 建立 PPAT 的渐近正态性，构建渐近有效的置信区间。

Result: 在表格回归和图像分类任务上，PPAT 在风险估计精度上超越现有方法；其置信区间以显著更少的标签和更窄的区间宽度达到目标覆盖率。

Conclusion: PPAT 通过将黑箱模型预测作为控制变量而非伪标签，成功在主动测试中实现了无偏、高效的风险估计，并提供可靠的置信区间，为标签高效的风险评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: Active testing provides a label--efficient approach to risk estimation by adaptively selecting which test points should be labelled. However, existing estimators fail to exploit the informative predictions of powerful black--box models, even though such predictions are increasingly available in settings where labels remain expensive. To address this, we propose \textbf{Prediction--Powered Active Testing (PPAT)}, a novel label--efficient risk estimation framework that combines the unbiased LURE estimator \citep{farquhar2021statistical} with a prediction--powered control variate. Rather than using proxy predictions as biased pseudo--labels, PPAT uses them to residualise the loss, preserving unbiasedness while reducing variance. Beyond the estimator itself, PPAT also changes which points should be acquired: we derive oracle and practical surrogate--based acquisition rules tailored to reducing the variance of our estimator. Moreover, we establish asymptotic normality for PPAT, yielding asymptotically valid confidence intervals and thus a principled estimate of the uncertainty around our estimates. Across tabular regression and image--classification tasks, PPAT outperforms existing methods in risk estimation, while its confidence intervals attain the target coverage with substantially fewer labels and smaller widths.

</details>


### [137] [Statistical Efficiency and Inference of Quantile Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08444)
*Zijie Cheng,Yang Peng,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从统计效率角度研究基于分位数的分布强化学习，重点分析分布策略评估问题。作者通过生成模型构建分位数估计量，证明了估计误差以 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{m/n})$ 速率收敛，达到最优参数化 $\sqrt{n}$ 收敛率，并推导了渐近分布、半参数效率界，以及在高维极限下的渐近效率与Berry-Esseen定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布强化学习研究多关注算法设计与经验表现，缺乏对分位数方法统计效率的理论理解。本文旨在填补这一空白，从统计学角度系统研究分位数分布强化学习在策略评估中的估计误差界、渐近分布与效率界，为该方法提供严谨的统计理论基础。

Method: 基于分位数投影的分布Bellman方程，定义分位数不动点 $\eta_m$；利用生成模型构建经验MDP上的估计量 $\eta_m^{(n)}$；在 $W_\infty$ 度量下建立非渐近误差界；推导分位数参数的渐近正态分布 $\sqrt{n}(\theta_m^{(n)}-\theta_m)$ 并刻画半参数效率界；进一步分析分位数数量发散时的极限协方差结构，并与非参数模型效率界对比；最后建立光滑泛函的Berry-Esseen定理。

Result: 在固定分位数数目下，估计误差在 $W_\infty$ 度量下满足 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{m/n})$ 的尺度，达到 $\sqrt{n}$ 最优参数收敛率。估计量达到半参数效率界。在分位数数目趋于无穷时，极限协方差结构与非参数分布策略评估的半参数效率界匹配，表明分位数估计量在无穷维极限下仍保持渐近效率。进一步建立了光滑泛函的Berry-Esseen定理，为统计推断提供理论基础。

Conclusion: 基于分位数的分布强化学习方法不仅在计算上具有吸引力，在统计效率上也具有坚实的理论保证：在有限维和无穷维极限下均可达到最优收敛率与效率界，且支持有效的统计推断。这为分位数方法在分布强化学习中的广泛应用提供了重要的理论支撑。

Abstract: In this paper, we study quantile-based distributional reinforcement learning from the perspective of statistical efficiency. We focus on distributional policy evaluation, whose goal is to characterize the return distribution, namely the distribution of discounted cumulative rewards under a given policy. To obtain a finite-dimensional representation of the return distribution, we consider the quantile fixed point $η_m$ induced by the quantile-projected distributional Bellman equation. Assuming access to a generative model, we construct an estimator $η_m^{(n)}$ based on an empirical Markov decision process. For a fixed number of quantiles $m$, we establish a non-asymptotic error bound for $η_m^{(n)}$ and $η_m$ under the supremum $W_\infty$ metric, showing that the estimation error scales as $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{m/n})$ with respect to $m$ and $n$. This implies that the quantile-based distributional policy evaluation problem can be solved with sample efficiency, achieving the optimal parametric $\sqrt{n}$ convergence rate. We derive the asymptotic distribution of the quantile parameters $\sqrt{n}(θ_m^{(n)}-θ_m)$ and characterize the semiparametric efficiency bound, which is attained by our estimator. Beyond the fixed-dimensional setting, we investigate the asymptotic regime in which the number of quantiles diverges. We characterize the limit covariance structure and show that it matches the semiparametric efficiency bound of the nonparametric model for distributional policy evaluation, showing that quantile-based estimators remain asymptotically efficient in the infinite-dimensional limit. Finally, we establish a Berry--Esseen theorem for smooth functionals $\sqrt{n}(η_m^{(n)}(s)-η_m(s))f$, thereby providing a foundation for statistically valid inference on functionals of the quantile-projected return distribution.

</details>


### [138] [High-Dimensional Procrustes Matching via Tree Counts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08538)
*Xiaochun Niu,Tselil Schramm,Jiaming Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究高维Procrustes匹配问题，提出了一种基于"宽树"加权计数的多项式时间算法，在常数相关性下即可实现精确恢复，并给出了改进的信息论下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Procrustes匹配问题在低维（d=O(log n)）已得到充分研究，但高维情形（d≫log n）此前几乎空白：现有匹配保证即使仅要求信息论恢复，也需要近乎完美的相关性ρ=1-o(1)。本文旨在填补这一空白，在常数相关性下实现高维精确匹配。

Method: 提出一种多项式时间算法，通过计算和比较一组精心选择的"宽树"（wide trees）的加权计数来实现匹配。该算法在d ≥ polylog(n)时，对任意ρ² > √α（α≈0.338为Otter树计数常数）以高概率成功。同时，作者还进行了低阶优势计算（low-degree advantage calculation），表明ρ² > √α是任何树计数算法的必要条件。

Result: 1. 算法结果：当d ≥ polylog(n)且ρ² > √α ≈ 0.581时，算法以高概率实现精确恢复；2. 信息论保证：精确恢复在ρ² ≳ max{log n/d, √(log n/n)}时是可能的；3. 下界：低阶优势计算表明ρ² > √α对任何树计数算法是必要的。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在高维Procrustes匹配问题中实现了常数相关性下的多项式时间精确恢复，揭示了Otter树计数常数α在其中的关键角色，并给出了近乎匹配的信息论下界与算法上界。

Abstract: Suppose we observe two sets of $n$ Gaussian vectors in $\mathbb{R}^d$, with the promise that, after applying a permutation of $[n]$ and a rotation of $\mathbb{R}^d$, the two sets are $ρ$-correlated. The Procrustes matching problem asks us to recover the unknown permutation of $[n]$ that aligns the two sets. The problem is well-studied in the low-dimensional regime $d=O(\log n)$, but the high-dimensional regime $d\gg \log n$ has remained largely uncharted: prior matching guarantees require nearly perfect correlation $ρ=1-o(1)$, even for information-theoretic recovery.
  Our main result is a polynomial-time algorithm for exact recovery at constant correlation. The algorithm works by computing and comparing weighted counts of a specially chosen family of ``wide'' trees. So long as $d\ge \mathrm{polylog}(n)$, the algorithm succeeds with high probability for any $ρ^2>\sqrtα$, where $α\approx 0.338$ is Otter's tree-counting constant.
  We complement this algorithmic result with an improved information-theoretic guarantee, showing that exact recovery is possible when $ρ^2 \gtrsim \max\{\log n/d,\sqrt{\log n/n}\}$. We also carry out a low-degree advantage calculation, which suggests that the condition $ρ^2 > \sqrtα$ is necessary for any tree-counting algorithm.

</details>


### [139] [Score Accuracy Along the Forward Diffusion Does Not Certify Numerical Stability in Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08757)
*Yiwei Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文揭示了扩散模型中得分匹配的一个关键问题：即使前向边际误差（forward-marginal error）任意小，离散化反向采样器仍可能出现数值不稳定，导致 Wasserstein 距离发散。作者构造了反例证明弱收敛可以与所有矩发散共存，并提出了对紧凑支撑数据通过去噪器投影（denoiser projection）来恢复 Wasserstein 收敛性的简单正结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示当前扩散模型理论中的一个盲区：得分匹配控制的是前向边际过程的误差，但实际采样时反向过程的离散化（如 Euler–Maruyama）沿自身轨迹运行，小前向边际误差并不能保证数值稳定性或矩的收敛。需要弥合这一理论与实践的鸿沟。

Method: 理论构造：1）构造一个光滑得分场，使前向边际 L² 误差任意小且路径空间全变差距离任意小，但其 Euler–Maruyama 离散化所有正阶矩发散；2）在固定有限神经架构内构造一簇有界全局 Lipschitz 去噪器，具有同样的失败模式。正结果：对紧凑支撑数据，将学习到的去噪器投影到包含支撑集的已知有界闭凸集上，保持逐点精度并获得网格均匀矩界。实验：使用小型 DiT 风格网络验证稀有轨迹上的大增长及去噪器投影的抑制效果。

Result: 反例表明弱收敛（依概率收敛）可与所有 p≥1 的 Wasserstein 距离 W_p 发散共存。正结果证明去噪器投影可保持逐点精度，给出网格均匀矩界，并在温和局部正则性下实现 Wasserstein 收敛。实验证实了稀有数值轨迹上的大幅增长以及投影方法的有效抑制。

Conclusion: 前向边际误差小不足以保证反向采样器的数值稳定性和矩收敛，但通过将去噪器投影到已知支撑集的凸集上，可以在紧凑支撑数据场景下恢复 Wasserstein 收敛性，这为扩散模型的可靠部署提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Score matching controls average error under the forward marginals, but a discretized reverse-time sampler evaluates the learned score along its own trajectory. We show that small forward-marginal error does not guarantee numerical stability. We construct a single smooth score field with arbitrarily small forward-marginal $L^2$ error. The learned reverse-time process is nonexplosive, has moments of every order, and can be arbitrarily close to the exact reverse-time process in path-space total variation. Yet its Euler--Maruyama discretizations converge in probability while every positive moment diverges. Thus weak convergence can hold even though every Wasserstein distance $W_p$, $p\ge1$, diverges.
  The same failure can occur within one fixed finite neural architecture. We construct a family of bounded, globally Lipschitz denoisers for which both the forward-marginal error and the path-space total variation distance tend to zero, while their Euler--Maruyama endpoints diverge in every $W_p$. For compactly supported data, we also give a simple positive result. Projecting the learned denoiser onto a known bounded closed convex set containing the support preserves pointwise accuracy, gives grid-uniform moment bounds, and yields Wasserstein convergence under mild local regularity. Experiments with a small fixed DiT-style network show large growth along rare numerical trajectories and its suppression by denoiser projection, while overall trajectory errors remain small.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [Context Graphs for Proactive Enterprise Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07721)
*Avinash Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出上下文图（Context Graph）结合增量检测引擎、主动性评分器和LLM驱动的呈现层，将企业AI从被动响应转变为主动推送，实验表明可将平均信息呈现时间从47分钟降至30秒以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于RAG和智能体框架的企业AI系统本质上是反应式的——它们等待人类查询后才行动。真正的企业生产力提升需要主动式智能体：在员工提问之前就能主动推送相关、可操作的信息。

Method: 提出三层架构：（1）上下文图（Context Graph）：一种实时关系数据结构，建模企业实体、关系及状态变迁；（2）增量检测引擎（Delta Detection Engine）：持续监控图中状态变化；主动性评分器（Proactivity Scorer）：按紧迫性、相关性和角色匹配度对候选洞察进行排序；（3）LLM驱动的呈现层（Surfacing Layer）：交付带接地解释的排序通知。最终统一为Proactivity Score函数，并使用NetworkX和Anthropic Claude API完成端到端Python实现。

Result: 在三个通用企业案例（合同生命周期管理、工程事件响应、销售管道健康监控）中评估，达到Precision@5为0.83，假阳性率0.11，平均呈现时间从反应式基线的47分钟降至30秒以内。

Conclusion: 上下文图驱动的主动性机制能显著提升企业AI的信息呈现时效性和精准度，验证了从被动到主动范式转变的可行性与有效性。
