Conclusion: INTENT框架能够在交叉路口场景中高精度地提前预测车辆意图，验证了LSTM在该任务上的有效性，为自动驾驶车辆在复杂交互场景下的安全决策提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Vehicle intention prediction is a pivotal aspect in the agility and safety of autonomous vehicles in all driving scenarios; if genuine enhancement of autonomous vehicles are required, we need to make them adopt human interpretation of driver's intention especially in cases that require a lot of human interaction as well as complex driving behaviors like the ones at intersections, roundabouts and emergency cases such as sudden stops where vehicle intention prediction helps in taking the correct evasive action within a real time period where every second of action makes an impact and can prevent a catastrophe from taking place. In the worst case, it helps minimize the damage and make safety a priority. Intention prediction can also be used to enhance trajectory prediction (intention conditioned trajectory prediction). In this study, The INTENT framework is proposed using LSTM model to predict the vehicle's intention at intersections 2 seconds ahead of the event occurrence to predict whether the cars in intersections are going straight, turning left, or turning right. Various model experiments and ablation study are thoroughly tested on InD dataset achieving 99.71% accuracy.

</details>


### [175] [Blind-Spots-Bench: Evaluating Blind Spots in Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08317)
*Matteo Santelmo,Xiuying Wei,Israa Fakih,Felix Bauer,Juan Garcia Giraldo,Chengkun Li,Etienne Bamas,Emmanuel Abbé*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 blind-spots-bench，一个包含 235 个样本的基准测试，旨在通过人类认为简单但现代 AI 模型仍然难以完成的任务来暴露模型的盲点。作者从 AI 课程学生收集原始问题，经清洗标注后建立任务分类法，并开发自动化评分流水线评估多种模型，发现闭源前沿模型显著优于开源模型（差距约 10%），且没有单一模型在所有任务类型上占据优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AI 模型在许多成熟基准上表现强劲，却仍然在人类觉得极其简单的任务上失败（如字符串操作、画五条腿的狗），这表明现有基准可能低估了模型的持久性盲点。因此需要设计一个专门针对这些盲点的诊断性压力测试基准。

Method: 从 AI 课程学生收集原始问题，经清洗和结构化参考答案标注，构建包含 235 个样本的数据集并提出任务分类法；开发自动化评分流水线，支持评估开源和闭源的语言模型、视觉-语言模型以及图像生成模型。

Result: 闭源前沿模型在 blind-spots-bench 上显著优于开源模型，差距约 10%，即便它们在现有基准上表现相当；细粒度分析显示没有单一模型在所有任务类型上占优，某些任务对所有评估模型都构成挑战。

Conclusion: blind-spots-bench 可作为一种有效的诊断性压力测试工具，用于识别当前现代模型中的具体弱点，揭示了现有基准未能充分衡量的盲点问题。

Abstract: Modern AI models achieve strong performance on many established benchmarks, yet they still fail on tasks that humans find almost trivial, such as manipulating a string or drawing a dog with five legs. These examples suggest that existing benchmarks may under-measure persistent blind spots in current systems. We introduce $\texttt{blind-spots-bench}$, a benchmark designed to expose such blind spots through tasks that appear simple for humans but remain challenging for modern AI. We collect raw questions from students in an AI course, clean and annotate them with structured reference solutions, and propose a task taxonomy tailored to the resulting dataset of 235 samples. We further develop an automated grading pipeline to evaluate a wide range of models, including open-weight and closed-source language, vision-language, and image-generation models. Our analysis on $\texttt{blind-spots-bench}$ reveals that closed-source frontier models can substantially outperform open-weight models with even $\approx10\%$ gap, even when they attain comparable performance on existing benchmarks. A more fine-grained analysis shows that no single model dominates across all task types, and that some tasks remain challenging for all evaluated models. These results highlight the value of $\texttt{blind-spots-bench}$ as a diagnostic stress test for identifying concrete weaknesses in current modern models.

</details>


### [176] [MobiDiff: Semantic-Aware Multi-Channel Discrete Diffusion for Human Mobility Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08357)
*Rongchao Xu,Lin Jiang,Dahai Yu,Ximiao Li,Taichi Liu,Desheng Zhang,Yuan Tian,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MobiDiff 是一个端到端的离散扩散框架，通过对多通道语义骨架直接去噪来生成移动性数据，无需昂贵的插值和潜在轨迹构建。它将签到事件分解为空间、活动和时间通道，并采用结构化掩码策略捕捉轨迹级模式和事件内依赖，在三个城市数据集上实现了高保真度、隐私保护和显著加速的生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类移动性数据对交通优化、城市规划和资源分配至关重要，但真实数据采集成本高且因隐私问题难以共享。现有扩散方法依赖连续或潜在时空轨迹，无法原生建模具有显式区域、活动、时间和间隔结构的离散语义事件，且存在插值昂贵、流程繁琐等问题。

Method: 提出 MobiDiff，一个端到端离散扩散框架。核心设计包括：(1) 将每个签到事件分解为空间、活动、时间三个离散通道；(2) 采用结构化的事件级、组级和通道级掩码策略，联合捕捉轨迹级移动模式和事件内依赖关系；(3) 直接在多通道语义骨架上进行去噪生成，避免了传统方法中的插值、潜在轨迹构建和粗到细实现流程。

Result: 在亚特兰大、波士顿和西雅图三个大规模真实数据集上验证：(1) 有效保留了轨迹长度和时间间隔分布，在更广泛的移动性统计指标上保持竞争力；(2) 推理速度显著领先，平均比 GeoGen 快 5.3 倍；(3) 具备良好的隐私保护能力。

Conclusion: 离散扩散为合成移动性数据生成提供了一个可解释且高效的框架，MobiDiff 通过直接建模离散语义事件结构，在保真度、隐私性和效率三方面均表现出色。

Abstract: Human mobility data are essential for transportation optimization, urban planning, and resource allocation, yet real-world mobility data are costly to collect and difficult to share due to privacy concerns. Recent diffusion-based methods have shown promise in synthesizing realistic mobility patterns, but they typically rely on continuous or latent spatio-temporal traces, limiting their ability to natively model discrete semantic events with explicit region, activity, time, and interval structures. To address this issue, we introduce MobiDiff, an end-to-end discrete diffusion framework that efficiently generates mobility data by directly denoising multi-channel semantic skeletons, avoiding the costly interpolation, latent trace construction, and coarse-to-fine realization pipelines widely used in existing diffusion-based methods. Specifically, MobiDiff decomposes each human check-in event into spatial, activity, and temporal channels, and employs structured event-, group-, and channel-level masking to jointly capture trajectory-level mobility patterns and within-event dependencies. We evaluate generation fidelity, privacy-preserving, and efficiency on three large-scale real-world datasets from Atlanta, Boston, and Seattle. Results show that MobiDiff effectively preserves trajectory length and temporal interval distributions while remaining competitive across broader mobility statistics; it is also much faster than state-of-the-art methods, e.g., 5.3$\times$ faster than GeoGen on average during inference. These findings suggest that discrete diffusion offers an interpretable and efficient framework for synthetic mobility data generation.

</details>


### [177] [FedOPAL: One-Shot Federated Learning via Analytic Visual Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08368)
*Lingyu Qiu,Daniela Annunziata,Stefano Izzo,Fabio Giampaolo,Francesco Piccialli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FedOPAL提出一种将视觉提示作为特征矫正器的方法，通过局部近端约束主动将异构数据的特征分布矫正到线性可分空间，使得解析联邦学习的静态特征假设得以满足，在保持零服务端训练成本的同时达到与迭代方法相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前联邦学习面临通信带宽瓶颈，单次联邦学习虽减少通信轮次但存在服务端计算成本高和超参数敏感问题；解析联邦学习虽通过最小二乘闭式解实现高效梯度自由聚合，但在非独立同分布环境下，其静态特征假设失效，导致特征流形错位、模型性能严重下降。

Method: 提出FedOPAL框架，核心思想是将视觉提示（visual prompts）作为特征矫正器（feature rectifiers），通过施加局部近端约束（local proximal constraints），主动将异构数据的特征分布矫正到一个线性可分空间，从而满足解析联邦学习的理论假设，实现梯度自由的联邦聚合。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，FedOPAL不仅显著优于原有解析联邦学习方法，而且在保持零服务端训练成本的条件下，分类精度与当前最先进的迭代式联邦学习方法相当。

Conclusion: FedOPAL成功弥合了解析联邦学习与非独立同分布数据之间的矛盾，为边缘侧大模型高效协作提供了一种新的工程范式。

Abstract: With the widespread deployment of basic models in edge intelligence, communication bandwidth has become a core bottleneck restricting the scalability of federated learning. Although one-shot federated learning alleviates this problem by minimizing communication rounds, existing iterative fine-tuning or knowledge distillation methods still face challenges such as high server-side computational costs and hyperparameter sensitivity. Analytical federated learning achieves efficient gradientfree aggregation using least-squares closed-form solutions, but in environments with non-independent and identically distributed data, its static feature assumptions fail, leading to feature manifold misalignment and severely impairing model performance. To address this contradiction, this paper proposes the FedOPAL framework. This framework adapts the visual prompts as feature rectifiers, actively correcting the feature distribution of heterogeneous data to a linearly separable space by applying local proximal constraints, thereby satisfying the theoretical assumptions of analytical federated learning. Experimental results show that FedOPAL not only significantly outperforms the original analytical methods on several benchmarks, but also achieves accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art iterative methods while maintaining zero server-side training costs, providing a new engineering paradigm for efficient collaboration of large models on the edge.

</details>


### [178] [Towards Mechanistically Understanding Why Memorized Knowledge Fails to Generalize in Large Language Model Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08393)
*Lu Dai,Ziyang Rao,Yili Wang,Hanqing Wang,Hao Liu,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 微调LLM注入新知识时存在"知道-使用差距"：模型能快速记忆新事实，但无法将其用于下游推理。作者通过自修补技术发现，记忆的表征虽存在但未正确路由到计算有效层，并提出简单启发式策略可恢复58-75%的泛化失败案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM微调注入新知识时，模型虽能快速记忆事实，却无法将其用于推理任务。该现象尚未被充分形式化定义和系统分析，缺乏对其内部机制的理解。

Method: 首先将问题形式化为"知道-使用差距"(Knowing–Using Gap)，从准确率差距和时间滞后两个维度刻画。然后提出自修补(self-patching)干预技术，通过重定位激活表征来追踪知识在模型内部的空间渗透动态。最后基于知识回路错位假说，设计简单启发式策略恢复泛化能力。

Result: 自修补识别出激活位置，重定位表征能显著改善泛化失败案例，验证了知识回路错位假说：记忆的表征存在但未被路由到计算有效层。启发式策略恢复58-75%的泛化失败oracle上限，跨领域实验证实了发现的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LLM的知识记忆与推理使用之间存在"知道-使用差距"，根源在于知识回路错位而非表征缺失。通过诊断性发现可设计实用策略大幅缓解该问题。

Abstract: Fine-tuning LLMs to inject new knowledge faces a critical challenge: LLMs can quickly memorize new facts, yet fail to use them for downstream reasoning tasks. We formalize this failure as the \textit{\textbf{Knowing--Using Gap}}, characterized by an accuracy gap and a temporal lag between memorization and generalization. To understand this phenomenon, we fine-tune LLMs with unseen knowledge and monitor the spatial permeation dynamics of the knowledge internally using a novel intervention technique called self-patching. Self-patching identifies activation locations where relocating representations substantially improves failed generalization cases. These results are consistent with a knowledge-circuit misalignment hypothesis: memorized representations can exist internally but may not be routed to computation-effective layers. To demonstrate the practicality of this diagnostic finding, we design a simple heuristic strategy which recovers 58--75\% of the oracle headroom in generalization failure. Experiments are done cross-domain for the robustness of this finding.

</details>


### [179] [Game Theory Driven Multi-Agent Framework Mitigates Language Model Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08403)
*Runzhe Liu,Biquan Bie,Zihao Wang,Yuchao Ma,Yexin Liu,Xinghai Li,Harry Yang,Wenbo Yang,Jinzhe Cao,Shengyang Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出G-Frame自适应多智能体框架，融合贝叶斯与团队博弈原理，构建闭环数据合成与训练流程，生成大规模专业语料，训练出7B模型OmniChem，在化学任务上媲美GPT-4o mini，幻觉降低79.46%，并在分子设计与合成规划中展现先进能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级大语言模型在规则驱动的科学领域（如化学）中，倾向于模仿语言表层模式而非进行公理化推理，导致幻觉频发，严重制约其实际应用。现有方法缺乏有效的领域约束内化机制。

Method: 提出G-Frame自适应多智能体框架，融合贝叶斯推断和团队博弈理论。通过结构化推理迫使模型内化领域约束，建立自动化闭环系统进行高质量数据合成：生成363,045条思维链和199,589对问答数据，并基于此训练7B参数模型OmniChem。

Result: OmniChem在自定义基准和ChemBench上与GPT-4o mini性能持平；相较于基础架构，幻觉率降低79.46%；在分子设计和合成规划等任务中展现出先进能力。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了利用自适应多智能体克服LLM固有推理缺陷的可扩展范式，为加速专门科学领域的知识发现提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The application of lightweight Large Language Models in rule-based scientific domains remains severely limited by their tendency to mimic linguistic patterns rather than reproduce axiomatic reasoning, causing frequent hallucinations. Here, we show that G-Frame, an adaptive multi-agent framework integrating Bayesian and team game principles, establishes an automated closed-loop for high-quality data synthesis and model training. By forcing the internalization of domain constraints through structured reasoning, we synthesized a specialized corpus of 363,045 chains-of-thought and 199,589 question-answer pairs. The resulting 7B model OmniChem achieves performance parity with GPT 4o mini on custom benchmarks and ChemBench while exhibiting a 79.46% reduction in hallucinations relative to its base architecture. We further demonstrate the advanced capabilities of OmniChem in molecular design and synthesis planning. This work establishes a scalable paradigm utilizing adaptive multi-agents to overcome inherent reasoning deficiencies, offering a feasible pathway for accelerating knowledge discovery in specialized scientific fields.

</details>


### [180] [OmniFood-Bench: Evaluating VLMs for Nutrient Reasoning and Personalized Health Advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08423)
*Qian Jiang,Zhecheng Shi,Jingpu Yang,Zirui Song,Miao Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OmniFood-Bench基准，评估视觉语言模型在食品理解中的三层能力：基础感知、定量推理和安全建议。实验揭示模型在视觉识别与物理量估计之间存在严重的“语义-物理鸿沟”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食品相关的VLM基准仅关注粗粒度分类（如菜品识别），无法评估真实饮食管理所需的推理链：从识别隐藏成分到估算物理质量，再到综合安全医疗建议。这种视觉外观与内在营养之间的“系统性信息不对称”是自主代理在食品系统领域面临的独特挑战。

Method: 基于MM-Food-100K数据集构建OmniFood-Bench基准，沿三层递进能力评估VLM：(1)基础感知（食材与烹饪方法识别）；(2)定量推理（份量大小与营养分析）；(3)安全关键建议（针对特定疾病的饮食推荐）。在包括GPT-5.1、Gemini-3-flash、Qwen3-VL-8B等6个SOTA模型上进行评估。

Result: 模型在菜品命名上接近人类水平，但在质量估计上出现灾难性失败，且常对高风险糖尿病用户产生无害但错误的建议幻觉。这种“语义-物理鸿沟”表明当前VLM在安全关键场景中存在严重可信度问题。

Conclusion: 该工作为部署于公共卫生领域的自主代理建立了严格的可信度标准，揭示了VLM在视觉识别与物理定量推理之间存在根本性能力断层，需引起高度重视。

Abstract: The rapid integration of Large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) into critical infrastructure promises to revolutionize
  personalized healthcare and dietary management. However, in the domain of food systems, autonomous agents face a
  unique and persistent challenge: the "Systemic Information Asymmetry" between visual appearance and intrinsic
  nutritional composition. Existing benchmarks primarily focus on coarse-grained classification tasks, such as food
  category recognition, which fail to evaluate the intricate reasoning chain required for real-world dietary management
  -- specifically, the ability to traverse from identifying hidden ingredients to estimating physical mass, and finally
  synthesizing safety-critical medical advice. In this paper, we introduce OmniFood-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark
  constructed from the MM-Food-100K dataset. Unlike previous works, OmniFood-Bench evaluates VLMs across three
  progressive capabilities: Basic Perception (Ingredients & Cooking Methods), Quantitative Reasoning (Portion Size &
  Nutritional Profiling), and Safety-Critical Advisory (Disease-Specific Recommendations). We evaluate six
  state-of-the-art VLMs, including gpt-5.1, gemini-3-flash, and qwen3-vl-8B. Our extensive experiments reveal a
  startling "Semantic-Physical Gap": while models achieve near-human accuracy in naming dishes, they exhibit
  catastrophic failure in mass estimation and frequently hallucinate benign advice for high-risk diabetic profiles. This
  work establishes a rigorous standard for trustworthiness in autonomous agents deployed for public health. The code
  and datasets are available in: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/OmniFood-Bench-7D0B

</details>


### [181] [Applying JEPA-Style Predictive Learning to JA4-Derived Network Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08465)
*Javier Izquierdo,Aygul Zagidullina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索将JEPA（联合嵌入预测架构）的预测性表征学习范式应用于JA4网络指纹，构建了JA4-JEPA模型，在协议家族分类任务上取得了0.9899的余弦相似度和0.9220的kNN准确率，验证了该方法在紧凑网络指纹上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: I-JEPA和V-JEPA在图像与视频领域已证明，通过匹配潜在预测与目标编码器输出（而非重构原始输入）的方式进行自监督学习效果良好。本文旨在探索这一目标函数是否同样适用于紧凑的网络指纹数据，以验证JEPA范式在网络安全领域的泛化能力。

Method: 构建JA4-JEPA，一个基于Transformer的模型，使用来自JA4DB和CIC-IDS-2017数据集的JA4、JA4H、JA4S和JA4X四个子字段进行训练，训练数据约397K样本（但无单一样本同时包含全部四类视图）。评估阶段采用冻结特征后的kNN探针，在TLS、DNS、SSH协议家族分类任务上衡量表征质量。

Result: 在39,416个留出样本上，模型取得了0.9899的余弦相似度和0.9220的kNN分类准确率。结果表明，即使训练数据中存在视图覆盖不完整的问题，JEPA风格的预测学习仍能从JA4衍生指纹中产生有用的嵌入表征。

Conclusion: JEPA风格的预测性表征学习能够成功应用于JA4网络指纹，为其在网络安全领域的自监督学习开辟了新方向，即使在跨数据源的视图覆盖不完全的情况下依然有效。

Abstract: I-JEPA and V-JEPA learn by matching latent predictions to target encoder outputs rather than regenerating the original input, and this has worked well for images and video. We explore whether the same objective works for compact network fingerprints. We built JA4-JEPA, a Transformer-based model trained on JA4, JA4H, JA4S, and JA4X subfields drawn from JA4DB and CIC-IDS- 2017. The training data combines roughly 397K samples from both sources, though no single sample contains all four view families. We evaluated the learned representations with a frozen kNN probe on protocol-family classification across TLS, DNS, and SSH. On 39,416 heldout samples the model achieved a cosine similarity of 0.9899 and a kNN accuracy of 0.9220. These results indicate that JEPA-style predictive learning can produce useful embeddings from JA4-derived fingerprints, even with incomplete view overlap across sources.
  Keywords: JA4, network fingerprinting, JEPA, predictive representation learning, self-supervised learning

</details>


### [182] [Drift-Aware Temporal Graph Rewiring (DATGR) for Adaptive Semantic Modeling in Biomedical Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08490)
*Bharathwaj Vijayakumar,Sahana K. Varadaraju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种漂移感知时序图重连框架（DATGR），通过逻辑更新规则动态调整共现边权重来建模生物医学概念的语义漂移，在无需重新训练嵌入的前提下提升链接预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学语言随新发现快速演化，传统静态嵌入和共现图无法捕捉这种语义变化，导致检索和知识发现任务性能下降。现有方法通常需要为每个时间片重新训练嵌入，计算成本高昂。

Method: 提出DATGR框架：基于估计的语义漂移，采用逻辑更新规则对共现图的边权重进行轻量级、反馈驱动的重连（rewiring），动态更新图结构以反映概念演化，避免为每个时间片重新训练嵌入。

Result: 在BIOMRC数据集上，AUROC均值从静态基线的0.633提升至0.699（绝对提升约0.066），AUPRC保持可比水平（0.738 vs. 0.744）。

Conclusion: 边级自适应策略能有效捕捉演化中的生物医学文本的时序语义变化，在保持计算高效和可解释性的同时，提升链接预测的召回率而不损失精度。

Abstract: Biomedical language evolves rapidly as new discoveries emerge, causing traditional text models to lose semantic fidelity over time. Static embeddings and co-occurrence graphs cannot capture such evolution, leading to performance degradation in retrieval and knowledge discovery tasks. This paper introduces a Drift-Aware Temporal Graph Rewiring (DATGR) framework that models concept evolution by dynamically updating co-occurrence edges based on estimated semantic drift. Instead of retraining embeddings for each time slice, DATGR performs lightweight, feedback-driven rewiring using a logistic update rule applied to edge weights. Evaluated on the Biomedical Multi-Relation Corpus (BIOMRC), the method achieved a mean Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic (AUROC) improvement of approximately 0.066 absolute difference (0.699 vs. 0.633) over a static baseline. Area Under the Precision-Recall Curve (AUPRC) remained comparable (0.738 vs. 0.744), showing that drift-aware adaptation enhances link-prediction recall without a loss in precision. These results demonstrate that edge-level adaptation effectively captures temporal semantic change in evolving biomedical text while remaining computationally efficient and interpretable.

</details>


### [183] [AI-guided stimuli discovery and generation to optimize facial emotion perception studies in autism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08533)
*Kushin Mukherjee,Na Yeon Kim,Maren Wehrheim,Ralph Adolphs,Kohitij Kar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了自闭症与神经典型成人在面部情绪感知上的差异并非均匀分布，而是集中在少数"诊断性"表情中。通过训练群体特异性神经网络模型，研究者能够筛选和生成最大化组间差异的面孔，并在独立样本中验证，从而建立了一个模型引导的框架，用于发现和转化能揭示感知差异的刺激。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于自闭症与神经典型人群在面部情绪感知上的差异研究结果不一致。作者认为这种变异性可能源于刺激材料的稀疏性——即组间差异集中于少数特定表情，而非均匀分布。现有行为检测方法缺乏足够的敏感性、可靠性和机制解释力，需要一种更优化的范式来识别神经分化感知发生分歧或趋同的具体条件。

Method: 研究分为三步：(1) 训练群体特异性人工神经网络模型，预测自闭症和神经典型参与者对每张面孔的情绪判断；(2) 利用这些模型筛选能最大化组间分离的新面孔，并在独立队列中验证模型选择图像与随机匹配图像的行为差异；(3) 结合生成对抗网络(GAN)对"诊断性"图像进行合成变换，使其朝向预测组间一致性更高的方向修改，并在表型匹配验证中检验合成图像是否降低了行为分离。

Result: 在独立队列中，模型选择的图像比随机匹配图像产生了更大的行为差异；通过GAN合成的图像相比原始图像显著降低了行为分离，即自闭症与神经典型参与者的判断趋于一致。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个模型引导的框架，能有效发现和转化揭示群体特异性感知差异的刺激。更重要的是，它表明行为表型分析可以超越传统固定刺激集的平均化方法，走向优化检测范式，精准识别神经分化感知分歧或趋同的条件。

Abstract: Understanding perceptual differences between autistic and neurotypical adults requires behavioral assays that are sensitive, reliable, and mechanistically informative. Facial emotion perception is a useful test case because group differences have been reported, but findings vary across studies. Here we show that this variability may reflect image-level sparsity: autistic-neurotypical differences in emotion judgments were concentrated in a small subset of diagnostic facial expressions rather than spread uniformly across stimuli. We trained population-specific artificial neural network models to predict image-level judgments for autistic and neurotypical participants, then used these models to select novel faces predicted to maximize group separation. In an independent cohort, model-selected images produced larger behavioral differences than matched random images. We then used the same models with a generative adversarial network to transform diagnostic images toward greater predicted group agreement. In phenotype-matched validation, synthesized images reduced behavioral separation relative to their matched originals. These results establish a model-guided framework for discovering and transforming stimuli that reveal population-specific perceptual differences. More broadly, they show how behavioral phenotyping can move beyond averaging across fixed stimulus sets toward optimized assays that identify the conditions under which neurodivergent perception diverges or converges.

</details>


### [184] [CommuniWave:A Machine Learning Model for Quantifying the Degree of Temporary Informal Behavior in Urban Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08554)
*Hongye Yang,Shien Liu,Zhihao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CommuniWave模型，利用机器学习从街景视频中检测和量化城市社区中居民的非正式行为程度(DIB)，通过动态波动图辅助城市管理者进行精细化决策以增强社区韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前社区规划多采用自上而下方式，缺乏有效指标来量化居民的非正式行为，导致规划与居民实际使用之间频繁产生冲突。面对日益复杂的城市环境，亟需一种能够动态监测非正式行为、提升社区韧性的工具。

Method: 提出CommuniWave模型，由三个组件构成：(1) 基于mmaction2的行为捕获网络(BCN)，用于从街景视频中识别居民行为；(2) 自研的YOLOv10改进模型(YLX)，用于目标检测；(3) 基于随机森林的行为评估模型(BEM)，用于量化非正式行为程度(DIB)。最终通过DIB波动图表实现动态监测。

Result: 该模型能够从街景视频中生成DIB波动图表，有效实现动态监测，为城市管理者提供精细化决策支持。

Conclusion: CommuniWave为城市社区的非正式行为量化提供了可行的技术方案，有助于弥合规划意图与实际使用之间的差距，增强社区整体韧性，支撑城市管理者做出更精准的治理决策。

Abstract: For urban managers and designers, improving the functional attributes of urban communities to enhance territorial resilience in the face of complexity and uncertainty is crucial. Currently, community planning often follows a top-down approach and lacks effective metrics to quantify informal behaviors of residents, leading to frequent conflicts with original plans. This study introduces CommuniWave, a machine learning model designed to efficiently detect and quantify the Degree of Informal Behavior (DIB) in urban communities. The model integrates a Behavior Capture Net (BCN) based on mmaction2, a self-developed YOLOv10 model (YLX), and a Behavior Eval Model (BEM) using random forest. Ultimately, by generating DIB fluctuation charts from street videos, the model facilitates dynamic monitoring, supporting urban managers in making refined decisions to enhance the overall resilience of communities.

</details>


### [185] [SHAP-Weighted Cross-Modal Expert Fusion for Emotion and Sentiment Recognition: Evidence and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08573)
*Adis Alihodzic,Selma Skopljakovic Hubljar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了基于XAI引导的自适应融合（XGAF）框架，重点研究SHAP归因归约方式在专家维度不均衡时的影响。实验表明，sum-abs归约能保留高维跨模态专家的总归因质量，在MELD和CMU-MOSEI上接近甚至超越早期融合，且增益主要来自添加跨模态专家而非复杂的逐样本路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感识别中，早期融合（early fusion）准确但结构单一，晚期融合（late fusion）模块化但丢失跨模态交互。现有XGAF方法存在一个问题：当各专家特征维度不均衡时，常用的mean-abs和median-abs归约方式会抑制高维跨模态专家，削弱融合效果。需要系统分析SHAP归约方式、专家维度与跨模态专家设计对模块化融合的影响。

Method: 采用XGAF框架，即基于树的单模态与跨模态专家混合模型，样本级权重由TreeSHAP归因幅值导出。系统对比三种SHAP归约方式：mean-abs（均值绝对值）、median-abs（中位数绝对值）和sum-abs（求和绝对值）。在MELD 7类情绪识别和CMU-MOSEI 3类情感识别数据集上，结合三种人脸序列聚合器（含Transformer变体）进行实验，并通过McNemar检验评估显著性，辅以消融实验和权重分布诊断。

Result: 在MELD上，sum-abs XGAF的Transformer变体达到0.5983加权F1，接近早期融合的0.6018，远超晚期融合的0.4598，McNemar检验显示与早期融合无显著差异（p=1.000），但显著优于晚期融合（p<0.0001）。在CMU-MOSEI上，sum-abs XGAF达到0.6519，略超早期融合的0.6485。消融实验表明主要增益来自添加跨模态专家（尤其是三模态专家），而非复杂路由。诊断显示mean-abs和median-abs权重近乎均匀，而sum-abs权重集中于三模态专家。

Conclusion: SHAP归约方式的选择对维度不均衡的多模态融合至关重要，sum-abs能有效保留高维跨模态专家的贡献，使模块化的XGAF达到与单一早期融合相当的性能，同时保持模块化优势。核心增益源于跨模态专家的引入，而非复杂的自适应路由机制。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion and sentiment recognition is commonly addressed by early fusion, which concatenates modalities before classification, or late fusion, which combines independently trained unimodal predictors. Early fusion can be accurate but monolithic, while late fusion is modular but may lose cross-modal interactions. This paper revisits XAI-guided adaptive fusion (\xgaf), a tree-based mixture of unimodal and cross-modal experts whose sample-level weights are derived from TreeSHAP attribution magnitudes. We focus on the effect of SHAP attribution reduction when experts have unequal feature dimensionalities. In this setting, mean-abs and median-abs reductions can suppress high-dimensional cross-modal experts, whereas sum-abs reduction preserves total attribution mass. On MELD 7-class emotion recognition, sum-abs \xgaf{} nearly matches early fusion across three face-sequence aggregators; the Transformer variant reaches 0.5983 \wf{}, compared with 0.6018 for early fusion and 0.4598 for probability-average late fusion. McNemar testing shows no significant difference between sum-abs \xgaf{} and early fusion on MELD ($p=1.000$), while \xgaf{} remains significantly better than late fusion ($p<0.0001$). On CMU-MOSEI 3-class sentiment recognition, sum-abs \xgaf{} reaches 0.6519 \wf{}, slightly exceeding early fusion (0.6485) and late fusion (0.5696). Ablation studies show that the main gain comes from adding cross-modal experts, especially the trimodal expert, rather than from complex per-sample routing. Diagnostics further show that mean-abs and median-abs weights are nearly uniform, while sum-abs weights concentrate on the trimodal expert. Thus, the main contribution is a transparent empirical analysis of how SHAP reduction, expert dimensionality, and cross-modal expert design affect modular multimodal fusion.

</details>


### [186] [Towards Precision Therapy in Hepatocellular Carcinoma: A Clinical-Reasoning LLM for Risk Stratification and Treatment Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08602)
*Peng Cui,Jitao Wang,Siyan Xue,Yao Huang,Haoming Xia,Dong Li,Dengxiang Liu,Weilin Wang,Liping Liu,Leida Zhang,Yunfu Cui,Tao Peng,Daolin Ji,Haitao Zhao,Wei Zhang,Xiaojuan Wang,Weijie Ma,Zongren Ding,Jinlong Li,Yuan Ding,Jiajing Zhao,Zhiyu Chen,Chengkun Yang,Ziyue Huang,Jiaqi Liu,Fusheng Liu,Yang Zhou,Xiaojuan Wang,Zhongquan Sun,Shiyun Bao,Xiaojun Wang,Ming Yang,Guangxin Li,Bin Shu,Yong Liao,Hongxuan Li,Yao Tang,Shizhong Yang,Yongyi Zeng,Yufeng Yuan,Yinpeng Dong,Jihui Hao,Jun Zhu,Jiahong Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HCC-STAR 是一个临床对齐的大语言模型，通过解读电子病历叙述文本，联合输出基于风险评分的分期、排序的指南一致性治疗建议及个体化生存预测。在12家医院的多中心队列中，该模型在治疗推荐和风险分层方面超越了现行临床指南及GPT-5等竞品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前HCC指南和分期系统仅提供粗粒度分类，常忽略分期内部的异质性和电子病历中的临床上下文信息，导致个体化决策能力不足。

Method: 1. 从SEER数据库收集约30,000例HCC病例，通过临床医生验证的提示增强工作流扩展为EMR风格叙事训练数据；2. 提出知识对齐的推理框架，采用步骤可验证的复合奖励进行优化，超越文本层面的指南背诵；3. 在12家中国医院共6,668例患者的多中心队列上进行验证。

Result: HCC-STAR在治疗推荐和风险分层方面达到最优性能，超越临床指南及GPT-5、Gemini-2.5 Pro。假设性总生存分析显示，遵循HCC-STAR推荐的中位生存为51个月，优于BCLC的29个月和CNLC的32个月。盲法评估中，肝胆专科医生认为其推理和循证依据可信，且模型在治疗准确性上超越住院医师和主治医师，作为辅助工具能帮助医生更快做出更准确的决策。

Conclusion: HCC-STAR是一个可靠且可验证的决策支持系统，可用于HCC的风险分层和精准治疗，具备临床转化的潜力。

Abstract: Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is a common malignancy and a leading cause of cancer-related mortality. Current guidelines and staging systems provide coarse categories, but often miss within-stage heterogeneity and the clinical context in electronic medical records (EMRs). We present HCC-STAR (Hepatocellular Carcinoma Staging, Treatment And pRognosis), a clinically aligned large language model that reads routine EMR narratives and jointly outputs risk score-based staging, ranked guideline-consistent treatments with evidence-based rationales, and individualized survival estimates. We curated about 30,000 HCC cases from SEER and expanded them into EMR-style narrative training data using a clinician-validated, prompt-based augmentation workflow. On this corpus, we developed a knowledge-aligned reasoning framework optimized with a step-verifiable composite reward, moving beyond text-level memorization of clinical guidelines. In a multi-center cohort of 6,668 patients from 12 hospitals in China, HCC-STAR achieved state-of-the-art performance in treatment recommendation and risk stratification compared with clinical guidelines and competitive models, including GPT-5 and Gemini-2.5 Pro. Hypothetical overall-survival analysis showed a median survival of 51 months under adherence to HCC-STAR recommendations, compared with 29 and 32 months under BCLC and CNLC. In clinician-centric evaluations, blinded hepatobiliary specialists rated HCC-STAR's reasoning and evidence-based justifications as trustworthy. The model surpassed resident and attending physicians in treatment accuracy and helped physicians make more accurate decisions faster when used as an assistant. These findings support HCC-STAR as a reliable and verifiable decision-support system for risk stratification and precision therapy in HCC.

</details>


### [187] [The complexities of patient-centred conversational artificial intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08625)
*João Matos,Olivia Buege,Donny Cheung,Gary S. Collins,Paula Dhiman,Nan Li,Bingyu Mao,Benjamin W. Nelson,Michail Ouroutzoglou,Paul Varghese,Jonathan Amar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究分析了真实患者与聊天机器人的对话，发现用户沟通模式差异巨大，据此开发了一个能分别建模临床内容、情绪状态、对话策略和沟通风格的患者模拟器。模拟对话在图灵测试中几乎无法与真实对话区分。研究发现沟通风格显著影响分诊结果，警示若AI系统仅针对理想化交互设计，在实际部署中可能表现不佳并加剧健康不平等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的健康聊天机器人在开发和评估中通常依赖合作性强、表达清晰的模拟患者，这与真实世界中患者沟通模式高度多样化的现状严重脱节。研究者希望揭示真实患者沟通行为的多样性，并量化沟通风格对AI分诊结果的影响。

Method: 首先对2,053段真实患者-聊天机器人对话进行沟通模式和情绪表达分析。随后开发了一个可独立控制临床内容、情绪状态、对话策略和沟通风格四个维度的患者模拟器。通过15名人类评分员参与的图灵测试评估模拟器真实性（准确率55%，接近随机）。最后使用五种不同患者人格，在1,164个经临床医生标注的案例上评估四种LLM的紧急程度分诊表现。

Result: （1）真实患者对话中的沟通模式和情绪表达呈现广泛多样性；（2）模拟对话在图灵测试中，人类评分员辨别准确率仅55%，接近随机水平，表明模拟器高度逼真；（3）沟通风格的差异可显著改变LLM的急诊分诊结果。

Conclusion: 以患者为中心的对聊AI必须适应沟通多样性。若系统仅针对理想化而非现实交互设计，将面临性能下降的风险，并在真实世界部署时可能放大健康不平等。

Abstract: Consumer-facing health chatbots powered by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for symptom assessment. However, chatbot development and evaluation often rely on cooperative, articulate, simulated patients. We analysed 2,053 real patient-chatbot conversations and found that communication patterns and expression of emotions vary widely across users. We developed a patient simulator that separately models clinical content, emotional state, conversational strategy, and communication style. In a Turing-inspired evaluation of realism with 15 human graders, simulated conversations were nearly indistinguishable from real ones, with human graders achieving an accuracy of 55%. We used five distinct patient personae, across 1,164 clinician-graded cases, to evaluate the performance of four LLMs in urgency assessment. We found that communication style can significantly alter triage outcomes. Patient-centred conversational artificial intelligence must accommodate communication diversity: systems designed for idealised, rather than realistic, interactions risk underperforming and amplifying health disparities when deployed in the real world.

</details>


### [188] [Formal Mechanisms for Market Stability in Self-Interested Agent Societies: A Marketplace Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08652)
*Eugene Ng Yi Sheng,Bingquan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过多智能体市场模拟，研究在开放式沟通之上叠加何种形式机制足以维持社会合作。实验比较了八种机制在渐进式恶意注入下的表现，发现调解机制表现最优，并且在经过迭代提示优化的对抗性攻击下，调解机制虽受损但仍保持韧性，市场不会崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自利智能体在重复社会困境中倾向于背叛，导致合作收益瓦解。现有研究缺乏对"在无限制沟通基础上，需要何种正式机制来维持市场稳定"的系统性探索，尤其是这些机制对对抗性攻击的韧性尚不明确。

Method: 构建一个多智能体市场模拟环境：18个DeepSeek-V3大语言模型智能体，各具互补的生产专长，在受限的社交网络中进行交易以获取效用。实验分为两阶段：(1) 在200轮中逐步注入恶意智能体，比较八种机制的表现；(2) 使用迭代提示优化的LLM驱动恶意智能体，对最优机制（调解）进行对抗性红队攻击。

Result: 第一阶段：调解机制在八种条件中表现最优。第二阶段：最佳攻击变体(v6)使诚实智能体效用降低13.3%，但无法使市场崩溃。调解机制即使在持续对抗性压力下仍能恢复市场。

Conclusion: 将对抗鲁棒性定义为机制在优化攻击下维持诚实智能体正效用的能力；调解机制具有鲁棒性——可被弯曲，但不会被折断。

Abstract: Self-interested agents, left unconstrained, tend toward defection in repeated social dilemmas, causing cooperative gains from trade to collapse. This paper investigates what formal mechanisms, layered on top of unrestricted communication, are sufficient for a society of such agents to maintain market stability, and how resilient those mechanisms are to adversarial attack. We instantiate the research question as a multi-agent marketplace simulation where 18 LLM agents (DeepSeek-V3) with complementary production specialties must trade within a constrained social network to obtain utility. We conduct two experimental phases: (1) a mechanism comparison across eight conditions under progressive troll injection over 200 rounds, identifying Mediation as the top-performing mechanism; and (2) adversarial red-teaming of Mediation using iteratively prompt-optimised LLM-driven trolls, finding that the best attack (v6) reduces honest-agent utility by 13.3% but cannot collapse the market. Mediation enables recovery even under sustained adversarial pressure. We define adversarial robustness as a mechanism's ability to sustain positive honest-agent utility under optimised attack, and find that Mediation is robust: it can be bent but not broken.

</details>


### [189] [SolarChain-Eval: A Physics-Constrained Benchmark for Trustworthy Economic Agents in Decentralized Energy Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08681)
*Shilin Ou,Yifan Xu,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SolarChain-Eval，一个物理约束的基准测试，用于评估去中心化能源市场中自主经济代理的可信度，包含 LLM 规划/审计层以增强审计能力，实验揭示了效用与安全性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体 AI 系统被应用于信息物理环境，评估不能仅限于任务性能，还需关注可信度。在去中心化能源市场中，自主代理可能提升市场效用，但也可能利用无效物理数据、制造人工流动性并产生不稳定的治理决策，因此需要一个兼顾物理约束和可信度评估的基准。

Method: 将市场治理建模为 Gymnasium 兼容的马尔可夫决策过程，代理每小时做决策。评估维度包括市场效用、物理安全、滑点、动作平滑性、空间公平性和可审计性。引入 LLM 规划器/审计器层：Planner 定义 episode 级动作边界和审计规则，Auditor 审查并修正高风险动作，所有干预通过结构化日志记录（触发信号、提议动作、修正动作、审计理由）。

Result: 在静态、随机、近视、RL 和 RL+LLM 策略上的实验显示清晰的效用-安全权衡：RL 代理提升市场效用但仍可能产生不安全行为；移除物理惩罚后，奖励最大化代理会利用无效发电量并增加人工流动性；LLM 规划/审计层改善了可审计性并缓解了部分风险，但无法完全弥补错误指定的奖励函数。

Conclusion: 可信的智能体 AI 评估需要同时具备物理约束和透明的干预追踪。仅靠 LLM 层面的后处理不足以纠正根本性的奖励设计缺陷。

Abstract: As agentic AI systems are increasingly applied to cyber-physical environments, their evaluation requires assessment of both task performance and trustworthiness. In decentralized energy markets, autonomous agents may improve market utility, but may also exploit invalid physical data, create artificial liquidity, and produce unstable governance decisions. Therefore, we propose SolarChain-Eval, a physics-constrained benchmark for evaluating trustworthy economic agents. It formulates market governance as a Gymnasium-compatible Markov Decision Process, where agents make hourly decisions. SolarChain-Eval evaluates each policy across multiple dimensions, including market utility, physical safety, slippage, action smoothness, spatial fairness, and auditability. To support agentic evaluation, SolarChain-Eval incorporates an LLM-based Planner/Auditor layer. The Planner defines episode-level action bounds and audit rules, while the Auditor reviews and revises high-risk actions. All interventions are recorded through structured logs, including trigger signals, proposed actions, revised actions, and audit rationales. Experiments with static, random, myopic, RL, and RL+LLM policies reveal a clear utility-safety trade-off. RL agents improve market utility but can still produce unsafe behavior. When the physics penalty is removed, reward-maximizing agents exploit invalid generation and increase artificial liquidity. The LLM Planner/Auditor improves auditability and mitigates selected risks, but it cannot fully compensate for a misspecified reward function. These results indicate that trustworthy agentic AI evaluation requires both physical constraints and transparent intervention traces. We release data and code as open access on GitHub for replicability.

</details>


### [190] [Remember When It Matters: Proactive Memory Agent for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08716)
*Yifan Wu,Lizhu Zhang,Yuhang Zhou,Mingyi Wang,Bo Peng,Serena Li,Xiangjun Fan,Zhuokai Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对长程任务中"行为状态衰减"问题，提出一个独立运行的记忆智能体，以主动干预而非被动检索的方式，在关键时机向动作智能体注入结构化记忆提醒，在多个基准上显著提升任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程任务中，决策相关状态随轨迹增长而分散、被掩埋甚至移出上下文窗口，导致"行为状态衰减"——既有任务要求、环境事实、过往尝试、诊断信息和开放子目标无法在需要时影响决策。现有记忆机制多为被动检索，缺乏主动干预能力。

Method: 提出一个独立于动作智能体的记忆智能体（Memory Agent），与未修改的动作智能体并行运行。该记忆智能体从近期轨迹中持续更新结构化记忆库（memory bank），并自主决定"注入基于记忆的提醒"或"保持沉默"。模块为即插即用设计，兼容主流动作智能体和现有智能体框架。同时，在SETA数据集上使用SFT和GRPO训练Qwen3.5-27B，探索开放权重的记忆策略。

Result: 在Terminal-Bench 2.0上pass@1提升+8.3个百分点，在τ²-Bench上提升+6.8个百分点，对强弱不同的动作智能体均有效。消融实验表明：选择性干预优于被动暴露记忆库、始终注入、仅顾问指导和通用检索。训练得到的记忆策略在验证奖励上有提升，并部分迁移至Terminal-Bench。

Conclusion: 主动记忆干预（选择性提醒）是解决长程任务行为状态衰减的有效范式。即插即用的记忆智能体设计具有广泛的兼容性和实用性，且训练开放权重记忆策略具有可行性，为未来研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: In long-horizon tasks, decision-relevant state is often scattered across an expanding trajectory, while the action agent must surface it and act. As trajectories grow, task requirements, environment facts, prior attempts, diagnoses, and open subgoals can be buried in the context window or pushed beyond it, failing to influence decisions when needed. We call this failure mode "behavioral state decay". We study memory as an active intervention mechanism rather than passive retrieval. A separate memory agent runs alongside an unmodified action agent, updating a structured memory bank from the recent trajectory and deciding whether to inject a memory-grounded reminder or remain silent. The module is plug-and-play with frontier action agents and existing agent harnesses. Across Terminal-Bench 2.0 and $τ^2$-Bench, it improves pass@1 for both weaker and stronger action agents, with gains of +8.3 pp on Terminal-Bench and +6.8 pp on $τ^2$-Bench. Ablations show that selective intervention outperforms passive bank exposure, always-on injection, advisor-only guidance, and general retrieval. As an early step toward open-weight memory policies, we train Qwen3.5-27B on SETA using SFT and GRPO, improving validation reward and achieving partial transfer to Terminal-Bench.

</details>


### [191] [The Illusion of Equivalency: Statistical Characterization of Quantization Effects in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08734)
*Baha Rababah,Cuneyt Gurcan Akcora,Carson K. Leung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 量化模型即便在困惑度/准确率不变的条件下，其预测行为已与原始模型出现显著分歧；论文提出"正确性一致性"指标并揭示注意力层中 Query/Key 投影对量化更敏感的规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化评估仅依赖准确率和困惑度，掩盖了量化引发的模型行为变化，缺乏从决策层面衡量模型一致性的手段。

Method: 提出"正确性一致性"（correctness agreement）指标：测量基座模型与其量化变体在正确预测上的重叠比例；同时将量化建模为注意力权重的结构算子，使用统计与分布度量逐层分析失真，定位 Query、Key、Value、Output 投影的敏感性差异。

Result: 中等量化强度下即出现行为分歧，即使任务性能看似保持；低比特宽度出现非线性断裂点；Query 和 Key 投影对量化一致性地比 Value 和 Output 投影更敏感。

Conclusion: 基座模型与量化模型之间存在"等效幻觉"，亟需引入行为层面的评估指标以补充传统性能指标。

Abstract: Post-training quantization is widely used to deploy large language models in resource-constrained settings, yet its evaluation relies almost exclusively on accuracy and perplexity. We show that these metrics fail to capture behavioral changes induced by quantization. We introduce correctness agreement, a decision-level metric that measures overlap in correct predictions between a base model and its quantized variants, independent of absolute accuracy. Across multiple models and quantization schemes from 8-bit to 2-bit, we find that behavioral divergence emerges under moderate quantization even when task performance appears preserved. To explain this effect, we analyze quantization as a structural operator on attention weights and quantify layer-wise distortions using statistical and distributional measures. Our results reveal non-linear breakpoints at low bit-widths and show that query and key projections are consistently more sensitive than value and output projections. These findings expose an illusion of equivalence between base and quantized models and motivate behavioral evaluation beyond conventional performance metrics.

</details>


### [192] [Workflow as Knowledge: Semantic Persistence for LLM-Mediated Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08740)
*Emanuele Quinto,Carlo Andrea Rozzi,Francesco Zanitti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受Lisp启发但与语言无关的概念模型，将工作流定义、实例、推理记录、上下文快照及依赖关系统一表示为持久化知识对象，核心语义区分在于derive（确定性计算）与infer（中介LLM判断），从而实现语义持久性——工作流本身成为可审查、可恢复、可复阅的知识对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM应用日益依赖显式工作流（工具调用、检索、分支、检查点、人工审批），现有工作流系统虽已解决诸多执行问题，但缺乏统一的概念框架来理解工作流产物的语义本质与持久化表示。

Method: 提出以符号形式、对象同一性和"活映像思维"（live-image thinking）为解释透镜的概念模型。将工作流定义、实例、推理记录、上下文快照和依赖关系统一建模为共享知识基底中的持久化知识对象。核心语义区分derive（基于已有状态的确定性计算）与infer（在声明上下文和执行器能力策略下的中介LLM判断）。

Result: 给出语义持久性的初步概念性说明：工作流不仅产生知识并留下痕迹，其本身也可被表示为可审查、可恢复、可复阅的知识对象。形式化迁移语义仍有待未来工作。

Conclusion: 该概念模型为LLM工作流提供了一种将过程性执行与声明性知识统一表示的新视角，但形式化语义的完备性仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) applications increasingly use explicit workflows for tool use, retrieval, branching, checkpointing, and human approval. Existing workflow systems already address many execution concerns. This paper proposes a Lisp-inspired but language-independent conceptual model: symbolic forms, object identity, and live-image thinking are used as explanatory lenses, not implementation commitments. In this model, workflow definitions, workflow instances, inference records, context snapshots, and dependency relations are represented as persistent knowledge objects in a shared knowledge substrate. Its central semantic distinction is between derive and infer: derive is deterministic computation over available state; infer is mediated LLM judgment under declared context and executor-controlled capability policy.
  The result is a preliminary conceptual account of semantic persistence: workflows do not merely produce knowledge and leave traces, but can themselves be represented as inspectable, resumable, and reviewable knowledge objects, while formal transition semantics remain future work.

</details>


### [193] [AUTOPILOT VQA: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models for Incident-Centric Dashcam Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08745)
*Siddharth Damodharan,Radhika Gupta,Ali Alshami,Ryan Rabinowitz,Jugal Kalita*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AUTOPILOT-VQA 是一个面向行车记录仪视频理解的事件中心化视觉问答基准，旨在评估视觉语言模型在安全关键驾驶事件上的时序推理能力，覆盖天气、道路、实体、事故可避免性等多维度，已作为 CVPR 2026 竞赛的一部分发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 VLM/LLM/MLLM 在自动驾驶任务上进展显著，但缺乏有效手段评估这些模型能否对安全关键事件进行可靠推理，尤其是在时序维度和可避免性判断方面存在评估空白。

Method: 构建了针对真实驾驶事件和近失事件的 VQA 数据集，设计结构化问题，覆盖天气与光照、交通环境、道路布局、路面状态、标志标线、涉事实体、事故发生情况、撞击位置以及可避免性推理等安全相关类别，从场景属性到事件级细节逐层考察模型的时序感知与安全推理。

Result: 该基准已作为 AUTOPILOT CVPR 2026 竞赛的组成部分发布，为不同场景下自动驾驶系统可靠性评估提供了标准化平台。

Conclusion: AUTOPILOT-VQA 推动了视觉语言系统从物体识别走向时序扎根、安全感知的推理，有助于开发更具可解释性、鲁棒性和安全意识的实际自动驾驶视觉语言系统。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models, Large Language Models, and Multimodal Large Language Models have improved autonomous driving tasks such as scene understanding, decision making, trajectory prediction, and visual question answering. However, evaluating whether these models can reliably reason about safety-critical incidents remains challenging. To address this gap, we present AUTOPILOT-VQA, an incident-centric visual question answering benchmark for dashcam video understanding. The dataset evaluates different systems through structured questions designed around real-world driving incidents and near-incidents. The benchmark covers diverse safety-relevant categories, including weather and lighting conditions, traffic environment, road layout, road surface state, signage, involved entities, accident occurrence, impact location, and avoidability-related reasoning. By requiring models to answer grounded questions about both contextual scene properties and event-level incident details, AUTOPILOT-VQA moves beyond object recognition toward temporally grounded, safety-aware reasoning. The dataset is released as part of the AUTOPILOT CVPR 2026 competition and provides a standardized benchmark for assessing the reliability of autonomous driving systems in different scenarios. Our benchmark support developments for more interpretable, robust, and safety-conscious vision-language systems for real-world autonomous driving.

</details>


### [194] [Using AI-based Learning Assistants in Higher Education: A Large-Scale Descriptive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08748)
*Kristina Schaaff,Quintus Stierstorfer,Valerie Heckel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究对77,543名远程学习学生使用AI学习助手Syntea的客观日志数据进行了大规模描述性分析，揭示了不同性别、年龄组、学科集群、学位层次和学习模式下的使用模式差异，填补了教育聊天机器人大规模实际使用行为研究的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于教育聊天机器人的研究大多依赖较小样本和自我报告的调查数据，缺乏基于客观行为日志的大规模实证证据。作者旨在通过大规模数据填补这一空白，了解AI学习助手在高等教育中的实际使用情况。

Method: 基于77,543名远程学习学生使用Syntea（AI学习助手）的客观日志数据，按性别、年龄组、学科集群、学位层次和学习模式等维度进行大规模描述性统计分析，考察不同群体间的使用模式差异。

Result: 研究发现Syntea已被许多学习者融入日常学习流程，但使用模式在人口统计学和结构性背景（性别、年龄、学科、学位、学习模式）上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 该研究为AI学习支持的进一步开发提供了实证基础，并为高等教育中教育聊天机器人的大规模使用行为分析做出了重要贡献。

Abstract: In this study, we present a large-scale descriptive analysis of the use of an AI-based learning assistant (Syntea) in higher education. Based on objective log data from 77,543 students enrolled in distance studies, we examine usage patterns across gender, age group, study cluster, degree, and study mode. To date, existing research on educational chatbots has largely relied on comparatively small samples and self-reported survey data, while large-scale evidence on actual usage behavior remains limited. Our findings show that Syntea is already embedded in the study routines of many learners, but that usage differs across demographic and structural contexts. By identifying these patterns, our study provides an empirical basis for the further development of AI-based learning support and contributes a large-scale analysis of educational chatbot usage in higher education.

</details>


### [195] [Ideas Have Genomes: Benchmarking Scientific Lineage Reasoning and Lineage-Grounded Idea Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08758)
*Yifan Zhou,Qihao Yang,Yan Li,Donggang Li,Xiru Hu,Hokin Deng,Ziyang Gong,Xuanyi Zhou,Huacan Wang,Xiangchao Yan,Wanghan Xu,Wenlong Zhang,Shaofeng Zhang,Yue Zhou,Yifan Yang,Zhihang Zhong,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 IdeaGene-Bench (IG-Bench)，一个评估 AI 系统科学谱系推理与谱系条件下想法生成能力的基准。包含 IG-Exam（42 种任务类型，1029 个实例，测试封闭式谱系推理）和 IG-Arena（基于种群进化评分 PES 评估生成质量）。14 个 LLM 系统实验显示最强系统谱系推理精确率仅 27.3%，谱系上下文会重塑系统排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学思想并非凭空产生，而是继承、修补、重组前人工作，类似生物基因组。现有基准无法评估 AI 系统是否理解并遵循这种"科学谱系"结构，即思想之间的继承、突变、损失、外部引入和新插入等演化关系。

Method: 提出 IdeaGene 框架：将每篇论文或提案表示为最小化、类型化、证据锚定的 Idea Genome 对象集合；GenomeDiff 记录对象间的继承、突变、损失、外部导入和新插入等六种演化动态。构建 IG-Bench，包含 1961 条黄金谱系轨迹、1085 个 Idea Genome 对象、920 对 GenomeDiff 记录，覆盖 10 个科学领域。设计两类评估：(1) IG-Exam：42 种任务类型、1029 个实例，测试 Idea Genome 抽象、继承追踪、演化推理和谱系验证；(2) IG-Arena：用谱系条件种群进化评分（PES）评估生成，考察提案是否能作为给定谱系种群的连贯后裔——继承正确的 Idea Genome 对象、与邻近工作产生有意义变异、并为未来研究提供选择价值。

Result: 14 个基于 LLM 的科学系统测试显示存在组合瓶颈：最强系统在谱系推理上的精确准确率仅 27.3%；结构化的谱系上下文会重新洗牌系统排名，而非均匀地帮助所有参与者。

Conclusion: 当前 LLM 在科学谱系推理方面存在显著的组合瓶颈，理解科学思想的演化继承关系仍然是一个开放性挑战；IG-Bench 为评估和推进 AI 系统的科学谱系理解能力提供了标准化测试平台。

Abstract: Scientific ideas rarely start from a blank page. They inherit mechanisms, repair known limitations, and recombine pieces of earlier work, much like biological genomes. Current benchmarks still say little about whether AI systems can follow this inheritance structure. We present IdeaGene-Bench (IG-Bench), a benchmark for scientific lineage reasoning and lineage-grounded idea generation. IG-Bench is organized around the IdeaGene framework: each paper or proposal is represented as a set of minimal, typed, evidence-grounded Idea Genome objects, and a GenomeDiff aligns these objects to record inheritance, mutation, loss, external import, and novel insertion under six operational evolutionary dynamics. The benchmark contains 1,961 golden lineage traces, 1,085 curated Idea Genome objects, and 920 pairwise GenomeDiff records across 10 scientific domains. It supports two evaluations. IG-Exam (42 task types, 1,029 instances) tests closed-form lineage reasoning across Idea Genome abstraction, inheritance tracing, evolutionary reasoning, and lineage verification. IG-Arena evaluates generation with a lineage-conditioned Population-Evolution Score(PES), asking whether a proposal can be inserted as a coherent descendant of a given lineage population: it should inherit the right Idea Genome objects, vary meaningfully from nearby work, and offer selection value for future research. Experiments on 14 LLM-based scientists expose a compositional bottleneck. The strongest system reaches only 27.3% exact accuracy on lineage reasoning, and structured lineage context reshuffles system rankings rather than helping every participant uniformly.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Towards the Explainability of Temporal Graph Networks via Memory Backtracking and Topological Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07716)
*Yazheng Liu,Xi Zhang,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向时序图网络（TGN）的解释方法MemExplainer，通过拓扑归因树和记忆回溯树来量化历史事件对模型预测的贡献，并设计了基于LRP的贡献分配机制和优化目标以识别关键事件，在九种数据集和三类任务上验证了其解释的忠实性和优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TGN解释方法忽略了记忆模块（memory module）——该模块是TGN记录和更新节点历史的核心组件，导致无法追溯历史事件对预测的影响。因此需要一种能够深入记忆模块、量化历史事件贡献的解释方法，以增强TGN的可信度。

Method: 提出MemExplainer，包含三个核心设计：（1）拓扑归因树（topology attribution tree），捕获邻居节点及其记忆向量对预测的影响；（2）记忆回溯树（memory backtracking tree），进一步量化历史事件如何塑造节点记忆向量；（3）在TGN中应用层次化相关性传播（LRP），确保所有事件的贡献之和等于模型输出的logits。此外，针对top-k选择因logits到概率的非线性映射可能不忠实的问题，设计了专门的优化目标来识别真正重要的事件。

Result: 在9个时序图数据集上进行了实验，涵盖节点属性预测、链路预测和图分类三类任务。结果表明，MemExplainer提供了忠实的解释（fidelity），并且在所有任务上均优于现有的SOTA基线方法。

Conclusion: MemExplainer通过显式建模TGN中的记忆模块，首次实现了对历史事件影响的完整追溯，其基于LRP的贡献分配保证了数学一致性，优化目标进一步提升了关键事件识别的忠实性，在多种任务和数据集上均取得最优性能。

Abstract: Temporal graphs are ubiquitous in real-world applications and Temporal Graph Networks (TGNs) have achieved superior predictive accuracy. Understanding which historical events drive model predictions can enhance trustworthiness of TGNs. Existing explanation methods overlook the memory module, the core component that records and updates node histories, leaving the influence of past events unexplored. To address this, we attribute TGNs predictions through the topology attribution tree and memory backtracking tree. The topology attribution tree captures the influence of neighbors and their memory vectors, then the memory backtracking tree quantifies how historical events shape node memory vectors. We apply the LRP in TGNs, ensuring that the total contribution of events equals the logits of model. Finally, top-k selection may be unfaithful due to the nonlinear mapping from logits to probabilities, we design optimization objectives to identify the important events. Experiments on nine temporal graph datasets, spanning node property prediction, link prediction tasks and graph classification tasks, show that our method provides faithful explanations and outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/yazhengliu/MemExplainer

</details>


### [197] [Who Gets Missed in the Tail? Thresholded Subgroup Underdiagnosis in Long-Tailed Chest X-ray Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07717)
*Ha-Hieu Pham,Hai-Dang Nguyen,Dang P. M. Cao,Thanh-Huy Nguyen,Min Xu,Trung-Nghia Le,Ulas Bagci,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对CXR分类中罕见阳性患者被遗漏的公平性问题进行了审计研究，发现罕见标签的公平性联合取决于发现类型、子群体和操作阈值，而非仅依赖于标签频率或排序指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在胸部X光分类中，即使整体排序性能可接受，罕见阳性患者（尤其是子群体中的患者）仍可能被遗漏在决策阈值之下。作者将此框定为部署前公平性审计问题：当长尾多标签CXR模型从分数转换为二元决策时，谁被漏掉了？

Method: 使用"诊断阶梯"框架，在VinDr-CXR和MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT两个数据集上，系统分离并比较四个层次的干预：类别级长尾损失处理、子群体感知加权、群体鲁棒性（GroupDRO）以及阈值选择。核心方法包括分组尾部加权、尾部感知阈值调整，并通过配对bootstrap对比和GroupDRO参考实验进行统计验证。

Result: 在VinDr-CXR上，分组尾部加权结合尾部感知阈值将尾部假阴性率（FNR）从0.665降至0.269，性别最差组FNR从0.705降至0.157，年龄最差组FNR从0.822降至0.133，同时宏观mAP从0.611提升至0.635。在MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT上，同样方法将尾部FNR从0.866降至0.741，并降低了性别、年龄、种族和保险等各子群体的最差组FNR，但残留漏检率仍然较高。GroupDRO实验表明，仅靠聚合群体鲁棒性无法消除罕见子群体的漏检。

Conclusion: 研究支持一个狭窄的审计结论：CXR中罕见标签的公平性并非由标签频率或排序指标单独决定，而是联合依赖于具体发现类型、子群体和操作阈值的选择。

Abstract: In chest X-ray (CXR) classification, acceptable ranking performance can still leave rare-positive patients below threshold, especially within subgroups. We study this pre-deployment fairness problem as an audit question: after a long-tailed multi-label CXR model is converted from scores into decisions, who is missed? Across VinDr-CXR and MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT, we use a diagnostic ladder to separate class-level long-tail losses, subgroup-aware weighting, group robustness, and threshold selection. On VinDr-CXR, group-tail weighting followed by tail-aware thresholding reduces tail FNR from 0.665 to 0.269, sex worst-group FNR from 0.705 to 0.157, and age worst-group FNR from 0.822 to 0.133, while macro-mAP increases from 0.611 to 0.635. On MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT, the same score-to-threshold comparison reduces tail FNR from 0.866 to 0.741 and lowers worst-group FNR across sex, age, race, and insurance; residual missed-positive rates nevertheless remain high. Paired bootstrap contrasts on VinDr support the thresholded FNR reductions, and GroupDRO reference runs indicate that aggregate group robustness alone does not remove rare subgroup misses in this setting. The study supports a narrow audit claim: rare-label fairness in CXR depends jointly on the finding, subgroup, and operating threshold, not on label frequency or ranking metrics alone.

</details>


### [198] [LLT: Local Linear Transformer for PDE Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07718)
*Oded Ovadia,Eli Turkel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出局部线性Transformer（LLT），一种结合线性全局注意力与局部空间混合的神经算子架构，用于学习PDE解映射。LLT在多种PDE问题上达到竞争性的精度，同时训练速度比Transolver快1.8至2.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制在应用于PDE时存在两大局限：(1) 计算复杂度随计算节点数呈二次方增长；(2) 缺乏对局部相互作用的显式偏置。现有Transformer神经算子未能同时高效解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出LLT架构，核心设计包括：(1) 线性全局注意力（linear global attention）降低计算复杂度；(2) 局部空间混合（local spatial mixing）引入局部相互作用偏置；(3) 融合坐标与几何信息，使其能处理结构化和非结构化网格。在三类离散化（有限元、有限体积、有限差分）及多种网格类型上评估。

Result: LLT在弹性力学、塑性力学、翼型绕流、管道流和Darcy流等PDE问题上，相对L₂误差与主流基线持平或更低。在匹配的结构化离散化上，每次训练迭代的时钟时间相比Transolver减少1.8至2.5倍。方法成功扩展到具有32,186个非结构化网格点的三维汽车空气动力学数据集。

Conclusion: LLT是一种精确且计算高效的PDE神经算子，在不同离散化方法、网格类型和问题设置下均表现良好，展示了其在科学计算与工程仿真中的实用潜力。

Abstract: Neural operators have become a common approach for learning PDE solution maps and accelerating numerical simulations. Transformer-based neural operators are of particular interest, since attention can learn long-range dependencies in the computational domain. However, standard attention has two major limitations when applied to PDEs: it scales quadratically with the number of computational nodes, and it lacks an explicit bias toward local interactions. To address these issues, we introduce Local Linear Transformer (LLT) for PDE operator learning. The architecture combines linear global attention with local spatial mixing, and incorporates coordinate and geometry information. We evaluate LLT on several PDE problems, including elasticity, plasticity, airfoil flow, pipe flow, and Darcy flow. The reference data for these problems span finite-element, finite-volume, and finite-difference discretizations on structured and unstructured meshes. Compared with other neural-operator and transformer baselines from prior studies, LLT achieves competitive or lower relative $L_2$ error across these problems. On matched structured discretizations, wall-clock time per training iteration is reduced by factors of 1.8 to 2.5 relative to Transolver. We also scale the approach and apply it to a three-dimensional car aerodynamics dataset with 32,186 unstructured mesh points per sample. Together, these results indicate that LLT provides an accurate and computationally efficient operator for PDE problems across discretizations, mesh types, and problem settings.

</details>


### [199] [ReCoLoRA: Spectrum-Aware Recursive Consolidation for Continual LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07719)
*Wentao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReCoLoRA 提出一种频谱感知的持续微调框架，通过对预训练权重进行随机SVD初始化适配器、基于肘部准则选择有效秩、递归重分解当前有效权重（而非原始权重），使每个新任务从已吸收前序任务的模型出发，避免LoRA式持续微调中的灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA风格方法在持续微调任务序列时，在相同的冻结权重上不断堆叠低秩更新，导致新任务覆盖旧任务（灾难性遗忘）。需要一种能让模型在持续学习新任务时保持旧任务知识的方法。

Method: ReCoLoRA包含三个核心设计：(1) 从预训练权重的随机化SVD初始化适配器；(2) 基于肘部准则（elbow criterion）为每层自动选择有效秩；(3) 在每个新任务前，递归重分解当前有效权重为冻结残差、缓慢更新的主成分和全新适配器三部分，实现"递归整合"，使每个任务都从一个已融合前序任务知识的模型出发。

Result: 在4个7-8B参数骨干模型上的六任务持续GLUE序列实验中，ReCoLoRA在三个骨干模型上取得了最佳最终平均分数，超越秩扫描的LoRA、PiSSA、AdaLoRA和DoRA基线，且训练参数更少；同时提供了oracle路由的任务库变体作为完全任务隔离情况下的上界。

Conclusion: ReCoLoRA通过递归整合低秩适配器的频谱感知策略，有效缓解了持续微调中的灾难性遗忘问题，在多个骨干模型和GLUE任务上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning adapts a large language model to one task cheaply, but across a task sequence LoRA-style methods keep stacking low-rank updates on the same frozen weight, so each new task tends to overwrite the previous ones. We present ReCoLoRA (Recursive Consolidation of Low-Rank Adapters), a spectrum-aware framework for continual fine-tuning: adapters are initialized from a randomized SVD of the pretrained weight, per-layer effective ranks are selected by an elbow criterion, and the principal subspace is adapted before residual capacity is opened. Before each new task, ReCoLoRA re-decomposes the current effective weight, rather than the original one, into a frozen residual, a slowly updated principal component, and a fresh adapter (recursive consolidation), so every task starts from the model that has already absorbed its predecessors. On a six-task continual GLUE sequence over four 7-8B backbones, ReCoLoRA attains the best final average score on three of the four backbones against rank-swept LoRA, PiSSA, AdaLoRA, and DoRA baselines while training fewer parameters; an oracle-routed task-bank variant serves as an upper bound under full task isolation. Code: https://github.com/bhqy666/ReCoLoRA.

</details>


### [200] [Omni-Sleep: A Sleep Foundation Model via Hierarchical Contrastive Learning of CNS--ANS Dynamic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07720)
*Zhoujie Hou,Song Wang,Kexin Lou,Mo Wang,Chen Wei,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Omni-Sleep 是一个睡眠基础模型，以 CNS/ANS 生理分区为先验，通过拓扑约束的表征学习来融合多模态 PSG 信号，在睡眠分期和多疾病分类上优于现有基准模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有睡眠基础模型在融合 EEG、EOG、EMG、ECG、呼吸等多模态信号时，通常忽略其生理组织架构，以拓扑无关的方式融合异构生物信号，未能充分利用中枢神经系统与自主神经系统的层次化生理结构。

Method: 提出 Omni-Sleep 模型，核心包含三个学习目标：(1) 系统内一致性，捕捉神经信号和心肺信号各自子系统内的共享因子；(2) 系统间同步，对齐子系统轨迹以建模脑-体动态；(3) 潜空间掩码时序建模，捕捉长程睡眠动态。在超过 10 万小时的多中心多模态 PSG 数据上预训练。

Result: 在睡眠分期和多疾病分类任务上，Omni-Sleep 在多个数据集和模态消融设置下均优于强基线模型，展现出更好的标签效率、跨数据集泛化能力和对缺失模态的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 生理层次结构（CNS/ANS 分区）作为先验能够有效提升睡眠表征学习的泛化能力，Omni-Sleep 验证了拓扑约束表征学习在多模态睡眠分析中的价值。

Abstract: Sleep physiology arises from the coordinated dynamics of the central nervous system (CNS) and autonomic nervous system (ANS), as reflected by multimodal polysomnography signals including EEG, EOG, EMG, ECG, and respiration. However, existing sleep foundation models often fuse heterogeneous biosignals in a topology-agnostic manner, overlooking their physiological organization. We introduce Omni-Sleep, a sleep foundation model that uses the CNS/ANS partition as a physiological prior for topology-constrained representation learning. Omni-Sleep learns structured representations through three objectives: intra-system consistency, which captures shared subsystem-level factors within neural and cardio-respiratory signals; inter-system synchronization, which aligns subsystem trajectories to model brain--body dynamics; and latent-space masked temporal modeling, which captures long-horizon sleep dynamics. Pre-trained on over 100,000 hours of multi-center multimodal PSG data, Omni-Sleep is evaluated on sleep staging and multi-disease classification. Across datasets and modality-ablation settings, Omni-Sleep outperforms strong foundation-model baselines, showing improved label efficiency, cross-dataset generalization, and robustness to missing modalities. These results highlight the value of physiological hierarchy for generalizable sleep representation learning. Code is available at https://github.com/AutoBrain-sleep/OmniSleep.

</details>


### [201] [Uncertainty-gated selection for block-sparse attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07724)
*Thomas Rossi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对长上下文语言模型中块稀疏注意力机制的"近视"问题，提出一种信息价值路由器(Value-of-Information Router)，通过衡量top-k截断的决策裕度，对模糊查询动态加倍保留块集合，显著提升召回率并逼近密集注意力精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有块稀疏注意力通过per-query top-k选择来降低复杂度，但该截断策略是"近视"的：当第k和第k+1个块的得分接近时，模型仅凭固定预算做出硬性选择，被丢弃的块可能包含关键答案证据且无法在下游恢复，导致信息不可逆丢失。

Method: 提出一种信息价值路由器，对每个查询测量top-k截断决策的"决断性"——即第k和第k+1块之间的得分差距。对于该差距最小的查询，将其保留块集合加倍。该方法与骨干架构无关，可叠加于现有块评分方法（如Quest）之上，并实现了融合选择与核函数的流水线。

Result: 在LongBench-v2 medium子集(n=215)上，Router-on-Quest的成对召回率达0.75，对比top-k基线的0.47提升28个百分点(McNemar p<0.01)；在RULER NIAH multikey任务上与密集注意力差距仅2pp。在四个模型、三种架构(Qwen2.5, Mistral-Nemo, Qwen3.6)上均复现提升。128K上下文下，Qwen2.5-7B-1M和Qwen3.6分别保留密集精度的0.81和0.89（而SSA式top-k在前者仅0.09），融合流水线运行时间分别为密集的0.62x和0.80x。

Conclusion: 信息价值路由器通过最小的额外计算开销，有效弥补了块稀疏注意力硬截断导致的信息丢失，在多个模型和架构上一致显著提升长上下文检索精度，同时保持良好的推理效率，是一种轻量且通用的改进方案。

Abstract: Block-sparse attention scales long-context language models by replacing the O(N^2) softmax with a per-query top-k selection over key blocks. This cutoff is myopic: when the k-th and (k+1)-th blocks are nearly tied in score, the selector commits without spending extra budget, and a dropped block carrying answer evidence is unrecoverable downstream. We propose a value-of-information router that measures, for each query, how decisively the top-k cut was made, and doubles the kept set for the queries where that gap is smallest; the rule is backbone-agnostic and stacks with existing block-scoring methods such as Quest. On LongBench-v2 medium at n=215 (the entire dataset subset), router-on-Quest reaches paired recall 0.75 vs. top-k 0.47 -- +28 pp over the SSA-style baseline (McNemar p<0.01) -- and lands within 2 pp of dense on RULER NIAH multikey at the same context. The lift reproduces on four models from three architectures (Qwen2.5, Mistral-Nemo, Qwen3.6). At 128K, the router preserves 0.81 and 0.89 of dense accuracy on Qwen2.5-7B-1M and Qwen3.6 (vs. SSA-style top-k at 0.09 on the former) while the fused selection-plus-kernel pipeline runs at 0.62x and 0.80x dense wall time.

</details>


### [202] [SHIFT: Survival Prediction from Incomplete and Heterogeneous Genomic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07725)
*Muhammet Sami Yavuz,Ayhan Can Erdur,Sabri Mustafa Kahya,Benedikt Wiestler,Jana Lipkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SHIFT是一个基于Transformer的生存预测模型，能够在基因组特征存在结构性缺失的情况下直接预测，无需测试时填补，并通过可变率特征掩码训练增强对异质性缺失模式的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因组预测模型在不同机构间迁移时，由于测序面板差异导致结构性特征缺失，现有方法（共享基因子集、排除不完整患者、测试时填补）均会降低鲁棒性并限制多中心数据的利用。

Method: 提出SHIFT模型：将每个基因组特征独立表示，利用掩码自注意力机制和特征可用性掩码，使预测仅基于观测到的输入；同时引入可变率特征掩码训练策略以应对异质性缺失模式。

Result: 在胶质母细胞瘤和肺鳞状细胞癌上进行多队列外部验证，包括严重的跨队列面板不匹配场景。SHIFT展现出强泛化能力，优于标准生存基线和基于填补的方法，且能使用单一模型处理不同特征集。不完全队列患者参与训练可提升外部数据表现。

Conclusion: 缺失感知建模是精准肿瘤学中实现多中心生存预测的实用策略，部分观测队列不应被排除在模型构建之外。

Abstract: Genomic prediction models often fail to transfer across institutions because sequencing panels differ across sites, creating structural feature missingness at deployment. Existing approaches to this challenge typically restrict analysis to genes shared across cohorts, exclude patients with incomplete profiles, or rely on test-time imputation, all of which can reduce robustness and limit the use of multi-center data. We propose Survival prediction Handling Incomplete Features using Transformer (SHIFT), a missingness-aware survival model that directly predicts from incomplete genomic inputs without test-time imputation. SHIFT represents each genomic feature separately and uses masked self-attention, along with a feature-availability mask, so that predictions are based only on observed inputs. Further, we introduce variable-rate feature masking during training to improve robustness to heterogeneous missingness patterns. We evaluate the approach on glioblastoma and lung squamous cell carcinoma with external validation across multiple cohorts, including a challenging setting with severe cross-cohort panel mismatch. Across these settings, SHIFT shows strong generalization and compares favorably with standard survival baselines and imputation-based approaches, while using a single model across differing feature sets. We also find that incorporating patients from incomplete cohorts during development can improve performance on external data, suggesting that partially observed cohorts need not be excluded from model building. These results support missingness-aware modeling as a practical strategy for multi-center survival prediction in precision oncology.

</details>


### [203] [Jet-Long: Efficient Long-Context Extension with Dynamic Bifocal RoPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07740)
*Haozhan Tang,Zerui Wang,Yuxian Gu,Song Han,Han Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Jet-Long 提出一种无需微调的零样本长上下文扩展方法，通过双焦点注意力机制（局部忠实于 RoPE 的窗口 + 动态缩放因子的远程窗口）配合 inclusion-exclusion 注意力融合与即时 RoPE 校正旋转，在 H100 上达到 FA2 的 1.39× 吞吐量，在 RULER、HELMET-RAG 和困惑度等基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本上下文扩展方法在部署时固定单一缩放因子，激进缩放牺牲短上下文保真度，保守缩放在长上下文时失效。实际应用中（RAG、代码仓库级任务、Agent 工作流）输入常远超预训练窗口，迫切需要一种能同时在短上下文保持精确、在长上下文干净外推的方法。

Method: 提出 Jet-Long：一种双焦点（bifocal）零样本方法。(1) 局部窗口使用 RoPE 原版旋转，保证短上下文精确复现基模型；(2) 远程窗口的缩放因子随当前序列长度动态调整；(3) 使用 inclusion-exclusion 注意力合并机制融合两个窗口的输出；(4) 在线 RoPE 校正旋转消除双焦点构造带来的偏差；(5) 融合为单一 CuTe 内核，在 H100 上高效实现。

Result: 在 Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B 上，128K 上下文的 RULER 指标分别超出最强基线 +4.79/+2.18/+2.03 pp；HELMET-RAG 上获得最佳整体精度（该基准被 HELMET 认定为下游长上下文性能最有效的预测指标）；PG-19 困惑度最低。推理开销方面：长上下文 prefill 在 H100 上达 FA2 吞吐量的 1.39×（接近仅 Hopper 架构的 FA4），单 batch 生成在各长度下额外开销 ≤4%。方法还泛化至 Jet-Nemotron 等混合注意力架构，且对超参数不敏感。

Conclusion: Jet-Long 作为一种无需微调的零样本上下文扩展方法，通过动态双焦点注意力设计同时解决了短上下文保真与长上下文外推的矛盾，推理开销极低，在多个基准上全面超越现有方法，并具备良好的架构泛化性和超参数鲁棒性，适合直接部署于开源模型的长上下文场景。

Abstract: Modern LLMs are increasingly deployed in long-context applications such as retrieval-augmented generation, repository-level coding, and agentic workflows whose accumulated reasoning and tool traces routinely push the input an order of magnitude past the pretraining window, making zero-shot context extension the dominant deployment path for open-weight checkpoints. Most existing zero-shot methods fix a single rescaling factor up front, so an aggressive factor sacrifices short-context fidelity while a conservative one breaks down at long contexts. We propose Jet-Long, a tuning-free zero-shot method that pairs a local RoPE-faithful window with a long-range window whose rescaling factor adapts dynamically to the current sequence length, recovering the base model exactly at short inputs while extrapolating cleanly at long ones. An inclusion-exclusion attention merge and an on-the-fly RoPE correction rotation make the bifocal construction essentially free at inference; fused into a single CuTe kernel, long-context prefill reaches up to $1.39\times$ FA2 throughput on H100 (approaching the Hopper-only FA4), and single-batch generation incurs $\le 4\%$ overhead at every length. On Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B up to 128K context, Jet-Long leads RULER by $+4.79$/$+2.18$/$+2.03$~pp over the strongest baseline at 1.7B/4B/8B, achieves the best overall accuracy on HELMET-RAG (a benchmark identified by HELMET as the most efficient predictor of downstream long-context performance) and attains the lowest PG-19 perplexity. Jet-Long also generalizes to hybrid attention architectures such as Jet-Nemotron for further long-context improvement without retraining, and remains hyperparameter-resilient for ease of deployment.

</details>


### [204] [LiST: Lipschitz Scaling Training for Robust and Calibrated Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07745)
*Arthur Chiron,Franck Mamalet,Thomas Massena,Thomas Deltort,Mathieu Serrurier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Lipschitz约束与温度缩放（Temperature Scaling）之间的理论联系，发现存在一个最优Lipschitz常数L*可同时实现开箱即用的校准。基于此提出LiST训练范式，通过迭代调整Lipschitz常数来构建完全校准的准确率-鲁棒性帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确率、鲁棒性和校准性对可靠神经网络至关重要，但通常被分别研究，三者兼顾仍是一个核心挑战。Lipschitz约束模型虽能保证鲁棒性，但其约束常数L的手动选择决定了准确率-鲁棒性的权衡，且其校准特性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出Lipschitz Scaling Training (LiST) 训练范式：首先从理论和实证上建立Lipschitz约束与温度缩放的关联，证明存在L*使网络开箱即校准；然后通过训练损失中的margin参数，迭代调整全局Lipschitz常数来达到该校准工作点，并可构建完全校准的帕累托前沿；收敛后，LiST支持将校准数据重新融入训练以提升样本效率。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100和Tiny-ImageNet上验证，LiST在对抗约束和无约束基线方法上展示了具有竞争力的准确率与鲁棒性，同时保持开箱即校准。

Conclusion: 校准性可作为选择准确率-鲁棒性帕累托前沿上工作点的原则性准则。LiST成功实现了准确率、鲁棒性和校准性三者的统一优化，为可靠神经网络的训练提供了新的范式。

Abstract: While accuracy, robustness, and calibration are all essential for reliable neural networks, they are often studied separately; developing models that satisfy all three simultaneously remains a central challenge. Lipschitz-constrained models guarantee robustness by design, yet the manual selection of the Lipschitz constraint L governs the resulting accuracy-robustness trade-off, and their calibration properties remain largely underexplored. In this work, we highlight a theoretical and empirical link between the enforced Lipschitz constraint and Temperature Scaling, a state-of-the-art calibration method. Specifically, we find that for a given training scheme, there exists a non-trivial value L* that yields an out-of-the-box calibrated network, and that calibration acts as a principled criterion to select a well-defined operating point on the accuracy-robustness Pareto front. Leveraging these insights, we introduce Lipschitz Scaling Training (LiST), a novel training paradigm that iteratively adjusts the global Lipschitz constant to reach this operating point. Through a margin parameter in the training loss, LiST further enables the construction of a fully calibrated Pareto front, allowing users to navigate the accuracy-robustness trade-off while remaining calibrated throughout. At convergence, LiST also enables the reintegration of calibration data into training, improving sample efficiency without sacrificing calibration. We validate LiST on CIFAR-10/100 and Tiny-ImageNet, demonstrating competitive accuracy and robustness against constrained and unconstrained baselines, while remaining calibrated out of the box. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [205] [Architecture Generalization with MetaNCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07743)
*Meet Barot,Daniel Berenberg,Sina Khajehabdollahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaNCA框架，通过局部规则网络以神经细胞自动机方式自组织生成人工神经网络权重，无需反向传播即可为多样架构（MLP、CNN、ResNet）生成参数，规模达200万参数，并展现出对未见架构的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受生物神经元通过局部突触交互实现高效学习和终身适应的启发，作者旨在利用神经细胞自动机（NCA）的局部交互与自适应特性，探索仅通过局部更新规则来生成人工神经网络权重的可能性，从而摆脱对全局反向传播的依赖。

Method: 提出Meta Neural Cellular Automata (MetaNCA) 框架：一个可学习的规则网络在计算图上通过局部交互迭代更新任务网络的权重。核心创新是Weight Transformer架构，利用线性注意力机制聚合来自相邻权重和隐藏状态的信号。元训练完成后，规则网络可在无反向传播的情况下，为不同架构生成权重。

Result: MetaNCA成功为MNIST和CIFAR-100上的前馈MLP、CNN和ResNet生成权重，规模扩展至200万参数。实验表明，MetaNCA能够泛化到元训练阶段未见过的网络架构，且训练阶段的架构多样性能够增强这种泛化能力。

Conclusion: MetaNCA证明了通过局部规则自组织生成神经网络权重是可行的，为摆脱反向传播的权重生成范式提供了新途径，其架构泛化能力显示出该方法的潜力。

Abstract: Self-organization is an emergent property of life, driven by the collective behavior of individual components acting on local information. Biological neurons, through local interactions transmitted through synapses, are able to learn efficiently and can adapt their connections over an organism's lifespan. Motivated by these desirable properties of adaptability and local interaction, neural cellular automata (NCA) models have been successful at learning morphogenesis solely through local update rules, demonstrating stability over many updates and robustness to perturbations. In this work, we introduce Meta Neural Cellular Automata (MetaNCA), a framework that learns local rules which self-organize the weights of artificial neural networks. A learned rule network iteratively updates the weights of a task network using only local interactions on the computation graph. We propose a novel Weight Transformer architecture for the local rule network, which uses linear attention to aggregate signals from neighboring weights and hidden states. Once trained, the rule network generates task networks of diverse architectures without backpropagation. We show that MetaNCA generates weights for feedforward MLPs, CNNs, and ResNets on MNIST and CIFAR-100, scaling to networks of 2 million parameters. We further show that MetaNCA generalizes to architectures not seen during meta-training, and that architectural diversity in the training phase strengthens this generalization.

</details>


### [206] [A law of robustness for two-layer neural networks with arbitrary weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07778)
*Yitzchak Shmalo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Bubeck等人猜想：对于一般数据，拟合n个噪声标签的两层神经网络，其Lipschitz常数至少为√(n/m)量级。本文对所有连续分段线性激活函数（特别是ReLU）证明了该猜想（仅差一对数因子），关键创新在于用函数空间覆盖替代了参数空间覆盖，并建立了一个刚性引理来控制典型折点的系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bubeck, Li和Nagaraj提出了一个关于两层神经网络Lipschitz常数的下界猜想，但该猜想在无界权重情形下未被证明。Bubeck和Sellke证明了参数化类在多项式参数界下的普适版本，但该有界性假设在三层网络中本质上是必要的。两层无界权重情形需要全新的论证方法。

Method: 1. 用函数空间覆盖替代参数空间覆盖（后者在无界权重下不可行）；2. 建立刚性引理：在B₂球面和S^{d-1}(d≥3)上，每个典型折点的系数由实现函数的Lipschitz常数控制，因为不同超平面上的折点在一般点处无法相互抵消。d=2时刚性失效，给出了一个O(1) Lipschitz常数的显式两层ReLU插值构造。

Result: 对于从S^{d-1}(d≥3)或N(0,I_d/d)均匀抽取的数据、[-1,1]内噪声水平σ²>0的标签，以及任意宽度m的两层网络（含任意实权重、偏置和仿射跳跃连接），若拟合误差低于噪声地板ε，则以高概率有：Lip(f) ≥ c·ε·√(n/(m̄·log(C·m̄·n·d/ε)))，其中m̄=(K-1)m+1。实际折点计数版本：对任何具有k(f)≤n个不同折点超平面的两层分段线性函数，用k(f)+1替换m̄，与冗余隐藏单元数量无关。

Conclusion: 该工作完成了两层无界权重情形下Lipschitz下界猜想的证明（仅差一对数因子），揭示了两层与三层网络的本质差异，并发现d=2是一个特殊的退化情形。

Abstract: Bubeck, Li and Nagaraj conjectured that, for generic data, any two-layer neural network with $m$ neurons that fits $n$ noisy labels must have Lipschitz constant at least of order $\sqrt{n/m}$, with no restriction on the size of the weights. Bubeck and Sellke proved a universal version of this law for Lipschitz-parameterized classes, but under a polynomial bound on the parameters; at depth three that boundedness hypothesis is genuinely necessary. The two-layer unbounded-weight case requires a different argument. We prove the conjectured law, up to one logarithmic factor, for every continuous piecewise-linear activation, in particular for ReLU networks. For data drawn uniformly from $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$, $d\ge3$, or from $N(0,I_d/d)$, labels in $[-1,1]$ with noise level $σ^2>0$, and any width-$m$ two-layer network with arbitrary real weights, biases and affine skip connection, fitting the data $\varepsilon$ below the noise floor forces $\mathrm{Lip}(f)\ge c\,\varepsilon\sqrt{n/(\bar m\log(C\bar m nd/\varepsilon))}$, $\bar m=(K-1)m+1$, with high probability. A realized-kink-count version holds on the same event: every realized two-layer piecewise-linear function with $k(f)\le n$ distinct kink hyperplanes obeys the bound with $\bar m$ replaced by $k(f)+1$, irrespective of how many redundant hidden units parameterize it. The proof replaces parameter-space covering, impossible for unbounded weights, by a function-space covering. The central deterministic ingredient is a rigidity lemma: on $B_2$, and on $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$ for $d\ge3$, the coefficient of each canonical kink is controlled by the Lipschitz constant of the realized function, because kinks on distinct hyperplanes cannot cancel at generic points. Rigidity genuinely fails at $d=2$, and an explicit two-layer ReLU interpolant with $O(1)$ Lipschitz constant at width $2n$ matches the law at the overparameterized endpoint.

</details>


### [207] [Selective Left-Shift: Turning Test-Time Compute and Difficulty-based Curation into Training Data for Low-Resource Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07748)
*Didula Samaraweera,Anjana Supun,Srinath Perera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对低资源编程语言（LRPLs）代码生成中“数据稀缺、推理成本高、强化学习效果差”的三难困境，提出三阶段流水线：离线合成验证训练数据 → SFT注入语法先验 → 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR），在Julia和Ballerina上显著提升小模型代码生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在Python等主流语言上代码生成表现优异，但在Julia等低资源编程语言上性能骤降。改进小语言模型面临三难困境：SFT受数据稀缺限制、推理时扩展成本过高、从头强化学习收益几乎为零。需要一种能同时解决这三个问题的方法。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线：(1) 将推理时计算“左移”到离线数据合成引擎，利用编译器反馈和测试用例迭代生成验证过的训练样本；(2) 在合成数据上对SLM进行监督微调（SFT），嵌入强语法先验；(3) 基于语言无关的输入/输出测试进行可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR），SFT先验约束探索空间、避免语法错误。以Qwen3-8B为基座模型进行实验。

Result: 在Julia上，MultiPL-E Pass@1提升高达+7.6点，Agnostics LiveCodeBench提升+14.2点，且仅使用SOTA方法1/3的数据和1/6的成本。在几乎没有预训练表示的Ballerina语言上，MultiPL-E Pass@1达到49.7%。消融实验证实SFT阶段和执行基础的奖励两者缺一不可。

Conclusion: 通过将语法获取与算法推理解耦的三阶段流水线，有效解决了低资源编程语言上小模型代码生成的三难困境，不仅在Julia上以更低成本超越SOTA，还展现出对几乎无预训练表示语言的强泛化能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models achieve strong code generation for high resource languages like Python and Java but suffer sharp performance drops on Low-Resource Programming Languages~(LRPLs) such as Julia. Improving Small Language Models~(SLMs) for these languages faces a trilemma: Supervised Fine-Tuning~(SFT) is bottlenecked by data scarcity, inference-time scaling is too expensive for deployment, and Reinforcement Learning from scratch yields near zero advantages. We propose a three-phase pipeline that resolves this trilemma by decoupling syntax acquisition from algorithmic reasoning. First, we \emph{left-shift} inference-time compute to an offline data synthesis engine that uses iterative compiler and test feedback to generate verified training examples. Second, we fine-tune an SLM on this synthetic, verified data to embed strong syntactic priors. Third, we apply Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Reward~(RLVR) grounded by language-agnostic Input/Output tests, where the SFT prior constrains exploration away from syntax errors. Applied to Qwen3-8B, our pipeline improves pass@1 by up to +7.6 points on MultiPL-E and +14.2 points on the Agnostics LiveCodeBench for Julia compared to SOTA results. Furthermore, the pipeline only used $\frac{1}{3}$ data and $\frac{1}{6}$ cost over the previous state-of-the-art. We further demonstrate that the pipeline generalizes to Ballerina achieving 49.7\% MultiPL-E Pass@1, a language with near-zero pretraining representation. Ablations confirm that both the SFT phase and execution-grounded rewards are necessary for stable training.

</details>


### [208] [Reinforcing the Generation Order of Multimodal Masked Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08056)
*Yidong Ouyang,Zhe Wang,Sourav Bhabesh,Dmitriy Bespalov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散语言模型（DLM）中的自适应生成顺序优化问题，提出一种基于GRPO训练的 learnable control module 来学习最优生成顺序，在文本到图像生成和多模态理解任务上分别取得4.08%和4.85%的相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究表明自适应token生成顺序能显著提升数学推理和代码合成等结构化语言生成任务的性能。但本文发现，对于文本到图像生成和多模态理解这类非结构化任务，仅靠模型logits不足以确定最优生成顺序，因此需要引入新的学习机制来优化生成顺序。

Method: 引入一个可学习的控制模块（learnable control module），使用Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）进行训练，来自动学习确定扩散语言模型中的最优token生成顺序。

Result: 在文本到图像对齐基准GenEval上取得4.08%的相对改进；在多模态理解基准VLMEvalKit上取得4.85%的相对改进。该方法增强了模型捕捉生成图像中细粒度空间关系的能力，并提升了多模态推理与理解性能。

Conclusion: 通过学习生成顺序控制模块，可以显著提升扩散语言模型在文本到图像对齐和多模态理解两大任务上的表现，证实了自适应生成顺序优化在非结构化多模态任务中的广泛有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have recently achieved substantial progress in natural language generation tasks. Recent research demonstrates that adaptive token generation ordering can significantly improve performance in mathematical reasoning and code synthesis applications. In this work, we investigate the optimization of generation order for both text-to-image synthesis and multimodal understanding.
